    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""

    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    33→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    35→
    36→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    37→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    38→
    39→    # Synthesis phase guardrail
    40→    from desloppify.engine.plan import synthesis_phase_banner
    41→    _banner = synthesis_phase_banner(plan)
    42→    if _banner:
    43→        print(colorize(f"  {_banner}", "yellow"))
    44→        print()
    45→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    46→    if total_skipped:
    47→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    48→    else:
    49→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    50→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    51→    if clusters:
    52→        manual = {n: c for n, c in clusters.items() if not c.get("auto")}
    53→        auto = {n: c for n, c in clusters.items() if c.get("auto")}
    54→        if manual:
    55→            for name, cluster in manual.items():
    56→                desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    57→                member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    58→                marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    59→                desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    60→                print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    61→        if auto:
    62→            auto_items = sum(len(c.get("finding_ids", [])) for c in auto.values())
    63→            focused_auto = f" (focused: {active})" if active and active in auto else ""
    64→            print(f"    ({len(auto)} auto-clusters, {auto_items} findings{focused_auto})")
    65→    if described or noted:
    66→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    67→    if active:
    68→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    69→    if superseded:
    70→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    71→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
    72→    if epics:
    73→        print(f"  Epics:            {len(epics)}")
    74→        for name, epic in epics.items():
    75→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
    76→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
    77→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
    78→            fcount = len(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
    79→            safe_tag = " [agent-safe]" if epic.get("agent_safe") else ""
    80→            print(f"    {name} [{direction}] ({fcount} findings, {status}){safe_tag}")
    81→            if thesis:
    82→                print(f"      {thesis}")
    83→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    84→    if meta and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
    85→        print(f"  Strategy:         {meta['strategy_summary']}")
    86→        print(f"  Last synthesis:   v{meta.get('version', 0)} ({meta.get('trigger', 'unknown')} @ {meta.get('last_run', 'never')})")
    87→

    89→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    90→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    91→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    92→
    93→    plan = load_plan()
    94→    reset_plan(plan)
    95→    save_plan(plan)

    99→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   100→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   101→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   102→
   103→    if plan_action is None:
   104→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   105→        return
   106→
   107→    if plan_action == "show":
   108→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   109→        return
   110→
   111→    if plan_action == "queue":
   112→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
   113→
   114→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   115→        return
   116→
   117→    if plan_action == "reset":
   118→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   119→        return
   120→
   121→    if plan_action == "move":
   122→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
   123→
   124→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   125→        return
   126→
   127→    if plan_action in (
   128→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   129→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   130→    ):
   131→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
   132→            cmd_plan_describe,
   133→            cmd_plan_done,
   134→            cmd_plan_focus,
   135→            cmd_plan_note,
   136→            cmd_plan_reopen,
   137→            cmd_plan_skip,
   138→            cmd_plan_unskip,
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        dispatch = {
   142→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   143→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   144→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   145→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   146→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   147→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   148→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   149→        }
   150→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   151→        return
   152→
   153→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   154→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   155→
   156→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   157→        return
   158→
   159→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   160→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   161→
   162→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   163→        return
   164→
   165→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   166→
   167→
   168→# Backwards-compatible alias
   169→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   170→
   171→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]

    20→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize

    27→
    28→def _scorecard_subjective(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    dim_scores: dict,
    31→) -> list[dict]:
    32→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    33→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    37→    state: dict,
    38→    dim_scores: dict,
    39→    *,
    40→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    41→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    42→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    43→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    44→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    45→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    46→            continue
    47→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    48→        if strict_val < threshold:
    49→            low.append(
    50→                (
    51→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    52→                    strict_val,
    53→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    54→                )
    55→            )
    56→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    57→    return low
    58→
    59→
    60→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    61→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    62→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    63→    state = runtime.state
    64→    config = runtime.config
    65→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    66→        return
    67→
    68→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    69→    if stale_warning:
    70→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    71→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    72→    if skill_warning:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    74→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    75→    if config_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→
    78→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    82→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    83→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    84→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    85→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    86→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    87→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    88→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    89→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    90→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    91→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    92→
    93→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    94→
    95→    # Load the living plan
    96→    plan = load_plan()
    97→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    98→    if (
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    ):
   103→        plan_data = plan
   104→
   105→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   106→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   107→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   108→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   109→        if active_cluster:
   110→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   111→
   112→    queue = build_plan_queue(
   113→        state,
   114→        tier=tier,
   115→        count=count,
   116→        scope=scope,
   117→        status=status,
   118→        target_strict=target_strict,
   119→        no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   120→        explain=explain,
   121→        plan=plan_data,
   122→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   123→        cluster=effective_cluster,
   124→    )
   125→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   126→
   127→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   128→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   129→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   130→        state,
   131→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   132→    )
   133→
   134→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   135→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   136→    )
   137→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   138→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   139→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   140→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   141→        state,
   142→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   143→    )
   144→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   145→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   146→    ]
   147→    write_query(payload)
   148→
   149→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   150→    if output_file:
   151→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   152→            output_file,
   153→            payload,
   154→            len(items),
   155→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   156→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   157→        ):
   158→            return
   159→        raise SystemExit(1)
   160→
   161→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   162→        return
   163→
   164→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   165→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   166→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   167→        return
   168→
   169→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   170→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   171→        state.get("findings", {}),
   172→        state.get("scan_path"),
   173→    )
   174→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   175→    try:
   176→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   177→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   178→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   179→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   180→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   181→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   182→    )
   183→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   184→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   185→        state,

1→"""Centralized score update display for all state-changing commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→def print_score_update(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    13→    *,
    14→    config: dict | None = None,
    15→    label: str = "Scores",
    16→) -> None:
    17→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    18→
    19→    Args:
    20→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    21→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    22→        config: Project config dict (loaded from disk if not provided).
    23→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    24→    """
    25→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    26→    if (
    27→        new.overall is None
    28→        or new.objective is None
    29→        or new.strict is None
    30→        or new.verified is None
    31→    ):
    32→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    33→        return
    34→
    35→    overall_s, overall_c = format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    36→    objective_s, objective_c = format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    37→    strict_s, strict_c = format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    38→    verified_s, verified_c = format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
    39→
    40→    print(
    41→        f"\n  {label}: "
    42→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
    43→        + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}", objective_c)
    44→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
    45→        + colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}", verified_c)
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    # Always show strict target + next-command nudge
    49→    if config is None:
    50→        from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
    51→
    52→        config = config_mod.load_config()
    53→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    54→
    55→    target = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    56→    print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target)
    57→
    58→
    59→def print_strict_target_nudge(
    60→    strict: float, target: float, *, show_next: bool = True,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Print a one-liner with strict→target and optional next-command nudge."""
    63→    gap = round(target - strict, 1)
    64→    if gap > 0:
    65→        suffix = " — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement" if show_next else ""
    66→        print(colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} (target: {target:.1f}){suffix}", "dim"))
    67→    else:
    68→        print(colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} — target {target:.1f} reached!", "green"))
    69→
    70→# Backward compatibility for older import paths.
    71→_print_strict_target_nudge = print_strict_target_nudge
    72→
    73→__all__ = ["print_score_update", "print_strict_target_nudge"]
    74→

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    build_synthesis_item,
    13→    scope_matches,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    16→    build_finding_items,
    17→    choose_fallback_tier,
    18→    group_queue_items,
    19→    item_explain,
    20→    item_sort_key,
    21→    tier_counts,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    27→class QueueBuildOptions:
    28→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    29→
    30→    tier: int | None = None
    31→    count: int | None = 1
    32→    scan_path: str | None = None
    33→    scope: str | None = None
    34→    status: str = "open"
    35→    include_subjective: bool = True
    36→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    37→    chronic: bool = False
    38→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    39→    explain: bool = False
    40→    plan: dict | None = None
    41→    include_skipped: bool = False
    42→    cluster: str | None = None
    43→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    44→
    45→
    46→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    47→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    48→
    49→    items: list[dict]
    50→    total: int
    51→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    52→    requested_tier: int | None
    53→    selected_tier: int | None
    54→    fallback_reason: str | None
    55→    available_tiers: list[int]
    56→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    57→
    58→
    59→def _apply_plan_order(
    60→    items: list[dict],
    61→    plan: dict,
    62→    *,
    63→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    64→    cluster: str | None = None,
    65→    state: dict | None = None,
    66→) -> list[dict]:
    67→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    68→
    69→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    70→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    71→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    72→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    73→    """
    74→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    75→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    76→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    77→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    78→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    79→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    80→
    81→    # Build lookup
    82→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    85→
    86→    # Build synthetic items for skipped entries missing from the work queue
    87→    if include_skipped and state:
    88→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    89→        for fid in skipped_ids:
    90→            if fid not in by_id and fid in findings:
    91→                f = findings[fid]
    92→                by_id[fid] = {
    93→                    "id": fid,
    94→                    "kind": "finding",
    95→                    "tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    96→                    "effective_tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    97→                    "confidence": f.get("confidence", "medium"),
    98→                    "detector": f.get("detector", ""),
    99→                    "file": f.get("file", ""),
   100→                    "summary": f.get("summary", ""),
   101→                    "status": f.get("status", "open"),
   102→                }
   103→
   104→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   105→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   106→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   107→        if override.get("description"):
   108→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   109→        if override.get("note"):
   110→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   111→        if override.get("cluster"):
   112→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   113→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   114→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   115→                "name": cluster_name,
   116→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   117→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   118→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   119→            }
   120→
   121→    # Normalise queue_order so subjective:: items always precede
   122→    # synthesis::pending — reviews generate the findings synthesis
   123→    # organises, so they must run first regardless of insertion order.
   124→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
   125→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in queue_order:
   126→        subj = [fid for fid in queue_order if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)]
   127→        if subj:
   128→            # Partition: subjective items first, then synthesis, then the rest
   129→            rest = [
   130→                fid for fid in queue_order
   131→                if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID and not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   132→            ]
   133→            queue_order = subj + [SYNTHESIS_ID] + rest
   134→
   135→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   136→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   137→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   138→    for fid in queue_order:
   139→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   140→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   141→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   142→
   143→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   144→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   145→    for item in items:
   146→        item_id = item["id"]
   147→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   148→            continue
   149→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   150→            skipped_items.append(item)
   151→        else:
   152→            remaining.append(item)
   153→
   154→    # Assign queue positions
   155→    result = ordered + remaining
   156→    if include_skipped:
   157→        result = result + skipped_items
   158→
   159→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   160→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   161→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   162→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   163→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   164→            if skip_entry:
   165→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   166→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   167→                if skip_reason:
   168→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   169→
   170→    # Filter to cluster if requested — but never filter out synthesis or
   171→    # subjective items; they are queue-level blockers that outrank cluster membership.
   172→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   173→    if effective_cluster:
   174→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   175→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   176→        result = [
   177→            item for item in result
   178→            if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids
   179→            or item.get("kind") in ("synthesis_needed", "subjective_dimension")
   180→        ]
   181→
   182→    return result
   183→
   184→
   185→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   186→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   187→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
   188→    if kind in ("synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   189→        return tier == 1  # Always T1
   190→    if kind == "cluster":
   191→        return any(
   192→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   193→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   194→        )
   195→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   196→
   197→
   198→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   199→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   200→    try:
   201→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   202→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   203→        if meta:
   204→            return meta.action_type
   205→    except ImportError:
   206→        pass
   207→    return "manual_fix"
   208→
   209→
   210→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   211→
   212→
   213→def _build_epic_items(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   214→    """Build synthetic queue items for each pending/in_progress synthesis-cluster."""
   215→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import synthesis_clusters
   216→
   217→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   218→    if not epics:
   219→        return []
   220→
   221→    total_epics = len(epics)
   222→    items: list[dict] = []
   223→    for epic_name, epic in sorted(
   224→        epics.items(),
   225→        key=lambda kv: kv[1].get("dependency_order", 999),
   226→    ):
   227→        status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   228→        if status == "completed":
   229→            continue
   230→        finding_ids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   231→        if not finding_ids:
   232→            continue
   233→
   234→        items.append({
   235→            "id": epic_name,
   236→            "kind": "epic",
   237→            "tier": 1,
   238→            "effective_tier": 1,
   239→            "confidence": "high",
   240→            "detector": "epic",
   241→            "file": ".",
   242→            "summary": f"[{epic.get('direction', 'simplify')}] {epic.get('thesis', '')}",
   243→            "status": "open",
   244→            "primary_command": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   247→                "direction": epic.get("direction", ""),
   248→                "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   249→                "epic_status": status,
   250→                "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   251→                "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   252→                "total_epics": total_epics,
   253→                "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   254→                "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   255→            },
   256→        })
   257→    return items
   258→
   259→
   260→def _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   261→    *,
   262→    all_items: list[dict],
   263→    plan: dict,
   264→    state: StateModel,
   265→    scope: str | None,
   266→) -> None:
   267→    """Inject synthesis::pending and epic items into the queue."""
   268→    existing_ids = {item.get("id") for item in all_items}
   269→
   270→    synthesis_item = build_synthesis_item(plan, state)
   271→    if synthesis_item and synthesis_item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   272→        if scope_matches(synthesis_item, scope):
   273→            all_items.append(synthesis_item)
   274→            existing_ids.add(synthesis_item["id"])
   275→
   276→    epic_items = _build_epic_items(plan, state)
   277→    for item in epic_items:
   278→        if item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   279→            if scope_matches(item, scope):
   280→                all_items.append(item)
   281→                existing_ids.add(item["id"])
   282→
   283→
   284→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   285→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   286→
   287→    Synthesis-clusters (those with a thesis) are represented by epic
   288→    items, so their member findings are excluded from cluster collapsing.
   289→    """
   290→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   291→
   292→    if not clusters:
   293→        return items
   294→
   295→    # Synthesis-clusters have their own epic queue items, so collect their
   296→    # finding IDs to exclude from regular cluster collapsing.
   297→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   298→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   299→        if cluster.get("thesis") and cluster.get("status") != "completed":
   300→            epic_finding_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   301→
   302→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   303→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   304→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   305→        if not cluster.get("auto") or cluster.get("thesis"):
   306→            continue
   307→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   308→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids:
   309→                fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   310→
   311→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   312→        return items
   313→
   314→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   315→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   316→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   317→
   318→    for item in items:
   319→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   320→        if cname:
   321→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   322→        else:
   323→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   324→
   325→    # Build cluster meta-items
   326→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   327→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   328→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   329→        if len(members) < 2:
   330→            result.extend(members)
   331→            continue
   332→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   333→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   334→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   335→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   336→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   337→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   338→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   339→            action_type = "reorganize"
   340→        else:
   341→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   342→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   343→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   344→                action_type = "refactor"
   345→
   346→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   347→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   348→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   349→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   350→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   351→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   352→        else:
   353→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   354→
   355→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   356→        if not primary_command:
   357→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   358→
   359→        meta_item: dict = {
   360→            "id": cname,
   361→            "kind": "cluster",
   362→            "action_type": action_type,
   363→            "summary": summary,
   364→            "members": members,
   365→            "member_count": len(members),
   366→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   367→            "cluster_name": cname,
   368→            "cluster_auto": True,
   369→            "confidence": "high",
   370→            "detector": detector,
   371→            "file": "",
   372→        }
   373→        result.append(meta_item)
   374→
   375→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   376→    return result
   377→
   378→
   379→def build_work_queue(
   380→    state: StateModel,
   381→    *,
   382→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   383→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   384→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   385→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   386→
   387→    status = resolved_options.status
   388→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   389→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   390→    try:
   391→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   392→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   393→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   394→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   395→
   396→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   397→        state,
   398→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   399→        status_filter=status,
   400→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   401→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   402→    )
   403→
   404→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   405→    if (
   406→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   407→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   408→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   409→    ):
   410→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   411→            state,
   412→            state.get("findings", {}),
   413→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   414→        )
   415→        for item in subjective_items:
   416→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   417→                all_items.append(item)
   418→
   419→    # Inject synthesis::pending and epic items when plan has them
   420→    if resolved_options.plan:
   421→        _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   422→            all_items=all_items,
   423→            plan=resolved_options.plan,
   424→            state=state,
   425→            scope=resolved_options.scope,
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   429→
   430→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   431→    if resolved_options.plan:
   432→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   433→            all_items,
   434→            resolved_options.plan,
   435→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   436→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   437→            state=state if resolved_options.include_skipped else None,
   438→        )
   439→
   440→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   441→    should_collapse = (
   442→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   443→        and resolved_options.plan
   444→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   445→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   446→    )
   447→    if should_collapse:
   448→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   449→
   450→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   451→
   452→    requested_tier = (
   453→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   454→    )
   455→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   456→    fallback_reason = None
   457→    filtered = all_items
   458→
   459→    if requested_tier is not None:
   460→        filtered = [
   461→            item
   462→            for item in all_items
   463→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   464→        ]
   465→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   466→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   467→            if chosen is not None:
   468→                selected_tier = chosen
   469→                filtered = [
   470→                    item
   471→                    for item in all_items
   472→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   473→                ]
   474→                fallback_reason = (
   475→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   476→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   477→                )
   478→        elif not filtered:
   479→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   480→
   481→    total = len(filtered)
   482→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   483→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   484→
   485→    if resolved_options.explain:
   486→        for item in filtered:
   487→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   488→
   489→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   490→    return {
   491→        "items": filtered,
   492→        "total": total,
   493→        "tier_counts": counts,
   494→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   495→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   496→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   497→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   498→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   499→    }
   500→
   501→
   502→__all__ = [
   503→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   504→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   505→    "WorkQueueResult",
   506→    "build_work_queue",
   507→    "group_queue_items",
   508→]
   509→

   124→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
   125→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   126→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   127→
   128→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
   129→    members = item.get("members", [])
   130→    if members:
   131→        # File distribution
   132→        from collections import Counter
   133→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   134→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   135→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   136→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   137→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   138→        else:
   139→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   140→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   141→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   142→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   143→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   144→
   145→        # Sample IDs
   146→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   147→        for m in members[:3]:
   148→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   149→        if len(members) > 3:
   150→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   151→
   152→    # Action
   153→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   154→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   155→    if primary_command:
   156→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   157→
   158→    # Resolution hints
   159→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" '
   160→                   f'--note "<what>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"', "dim"))
   161→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   162→                   "dim"))
   163→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   164→
   165→
   166→def _render_item(
   167→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   168→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   169→) -> None:
   170→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   171→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   172→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   176→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   177→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   178→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   179→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   180→
   181→    # Effort/type label
   182→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   183→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   184→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   185→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   186→
   187→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   188→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   189→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   190→        subjective_score = float(
   191→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   192→        )
   193→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   194→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}%")
   195→        print(
   196→            colorize(
   197→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   198→                "cyan",
   199→            )
   200→        )
   201→        print(colorize(
   202→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   203→            "dim",
   204→        ))
   205→        if explain:
   206→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   207→                "policy",
   208→                "subjective review tasks are prioritised above synthesis and mechanical items.",
   209→            )
   210→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   211→        return
   212→
   213→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   214→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   215→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   216→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   217→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   218→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   219→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   220→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   221→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   222→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   223→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   224→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   225→
   226→    file = item.get("file", "")
   227→    if file and file != ".":
   228→        print(f"  File: {file}")
   229→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   230→
   231→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   232→    if detail.get("lines"):
   233→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   234→    if detail.get("category"):
   235→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   236→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   237→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   238→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   239→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   240→
   241→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   242→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   243→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   244→        if snippet:
   245→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   246→            print(snippet)
   247→
   248→    if dim_scores:
   249→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   250→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   251→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   252→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   253→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   254→            print(
   255→                colorize(
   256→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   257→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   258→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   259→                    "dim",
   260→                )
   261→            )
   262→
   263→    # Score impact estimate
   264→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   265→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   266→        try:
   267→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_score_impact
   268→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   269→            if impact > 0:
   270→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   271→            else:
   272→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   273→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   274→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   275→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   276→                    if issues > 1:
   277→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   278→                        if bulk > 0:
   279→                            print(colorize(
   280→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   281→                                "cyan",
   282→                            ))
   283→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   284→            pass
   285→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   286→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   287→        try:
   288→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
   289→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   290→            if dim_key:
   291→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   292→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   293→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   294→                        continue
   295→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   296→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   297→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   298→                        if drag > 0.01:
   299→                            print(colorize(
   300→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   301→                                "cyan",
   302→                            ))
   303→                        break
   304→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   305→            pass
   306→
   307→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   308→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   309→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   310→        similar_count = sum(
   311→            1
   312→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   313→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   314→        )
   315→        if similar_count > 1:
   316→            print(
   317→                colorize(
   318→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   319→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   320→                    "cyan",
   321→                )
   322→            )
   323→    if explain:
   324→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   325→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   326→        base = (
   327→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   328→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   329→        )
   330→
   331→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   332→        if dim_scores and detector:
   333→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   334→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   335→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   336→                base += (
   337→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   338→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   339→                )
   340→
   341→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   342→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   343→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   344→            if dim_key:
   345→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   346→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   347→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   348→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   349→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   350→                        break
   351→
   352→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   353→        if policy:
   354→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   355→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   356→
   357→
   358→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   359→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   360→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   361→        return
   362→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   363→    if not explain:
   364→        return
   365→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   366→    if available:
   367→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   368→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   369→
   370→
   371→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   372→    if queue.get("items"):
   373→        return False
   374→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   375→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   376→    if tier is not None:
   377→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   378→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   379→        if available:
   380→            commands = " | ".join(
   381→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   382→            )
   383→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   384→    return True
   385→
   386→
   387→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   388→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   389→    if not plan:
   390→        return
   391→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   392→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   393→    if not summary:
   394→        return
   395→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   396→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   397→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   398→
   399→
   400→def _render_subjective_task(item: dict) -> None:
   401→    """Render a subjective dimension review as a proper task card."""
   402→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   403→    dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "unknown")
   404→    strict_score = float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
   405→    open_review = int(detail.get("open_review_findings", 0))
   406→    is_stale = "stale" in item.get("summary", "")
   407→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "desloppify review --prepare")
   408→
   409→    tag = " \u2014 stale, re-review" if is_stale else ""
   410→    print(colorize(f"  (Subjective review{tag})", "bold"))
   411→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   412→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   413→    print(f"  Dimension: {dim_name}")
   414→    print(f"  Score: {strict_score:.1f}%")
   415→    if open_review:
   416→        print(f"  Open review findings: {open_review}")
   417→    print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary}", "cyan"))
   418→    print(colorize(
   419→        "  Note: re-review scores what it finds \u2014 scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   420→        "dim",
   421→    ))
   422→
   423→
   424→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   425→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   426→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   427→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   428→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   429→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   430→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   431→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   432→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   433→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   434→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   435→    stages = detail.get("stage_progress", {})
   436→    enrichment_gaps = detail.get("enrichment_gaps", [])
   437→    enriched_count = detail.get("enriched_cluster_count", 0)
   438→
   439→    print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
   440→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   441→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   442→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
   443→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
   444→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   445→    print()
   446→    if new:
   447→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   448→    if resolved:
   449→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   450→    if epic_count:
   451→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   452→
   453→    # Stage progress
   454→    print()
   455→    print("  Stages:")
   456→    for stage_name, label in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS:
   457→        if stage_name in stages:
   458→            print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label}", "green"))
   459→        elif SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
   460→            print(colorize(f"    \u2192 {label} (current)", "yellow"))
   461→        else:
   462→            print(colorize(f"    \u25cb {label}", "dim"))
   463→
   464→    # Show enrichment status when in organize phase
   465→    if "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
   466→        if enrichment_gaps:
   467→            print(colorize(f"\n  {len(enrichment_gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   468→            for gap_name, gap_fields in enrichment_gaps:
   469→                print(colorize(f"    {gap_name}: missing {', '.join(gap_fields)}", "yellow"))
   470→        elif enriched_count > 0:
   471→            print(colorize(f"\n  All {enriched_count} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
   472→
   473→    # Next command based on current stage
   474→    print()
   475→    if "observe" not in stages:
   476→        print(f"  Next: {SYNTH_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   477→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   478→        print(f"  Next: {SYNTH_CMD_REFLECT}")
   479→        print(f"    (View completed work and recurring patterns, then record reflect stage)")
   480→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   481→        if enrichment_gaps:
   482→            print("  Enrich clusters before proceeding:")
   483→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH}")
   484→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}   (when all clusters are enriched)")
   485→        elif enriched_count == 0:
   486→            print("  Defer contradictions, then create and enrich clusters:")
   487→            print("    desloppify plan skip <hash>   (temporarily defer contradictory findings)")
   488→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE}")
   489→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD}")
   490→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS}")
   491→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   492→        else:
   493→            print(f"  All clusters enriched! Record organize:")
   494→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   495→        if detail.get("has_prior_synthesis"):
   496→            print(f"  Skip: {SYNTH_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING}")
   497→    else:
   498→        print(f"  Next: {SYNTH_CMD_COMPLETE}")
   499→
   500→    print()
   501→    print(colorize("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow"))
   502→    print()
   503→    print(colorize("  Forbidden during this phase:", "red"))
   504→    print("    - desloppify fix / resolve / editing code")
   505→    print(colorize("  Allowed: plan skip (to defer contradictory findings during organize)", "dim"))
   506→    print()
   507→    print(colorize("  View plan:", "dim"))
   508→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan synthesize  \u2014 findings and stage progress", "dim"))
   509→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster list \u2014 current clusters", "dim"))
   510→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan queue        \u2014 items in priority order", "dim"))
   511→
   512→
   513→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   514→    """Render an epic card."""
   515→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   516→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   517→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   518→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   519→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   520→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   521→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   522→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   523→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   524→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   525→
   526→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   527→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   528→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   529→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   530→    if root_cause:
   531→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   532→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   533→    if total_epics:
   534→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   535→    print()
   536→    if action_steps:
   537→        print("  Steps:")
   538→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   539→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   540→        print()
   541→    if finding_ids:
   542→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   543→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   544→        for fid in shown:
   545→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   546→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   547→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   548→        print()
   549→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   550→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   551→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   552→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   553→
   554→
   555→def render_terminal_items(
   556→    items: list[dict],
   557→    dim_scores: dict,
   558→    findings_scoped: dict,
   559→    *,
   560→    group: str,
   561→    explain: bool,
   562→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   563→    plan: dict | None = None,
   564→) -> None:
   565→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   566→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   567→
   568→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   569→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   570→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   571→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   572→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   573→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   574→        visible = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed")
   575→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({visible} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   576→
   577→        # Exclude the synthesis item from the focus count — it's a queue-level
   578→        # blocker, not a cluster member, so don't count it in "N of M remaining".
   579→        focus_items = [it for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed"]
   580→        if len(focus_items) == 0:
   581→            print(colorize(
   582→                "  This cluster has no remaining items. "
   583→                "Run `desloppify plan focus --clear` to unfocus, "
   584→                "or `desloppify plan cluster show` to inspect.",
   585→                "yellow",
   586→            ))
   587→
   588→    # When synthesis is pending, show subjective review tasks (they must
   589→    # complete before synthesis) then the planning card — don't leak
   590→    # mechanical implementation tasks that would tempt agents to skip planning.
   591→    has_synthesis = any(it.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed" for it in items)
   592→    if has_synthesis:
   593→        # Collect the prefix: subjective items + synthesis card
   594→        prefix_items = []
   595→        for it in items:
   596→            if it.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   597→                prefix_items.append(it)
   598→                break
   599→            if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   600→                prefix_items.append(it)
   601→            else:
   602→                break  # non-subjective, non-synthesis — stop
   603→        for idx, it in enumerate(prefix_items):
   604→            if idx > 0:
   605→                print()
   606→            kind = it.get("kind", "")
   607→            queue_pos = it.get("queue_position")
   608→            if queue_pos and len(prefix_items) > 1:
   609→                label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   610→            else:
   611→                pos_str = f"  (#{queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   612→                label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   613→            print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   614→            if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   615→                _render_synthesis_item(it)
   616→            else:
   617→                _render_subjective_task(it)
   618→        return
   619→
   620→    if group != "item":
   621→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   622→        return
   623→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   624→        if idx > 0:
   625→            print()
   626→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   627→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   628→
   629→        # All item types get the same position label — no visual hierarchy
   630→        # that makes synthetic items look like metadata instead of tasks.
   631→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   632→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   633→        elif len(items) > 1:
   634→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   635→        else:
   636→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   637→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   638→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   639→
   640→        if kind == "epic":
   641→            _render_epic_item(item)
   642→            continue
   643→        if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   644→            _render_subjective_task(item)
   645→            continue
   646→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   647→
   648→
   649→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   650→    if len(items) != 1:
   651→        return
   652→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   653→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic", "subjective_dimension"):
   654→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   655→    if kind != "finding":
   656→        return
   657→    item = items[0]
   658→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   659→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   660→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   661→        primary = item.get(
   662→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   663→        )
   664→        print(f"    {primary}")
   665→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   666→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   667→        return
   668→
   669→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   670→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   671→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   672→        print(f"    {primary}")
   673→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   674→    else:
   675→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   676→
   677→    print(
   678→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   679→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   680→    )
   681→    print(
   682→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   683→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   684→    )
   685→
   686→
   687→def render_followup_nudges(
   688→    state: dict,
   689→    dim_scores: dict,
   690→    findings_scoped: dict,
   691→    *,
   692→    strict_score: float | None,
   693→    target_strict_score: float,
   694→) -> None:
   695→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   696→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   697→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   698→        state,
   699→        subjective_entries,
   700→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   701→        max_quality_items=3,
   702→        max_integrity_items=5,
   703→    )
   704→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   705→    if strict_score is not None:
   706→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   707→        if gap > 0:
   708→            print(
   709→                colorize(
   710→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   711→                    "cyan",
   712→                )
   713→            )
   714→        else:
   715→            print(
   716→                colorize(
   717→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   718→                    "green",
   719→                )
   720→            )
   721→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   722→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   723→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   724→
   725→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   726→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   727→        findings_scoped
   728→    )
   729→    parts: list[str] = []
   730→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   731→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   732→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   733→    open_review = [
   734→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   735→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   736→    ]
   737→    if low_dims:
   738→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   739→    if stale_count:
   740→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   741→    if unassessed_count:
   742→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   743→    if len(open_review):
   744→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   745→    if coverage_open > 0:
   746→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   747→
   748→    if parts:
   749→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   750→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   751→
   752→
   753→__all__ = [

    36→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
    37→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
    38→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
    39→
    40→    concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
    41→    if not concerns:
    42→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
    43→        return
    44→
    45→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
    46→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
    47→
    48→    for i, c in enumerate(concerns, 1):
    49→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
    50→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
    51→        for ev in c.evidence:
    52→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))
    53→        print(colorize(f"     ? {c.question}", "yellow"))
    54→
    55→        # Check if previously dismissed (but resurface due to changed findings).
    56→        prev = dismissals.get(c.fingerprint)
    57→        if isinstance(prev, dict):
    58→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
    59→            if reasoning:
    60→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
    61→        print()

    64→def _print_dimension_score(dim_data: dict, display_name: str) -> None:
    65→    """Print the health/strict score line for a dimension if available."""

    67→    strict_val = (

    69→    )

    71→        print(
    72→            colorize(
    73→                f"  {display_name}: {score_val:.1f}% health (strict: {strict_val:.1f}%)",
    74→                "bold",

    76→        )

   100→        if f.get("detector") == "review"

   102→        and lowered

   119→def _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state: dict, entity) -> None:
   120→    """Show score + 'no open findings' for a mechanical dimension with zero findings."""
   121→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, entity.display_name)
   122→    _print_dimension_score(dim_data, display_name)
   123→    det_list = ", ".join(entity.detectors) if entity.detectors else "none"
   124→    print(

   127→            "green",

   132→def _load_dimension_findings(
   133→    state: dict,
   134→    entity,
   135→    status_filter: str,
   136→) -> list[dict]:
   137→    """Load findings for all detectors in a mechanical dimension."""
   138→    all_matches: list[dict] = []
   139→    for detector in entity.detectors:
   140→        matches = load_matches(
   141→            state, scope=detector, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=False
   142→        )
   143→        all_matches.extend(matches)
   144→    # Deduplicate by finding ID
   145→    seen: set[str] = set()
   146→    unique: list[dict] = []
   147→    for item in all_matches:
   148→        fid = item.get("id", "")
   149→        if fid not in seen:
   150→            seen.add(fid)
   151→            unique.append(item)
   152→    return unique

   167→            "narrative": narrative,
   168→        }
   169→    )
   170→    if entity.kind == "special_view":
   171→        show_subjective_followup(

   174→        )
   175→    else:

   186→def _render_subjective_views_guide(entity) -> None:
   187→    """Print 'Related views' hint after subjective/subjective_review output."""
   188→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() in (
   189→        "subjective",

   191→    ):
   192→        print(colorize("  Related views:", "dim"))
   193→        print(colorize("    `show review --status open`            Per-file design review findings", "dim"))
   194→        print(colorize("    `show subjective_review --status open`  Files needing re-review", "dim"))
   195→
   196→
   197→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   198→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   199→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   200→    state = runtime.state
   201→    config = runtime.config
   202→
   203→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   204→        return
   205→
   206→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
   207→    if stale_warning:
   208→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
   209→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   210→    if skill_warning:
   211→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   212→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   213→    if config_warning:
   214→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   215→
   216→    # Synthesis phase guardrail
   217→    print_synthesis_guardrail_banner()
   218→
   219→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
   220→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
   221→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   222→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   223→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   224→        return
   225→
   226→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   227→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   228→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   229→        state,
   230→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   231→    )
   232→
   233→    # --- Entity resolution: classify what the user asked for ---
   234→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   235→
   236→    # Dispatch: concerns special view
   237→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
   238→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    # Dispatch: subjective dimension
   242→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
   243→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
   244→        return
   245→
   246→    # Dispatch: mechanical dimension
   247→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
   248→        matches = _load_dimension_findings(state, entity, status_filter)
   249→        if not matches:
   250→            _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
   251→            return
   252→        pattern = entity.display_name
   253→    else:
   254→        # All other kinds: existing load_matches path
   255→        matches = load_matches(
   256→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
   257→        )
   258→
   259→    if not matches:
   260→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
   261→        return
   262→
   263→    (
   264→        surfaced_matches,
   265→        hidden_by_detector,
   266→        noise_budget,
   267→        global_noise_budget,
   268→        budget_warning,
   269→    ) = resolve_noise(
   270→        config,
   271→        matches,
   272→    )
   273→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   274→
   275→    payload = build_show_payload(
   276→        surfaced_matches,
   277→        pattern,
   278→        status_filter,
   279→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   280→            total_matches=len(matches),
   281→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   282→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   283→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   284→        ),
   285→    )
   286→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   287→
   288→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   289→    if output_file:
   290→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   291→            return
   292→        raise SystemExit(1)
   293→
   294→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   295→    render_findings(
   296→        surfaced_matches,
   297→        pattern=pattern,
   298→        status_filter=status_filter,
   299→        show_code=show_code,
   300→        top=top,
   301→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   302→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   303→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   304→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   305→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   306→    )
   307→    try:
   308→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan as _load_plan
   309→        _plan = _load_plan()
   310→        _plan_active = _plan if (_plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")) else None
   311→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   312→        print(
   313→            colorize(
   314→                "  Warning: plan context unavailable for show output "
   315→                f"({exc.__class__.__name__}: {exc}).",
   316→                "yellow",
   317→            )
   318→        )
   319→        _plan_active = None
   320→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=_plan_active)
   321→    show_subjective_followup(
   322→        state,
   323→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   324→    )
   325→
   326→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   327→
   328→
   329→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   330→
