1→"""Dimension projection policy for scorecard rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimension_policy import (
     6→    _DEFAULT_ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS,
     7→    _ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS_BY_LANG,
     8→    _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_BY_LANG,
     9→    _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_DEFAULT,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimension_policy import (
    12→    _SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS as SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS,
    13→)
    14→
    15→
    16→def resolve_scorecard_lang(state: dict) -> str | None:
    17→    """Best-effort current scan language key for scorecard display policy."""
    18→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    19→    if isinstance(history, list):
    20→        for entry in reversed(history):
    21→            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    22→                continue
    23→            lang = entry.get("lang")
    24→            if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    25→                return lang.strip().lower()
    26→
    27→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    28→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    29→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    30→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    31→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    32→
    33→    findings = state.get("findings")
    34→    if isinstance(findings, dict):
    35→        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    36→        for finding in findings.values():
    37→            if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    38→                continue
    39→            lang = finding.get("lang")
    40→            if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    41→                key = lang.strip().lower()
    42→                counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    43→        if counts:
    44→            return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→
    48→def is_unassessed_subjective_placeholder(data: dict) -> bool:
    49→    return (
    50→        "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
    51→        and data.get("score", 0) == 0
    52→        and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→def collapse_elegance_dimensions(
    57→    active_dims: list[tuple[str, dict]],
    58→    *,
    59→    lang_key: str | None,
    60→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
    61→    """Collapse High/Mid/Low elegance rows into one aggregate display row."""
    62→    component_names = set(
    63→        _ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS_BY_LANG.get(lang_key or "", _DEFAULT_ELEGANCE_COMPONENTS)
    64→    )
    65→    elegance_rows = [
    66→        (name, data) for name, data in active_dims if name in component_names
    67→    ]
    68→    if not elegance_rows:
    69→        return active_dims
    70→
    71→    remaining_rows = [
    72→        (name, data) for name, data in active_dims if name not in component_names
    73→    ]
    74→    count = len(elegance_rows)
    75→    score_avg = round(
    76→        sum(float(data.get("score", 100.0)) for _, data in elegance_rows) / count, 1
    77→    )
    78→    strict_avg = round(
    79→        sum(
    80→            float(data.get("strict", data.get("score", 100.0)))
    81→            for _, data in elegance_rows
    82→        )
    83→        / count,
    84→        1,
    85→    )
    86→    checks_total = sum(int(data.get("checks", 0)) for _, data in elegance_rows)
    87→    issues_total = sum(int(data.get("issues", 0)) for _, data in elegance_rows)
    88→    tier = max(int(data.get("tier", 4)) for _, data in elegance_rows)
    89→    placeholder_flags = [
    90→        bool(
    91→            data.get("detectors", {})
    92→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    93→            .get("placeholder")
    94→        )
    95→        for _, data in elegance_rows
    96→    ]
    97→
    98→    label = "Elegance"
    99→    if any(name.lower() == label.lower() for name, _ in remaining_rows):
   100→        label = "Elegance (combined)"
   101→
   102→    pass_rate = round(score_avg / 100.0, 4)
   103→    subjective_detector = {
   104→        "potential": checks_total,
   105→        "pass_rate": pass_rate,
   106→        "issues": issues_total,
   107→        "weighted_failures": round(checks_total * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   108→        "components": [name for name, _ in elegance_rows],
   109→    }
   110→    subjective_detector["placeholder"] = any(placeholder_flags)
   111→    return [
   112→        *remaining_rows,
   113→        (
   114→            label,
   115→            {
   116→                "score": score_avg,
   117→                "strict": strict_avg,
   118→                "checks": checks_total,
   119→                "issues": issues_total,
   120→                "tier": tier,
   121→                "detectors": {
   122→                    "subjective_assessment": subjective_detector
   123→                },
   124→            },
   125→        ),
   126→    ]
   127→
   128→
   129→def limit_scorecard_dimensions(
   130→    active_dims: list[tuple[str, dict]],
   131→    *,
   132→    lang_key: str | None,
   133→    max_rows: int = SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS,
   134→) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
   135→    """Limit scorecard rows with language-specific subjective priority."""
   136→    if len(active_dims) <= max_rows:
   137→        return active_dims
   138→
   139→    mechanical = [
   140→        (name, data)
   141→        for name, data in active_dims
   142→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   143→    ]
   144→    subjective = [
   145→        (name, data)
   146→        for name, data in active_dims
   147→        if "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   148→    ]
   149→    if len(mechanical) >= max_rows:
   150→        return mechanical[:max_rows]
   151→
   152→    budget = max_rows - len(mechanical)
   153→    preferred_order = _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_BY_LANG.get(
   154→        lang_key or "",
   155→        _SUBJECTIVE_SCORECARD_ORDER_DEFAULT,
   156→    )
   157→
   158→    remaining = {name: (name, data) for name, data in subjective}
   159→    selected: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   160→    for name in preferred_order:
   161→        row = remaining.pop(name, None)
   162→        if row is None:
   163→            continue
   164→        selected.append(row)
   165→        if len(selected) >= budget:
   166→            break
   167→
   168→    if len(selected) < budget and remaining:
   169→        extras = sorted(
   170→            remaining.values(),
   171→            key=lambda item: (
   172→                float(item[1].get("strict", item[1].get("score", 100.0))),
   173→                item[0],
   174→            ),
   175→        )
   176→        selected.extend(extras[: budget - len(selected)])
   177→
   178→    return [*mechanical, *selected]
   179→
   180→
   181→def prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state: dict) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
   182→    """Prepare scorecard rows (active, elegance-collapsed, capped).
   183→
   184→    Dimensions are derived dynamically from what the scoring engine produced
   185→    in ``dimension_scores`` — no per-language hard-coded dimension list.
   186→    Unassessed subjective placeholders (score 0, no issues) are excluded so
   187→    the scorecard only shows dimensions that have real data.
   188→    """
   189→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   190→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict):
   191→        return []
   192→
   193→    all_dims = [
   194→        (name, data) for name, data in dim_scores.items() if isinstance(data, dict)
   195→    ]
   196→
   197→    [REDACTED](state)
   198→    all_dims = collapse_elegance_dimensions(all_dims, lang_key=lang_key)
   199→
   200→    # Show dimensions with real data; skip unassessed subjective placeholders.
   201→    active_dims = [
   202→        (name, data)
   203→        for name, data in all_dims
   204→        if data.get("checks", 0) > 0 and not is_unassessed_subjective_placeholder(data)
   205→    ]
   206→    active_dims = limit_scorecard_dimensions(active_dims, lang_key=lang_key)
   207→    active_dims.sort(key=lambda x: (0 if x[0] == "File health" else 1, x[0]))
   208→    return active_dims
   209→
   210→
   211→__all__ = [
   212→    "SCORECARD_MAX_DIMENSIONS",
   213→    "collapse_elegance_dimensions",
   214→    "prepare_scorecard_dimensions",
   215→    "limit_scorecard_dimensions",
   216→    "resolve_scorecard_lang",
   217→]
   218→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,

    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    88→
    89→    # Subjective dimension items sort first — reviews must complete before
    90→    # synthesis can organise their findings.
    91→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    92→        return (
    93→            -3,
    94→            subjective_score_value(item),
    95→            item.get("id", ""),
    96→        )
    97→
    98→    # Synthesis and epic items sort before mechanical work (T0-equivalent)
    99→    # but after subjective review tasks.
   100→    if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   101→        return (-2, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   102→    if kind == "epic":
   103→        dep_order = 999
   104→        detail = item.get("detail")
   105→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   106→            dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   107→        return (-1, dep_order, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   108→
   109→    if kind == "cluster":
   110→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   111→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
   112→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
   113→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
   114→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   115→        )
   116→        return (
   117→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
   118→            action_pri,
   119→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   120→            item.get("id", ""),
   121→        )
   122→
   123→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   124→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   125→    return (
   126→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   127→        0,
   128→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   129→        -review_weight,
   130→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   131→        item.get("id", ""),
   132→    )
   133→
   134→
   135→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   136→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   137→
   138→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   139→        return {
   140→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   141→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   142→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   143→            "policy": (
   144→                "Subjective review tasks are prioritised above synthesis and "
   145→                "mechanical items because reviews generate the findings those depend on."
   146→            ),
   147→            "ranking_factors": ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   148→        }
   149→
   150→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   151→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   152→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   153→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   154→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   155→    if is_subjective:
   156→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   157→    elif is_review:
   158→        ranking_factors = [
   159→            "tier asc",
   160→            "confidence asc",
   161→            "review_weight desc",
   162→            "count desc",
   163→            "id asc",
   164→        ]
   165→    else:
   166→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   167→    explain = {
   168→        "kind": "finding",
   169→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   170→        "confidence": confidence,
   171→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   172→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   173→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   174→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   175→    }
   176→    if is_review:
   177→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   178→    if is_subjective:
   179→        explain["policy"] = (
   180→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   181→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   182→        )
   183→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   184→    return explain
   185→
   186→
   187→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   188→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   189→    for item in items:
   190→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   191→            # Count each member at its own tier
   192→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   193→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   194→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   195→            continue
   196→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   197→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   198→    return counts
   199→
   200→

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:

1→"""Rendering and output helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup

    16→from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    19→
    20→from .formatting import format_detail
    21→
    22→
    23→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    24→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    25→    try:
    26→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    27→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    28→    except OSError as exc:
    29→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    30→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    31→        return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    36→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    37→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    38→    for finding in matches:
    39→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    40→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    44→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    45→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    46→    status_icon = {
    47→        "open": "○",
    48→        "fixed": "✓",
    49→        "wontfix": "—",
    50→        "false_positive": "✗",
    51→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    52→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    53→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    54→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    60→    if detail_parts:
    61→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    62→    if show_code:
    63→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    64→        target_line = (
    65→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    66→        )
    67→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    68→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    69→            if snippet:
    70→                print(snippet)
    71→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    72→        print(
    73→            colorize(
    74→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    75→                "red",
    76→            )
    77→        )
    78→    if finding.get("note"):
    79→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))
    81→
    82→
    83→def render_findings(
    84→    matches: list[dict],
    85→    *,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    show_code: bool,
    89→    top: int,
    90→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    91→    hidden_total: int,
    92→    noise_budget: int,
    93→    global_noise_budget: int,
    94→    budget_warning: str | None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
    97→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   101→            "bold",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→    if budget_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   106→    if hidden_total:
   107→        global_label = (
   108→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   109→        )
   110→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   111→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   112→        )
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                (
   116→                    "  Noise budget: "
   117→                    f"{noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
   118→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   119→                ),
   120→                "dim",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   125→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   126→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   127→
   128→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   129→        findings.sort(
   130→            key=lambda finding: (
   131→                finding["tier"],
   132→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
   133→            )
   134→        )
   135→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   136→        print(
   137→            colorize(f"  {display_path}", "cyan")
   138→            + colorize(f"  ({len(findings)} findings)", "dim")
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        for finding in findings:
   142→            _print_single_finding(finding, show_code=show_code)
   143→        print()
   144→
   145→    if remaining_findings:
   146→        print(
   147→            colorize(
   148→                (
   149→                    f"  ... and {len(remaining_files)} more files "
   150→                    f"({remaining_findings} findings). "
```

    90→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: dict) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return a warning banner if synthesis::pending is blocking, else None."""
    92→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    93→        return None
    94→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    95→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    96→    progress = []
    97→    for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize"):
    98→        if s in stages:
    99→            progress.append(f"{s} \u2713")
   100→        else:
   101→            progress.append(f"{s} \u25cb")
   102→    arrow = " \u2192 "
   103→    stage_line = f"  Stages: {arrow.join(progress)} \u2192 commit"
   104→
   105→    # Show what to do next based on current stage
   106→    if "observe" not in stages:
   107→        next_hint = '  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see findings and begin.'
   108→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   109→        next_hint = (
   110→            '  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see completed work and recurring patterns.\n'
   111→            '  Then: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."`'
   112→        )
   113→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   114→        next_hint = (
   115→            "  Create clusters, enrich each with action steps, then record organize.\n"
   116→            '  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see progress.'
   117→        )
   118→    else:
   119→        next_hint = '  Complete with: `desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."`'
   120→
   121→    return (
   122→        "PLANNING PHASE \u2014 synthesis::pending is blocking the queue.\n"
   123→        "  Goal: resolve contradictions, then cluster by root cause with action steps.\n"
   124→        f"{stage_line}\n"
   125→        f"{next_hint}\n"
   126→        "  Do NOT fix code or resolve findings until planning is done.\n"
   127→        "  (Exception: use `plan skip` during organize to defer contradictory findings.)"
   128→    )
   129→
   130→

1→"""Target-score normalization helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     6→
     7→
     8→def coerce_target_score(value: object, *, fallback: float = 95.0) -> float:
     9→    """Normalize target score-like values to a safe [0, 100] float."""
    10→    if is_numeric(fallback):
    11→        fallback_value = float(fallback)
    12→    else:
    13→        fallback_value = 95.0
    14→
    15→    if is_numeric(value):
    16→        parsed = float(value)
    17→    elif isinstance(value, str):
    18→        text = value.strip()
    19→        if not text:
    20→            parsed = fallback_value
    21→        else:
    22→            try:
    23→                parsed = float(text)
    24→            except ValueError:
    25→                parsed = fallback_value
    26→    else:
    27→        parsed = fallback_value
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, parsed))
    29→
    30→
    31→def target_strict_score_from_config(
    32→    config: dict | None, *, fallback: float = 95.0
    33→) -> float:
    34→    """Read and normalize target strict score from config."""
    35→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    36→        raw = config.get("target_strict_score", fallback)
    37→    else:
    38→        raw = fallback
    39→    return coerce_target_score(raw, fallback=fallback)
    40→
    41→

1→"""Shared queue-construction policy for next/plan command surfaces."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.enums import Status, canonical_status_filter
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     9→
    10→
    11→def build_plan_queue(
    12→    state: dict[str, Any],
    13→    *,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int | None = None,
    16→    scope: str | None = None,
    17→    status: str = Status.OPEN.value,
    18→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
    19→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False,
    20→    explain: bool = False,
    21→    plan: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    22→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    23→    cluster: str | None = None,
    24→    collapse_clusters: bool = False,
    25→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    26→    """Build one queue with consistent policy defaults used by CLI surfaces."""
    27→    normalized_status = canonical_status_filter(status, default=Status.OPEN.value)
    28→    return build_work_queue(
    29→        state,
    30→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    31→            tier=tier,
    32→            count=count,
    33→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    34→            scope=scope,
    35→            status=normalized_status,
    36→            include_subjective=True,
    37→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
    38→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
    39→            explain=explain,
    40→            plan=plan,
    41→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    42→            cluster=cluster,
    43→            collapse_clusters=collapse_clusters,
    44→        ),
    45→    )
    46→
    47→
    48→__all__ = ["build_plan_queue"]
    49→

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
```

"""State schema/types, constants, and validation helpers."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, NotRequired, Required, TypedDict

from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status, finding_status_tokens
from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import ScanCoverageRecord

__all__ = [
    "ConcernDismissal",
    "FindingStatus",
    "Finding",
    "TierStats",
    "StateStats",
    "DimensionScore",
    "ScanHistoryEntry",
    "SubjectiveAssessment",
    "SubjectiveIntegrity",
    "StateModel",
    "ScanDiff",
    "STATE_DIR",
    "STATE_FILE",
    "CURRENT_VERSION",
    "utc_now",
    "empty_state",
    "ensure_state_defaults",
    "validate_state_invariants",
    "json_default",
    "get_overall_score",
    "get_objective_score",
    "get_strict_score",
    "get_verified_strict_score",
]

FindingStatus = Literal["open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive"]
_ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES: set[str] = {
    *finding_status_tokens(),
}


class Finding(TypedDict):
    """The central data structure: a normalized finding from any detector."""

    id: str
    detector: str
    file: str
    tier: int
    confidence: str
    summary: str
    detail: dict[str, Any]
    status: FindingStatus
    note: str | None
    first_seen: str
    last_seen: str
    resolved_at: str | None
    reopen_count: int
    suppressed: NotRequired[bool]
    suppressed_at: NotRequired[str | None]
    suppression_pattern: NotRequired[str | None]
    resolution_attestation: NotRequired[dict[str, str | bool | None]]
    lang: NotRequired[str]
    zone: NotRequired[str]


class TierStats(TypedDict, total=False):
    open: int
    fixed: int
    auto_resolved: int
    wontfix: int
    false_positive: int


class StateStats(TypedDict, total=False):
    total: int
    open: int
    fixed: int
    auto_resolved: int
    wontfix: int
    false_positive: int
    by_tier: dict[str, TierStats]


class DimensionScore(TypedDict, total=False):
    score: float
    strict: float
    checks: int
    issues: int
    tier: int
    detectors: dict[str, Any]


class ScanHistoryEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    timestamp: str
    lang: str | None
    strict_score: float | None
    verified_strict_score: float | None
    objective_score: float | None
    overall_score: float | None
    open: int
    diff_new: int
    diff_resolved: int
    ignored: int
    raw_findings: int
    suppressed_pct: float
    ignore_patterns: int
    subjective_integrity: dict[str, Any] | None
    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, float]] | None
    score_confidence: dict[str, Any] | None


class SubjectiveIntegrity(TypedDict, total=False):
    """Anti-gaming metadata for subjective assessment scores."""

    status: str  # "disabled" | "pass" | "warn" | "penalized"
    target_score: float | None
    matched_count: int
    matched_dimensions: list[str]
    reset_dimensions: list[str]


class SubjectiveAssessment(TypedDict, total=False):
    """A single subjective dimension assessment payload."""

    score: float
    integrity_penalty: str | None
    provisional_override: bool
    provisional_until_scan: int
    needs_review_refresh: bool
    refresh_reason: str | None
    stale_since: str | None


class ConcernDismissal(TypedDict, total=False):
    """Record of a dismissed concern from review output."""

    dismissed_at: str
    reason: str | None
    dimension: str


class StateModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    version: Required[int]
    created: Required[str]
    last_scan: Required[str | None]
    scan_count: Required[int]
    overall_score: Required[float]
    objective_score: Required[float]
    strict_score: Required[float]
    verified_strict_score: Required[float]
    stats: Required[StateStats]
    findings: Required[dict[str, Finding]]
    scan_coverage: dict[str, ScanCoverageRecord]
    score_confidence: dict[str, Any]
    scan_history: list[ScanHistoryEntry]
    subjective_integrity: Required[SubjectiveIntegrity]
    subjective_assessments: Required[dict[str, SubjectiveAssessment]]
    concern_dismissals: dict[str, ConcernDismissal]


class ScanDiff(TypedDict):
    new: int
    auto_resolved: int
    reopened: int
    total_current: int
    suspect_detectors: list[str]
    chronic_reopeners: list[dict]
    skipped_other_lang: int
    skipped_out_of_scope: int
    ignored: int
    ignore_patterns: int
    raw_findings: int
    suppressed_pct: float
    skipped: NotRequired[int]
    skipped_details: NotRequired[list[dict]]


STATE_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify"
STATE_FILE = STATE_DIR / "state.json"
CURRENT_VERSION = 1


def utc_now() -> str:
    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")


def empty_state() -> StateModel:
    """Return a new empty state payload."""
    return {
        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
        "created": utc_now(),
        "last_scan": None,
        "scan_count": 0,
        "overall_score": 0,
        "objective_score": 0,
        "strict_score": 0,
        "verified_strict_score": 0,
        "stats": {},
        "findings": {},
        "scan_coverage": {},
        "score_confidence": {},
        "subjective_integrity": {},
        "subjective_assessments": {},
    }


def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
    try:
        parsed = int(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return default
    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0


def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
    for key, value in empty_state().items():
        state.setdefault(key, value)

    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
        state["findings"] = {}
    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
        state["stats"] = {}
    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
        state["scan_history"] = []
    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
        state["score_confidence"] = {}
    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}

    findings = state["findings"]
    to_remove: list[str] = []
    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
            to_remove.append(finding_id)
            continue

        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
        finding.setdefault("file", "")
        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
        finding.setdefault("detail", {})

"created": utc_now(),
        "last_scan": None,
        "scan_count": 0,
        "overall_score": 0,
        "objective_score": 0,
        "strict_score": 0,
        "verified_strict_score": 0,
        "stats": {},
        "findings": {},
        "scan_coverage": {},
        "score_confidence": {},
        "subjective_integrity": {},
        "subjective_assessments": {},
    }


def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
    try:
        parsed = int(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return default
    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0


def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
    for key, value in empty_state().items():
        state.setdefault(key, value)

    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
        state["findings"] = {}
    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
        state["stats"] = {}
    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
        state["scan_history"] = []
    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
        state["score_confidence"] = {}
    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}

    findings = state["findings"]
    to_remove: list[str] = []
    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
            to_remove.append(finding_id)
            continue

        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
        finding.setdefault("file", "")
        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
        finding.setdefault("detail", {})
        finding.setdefault("status", "open")
        finding["status"] = canonical_finding_status(
            finding.get("status"),
            default="open",
        )
        finding.setdefault("note", None)
        finding.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
        finding.setdefault("last_seen", finding["first_seen"])
        finding.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
        finding["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
            finding.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
        )
        finding.setdefault("suppressed", False)
        finding.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
        finding.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)

    for finding_id in to_remove:
        findings.pop(finding_id, None)

    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            continue
        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None

    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
    return None


def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")

    findings = state["findings"]
    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
            raise ValueError(
                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
            )

        tier = finding.get("tier")
        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")

        reopen_count = finding.get("reopen_count")
        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
            )


def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
    """JSON serializer that handles known types and rejects unknowns."""
    if isinstance(obj, set):
        return sorted(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, Path):
        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
        return obj.isoformat()
    raise TypeError(
        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
    )


def get_overall_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
    """Canonical overall score (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)."""
    return state.get("overall_score")


def get_objective_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
    """Canonical objective score (lenient, mechanical dimensions only)."""
    return state.get("objective_score")


def get_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
    """Canonical strict score (strict, includes subjective dimensions)."""
    return state.get("strict_score")


def get_verified_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
    """Strict score that only credits scan-verified fixes.

    Returns None if no scan-verified score exists yet (fresh state or
    no scan has run). Does not fall back to the unverified strict_score.
    """
    return state.get("verified_strict_score")

     3→This module preserves the original import path. The full plan command

"""Planning public API with lazy imports to avoid import cycles."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER, TIER_LABELS

def get_next_items(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any):

    return _get_next_items(*args, **kwargs)

    "generate_plan_md",
    "get_next_item",

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the system. Let me create a detailed summary document:

## Comprehensive Workflow Analysis: Plan/Next/Score Systems in Desloppify

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here is the complete user journey and information architecture:

---

### **1. USER JOURNEY: scan → plan → next → fix → repeat**

#### **Phase 1: `desloppify scan` (Entry Point)**
The scan command populates the state (`.desloppify/state.json`) with:
- **Findings**: Problems discovered by language-specific detectors (tiered 1-4, with confidence levels)
- **Four canonical scores**: 
  - `overall_score` (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)
  - `objective_score` (mechanical dimensions only)
  - `strict_score` (strict, includes subjective dimensions)
  - `verified_strict_score` (only credits scan-verified fixes)
- **Dimension scores**: Health assessment per codebase dimension (e.g., "File Health", "Elegance", etc.)
- **Subjective assessments**: Scores from design reviews (integrity-protected against gaming)

State is persisted atomically with `.json.bak` backups. A `scan_history` list tracks score progression over time.

---

#### **Phase 2: `desloppify plan` (Generate & Manage Work Queue)**

**The "Living Plan"** (`.desloppify/plan.json`) is a persistent, user-editable strategy document. It contains:

1. **`queue_order`** (list of finding IDs): Manually-prioritized work sequence
2. **`clusters`** (dict): Grouped findings by root cause
   - **Auto-clusters**: Synthesized by the system (e.g., `auto/stale-review`, `auto/initial-review`)
   - **Manual clusters**: User-created via `plan cluster create`
   - Each cluster has: `finding_ids`, `description`, `action` (command to fix), optionally `thesis` (for epics)
3. **`skipped`** (dict): Items deferred temporarily/permanently with reason
   - Kinds: `"temporary"`, `"permanent"` (wontfix), `"false_positive"`
4. **`overrides`** (dict): Per-finding metadata
   - `description`: Custom explanation
   - `note`: User notes
   - `cluster`: Which cluster it belongs to
5. **`active_cluster`** (string): Focus mode — limit next queue to one cluster
6. **`epic_synthesis_meta`**: Meta-plan orchestration
   - `strategy_summary`: High-level direction
   - `synthesis_stages`: Track planning phases (observe → reflect → organize → complete)
   - List of epics with `thesis`, `root_cause`, `action_steps`, `finding_ids`

**`plan` Command Output:**
- **`plan`** (no subcommand): Generates markdown with tier-grouped findings, dimension health, and action recommendations
- **`plan show`**: Displays plan metadata summary (queue size, clusters, skipped counts, epics, focus status)
- **`plan queue`**: Shows prioritized items in order
- **`plan move/describe/note/focus/skip/cluster`**: Edit the living plan
- **`plan synthesize`**: Interactive planning workflow (observe findings, group by root cause, create action steps)

The plan is **plan-aware**: it reorders the queue so user-prioritized items appear first, and applies plan metadata (descriptions, cluster memberships) to every item.

---

#### **Phase 3: `desloppify next` (Show Next Highest-Priority Item)**

This is the **primary user-facing command**. It displays:

1. **Tier Navigator** (header)
   ```
   Open: T1:5 T2:12 T3:28 T4:3
   ```
   Shows how many items exist at each tier. User can switch: `desloppify next --tier 2`

2. **Fallback Reason** (if requested tier has 0 items)
   - E.g., "Requested T1 has 0 open → showing T2 (nearest non-empty)"

3. **Queue Items** (in priority order)
   - Built by `build_work_queue()` (ranked by: tier asc, confidence asc, count desc, id asc)
   - Applied plan order (user-prioritized items first)
   - Collapsed auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster with `--cluster`)
   - Includes synthesis::pending and epic items when plan has them

4. **Item Card Format** (for each of the `--count` items, default 1):
   ```
   [#1]
   (Tier 2, high confidence)
   ────────────────────
   Find orphaned imports in typescript files
   → [plan description if set]
   Cluster: ... (if member of cluster)
   Note: ... (if annotated)
   File: src/main.ts
   ID: ts::orphaned_imports::abc123def
   Lines: 45, 67, 89
   Dimension: Code Quality — 87.5% (strict: 85.0%) (3 of 8 checks failing)
   Impact: fixing this is worth ~+2.1 pts on overall score
   Auto-fixable: 3 similar findings. Run `desloppify fix orphaned_imports --dry-run` to fix all at once.
   ```

5. **Special Item Types:**
   - **Synthesis card** (when `synthesis::pending` is in queue): Full planning orchestrator showing stages, enrichment gaps, next actions
   - **Subjective dimension cards**: Re-review tasks for design dimensions below target
   - **Epic cards**: Large epics with thesis, root cause, dependency order, action steps
   - **Cluster meta-items**: Collapsed groups showing member count, file distribution, sample IDs

6. **North Star Section** (bottom):
   ```
   North star: strict 87.3/100 → target 95.0 (+7.7 needed)
   ```
   Gap shown in dim; when target reached, prints in green: "target 95.0 reached!"

7. **Subjective Followup Summary**:
   ```
   Subjective: 2 dimensions below target, 1 stale, 3 unassessed.
   Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.
   ```

**Key Flags:**
- `--tier N`: Show only T1/T2/T3/T4 items
- `--count N`: Show top N items (default 1)
- `--scope PATTERN`: Filter by file/detector/dimension
- `--status open|fixed|all`: Which findings to include
- `--group item|detector|file`: Organize output
- `--explain`: Show ranking rationale for each item
- `--cluster NAME`: Drill into auto-cluster (show members)
- `--output FILE`: Write JSON payload to file

**Query Payload** (written to `.desloppify/query.json` for IDE integration):
Contains items, queue metadata, scores, narrative, plan summary.

---

#### **Phase 4: `desloppify fix` / Manual Work**

For each next item:
- Run the suggested command (e.g., `desloppify fix orphaned_imports --dry-run`)
- Or manually resolve and annotate: `desloppify plan done <id> --note "..." --attest "..."`
- Confidence scores and scoring engine credit fixes

**Score Update Display** (shown after fix):
```
Scores: overall 72.5/100 (+1.2)  objective 71.0/100 (+0.8)  strict 69.5/100 (+0.9)  verified 68.0/100 (+1.1)
Strict 69.5 (target: 95.0) — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement
```

---

#### **Phase 5: `desloppify scan` (Re-scan to Close Loop)**

Scan again:
- Reconciles plan (purges resolved findings from queue, resurfaces stale skips)
- Auto-clusters findings by detector
- Updates all four scores
- Triggers optional synthesis sync if subjective dimensions changed

---

### **2. THE "WORK QUEUE" CONCEPT**

The work queue is a **ranked list of actionable items** built dynamically every time `next` or `plan` is called. It is **not** persisted (that's what the living plan is for).

**Queue Construction Pipeline:**
1. **Filter findings** by status (open/fixed/all), scope (path/detector/dimension)
2. **Convert to queue items**: Each finding becomes an item dict with:
   - `id`, `tier`, `effective_tier`, `confidence`, `detector`, `file`, `summary`, `detail`
   - `kind`: "finding", "synthesis_needed", "epic", "subjective_dimension", "cluster"
   - `primary_command`: Action to take (auto-fix, manual plan done, or review)
   - `subjective_score`: For review findings
3. **Add synthetic items**: subjective dimension review tasks, synthesis blocker, epics (from plan)
4. **Sort mechanically**:
   - Subjective reviews first (T4, by score ascending)
   - Synthesis and epics next (T0-equivalent, by dependency order)
   - Mechanical findings (T1-4, by confidence and count)
5. **Apply plan order**: User-prioritized items bubble to top; skipped items sink to bottom
6. **Collapse auto-clusters**: Multiple findings → single meta-item (unless drilling in)
7. **Filter by tier/scope/count**

**Result**: `WorkQueueResult` TypedDict with:
- `items`: The ranked, filtered queue
- `total`: How many items match before `count` limit
- `tier_counts`: Dict of {1: N, 2: M, ...} for tier navigator
- `requested_tier` / `selected_tier`: Tier fallback info
- `available_tiers`: Non-empty tier list

---

### **3. STRICT SCORE AND SCORING DISPLAY**

The **strict score** is the primary target metric. It appears in:

**A. All state objects:**
- `state.strict_score`: Current strict score (0-100)
- `state.verified_strict_score`: Scan-verified fixes only (no provisional credits)
- Also: `overall_score`, `objective_score`

**B. Dimension scores** (in `state.dimension_scores`):
```python
{
  "File Health": {
    "score": 82.5,        # lenient health %
    "strict": 79.0,       # strict health % (what counts toward strict_score)
    "checks": 150,        # total checks for this dimension
    "issues": 30,         # open issues in this dimension
    "tier": 2,            # dimension's impact tier
    "detectors": {        # per-detector metadata
      "unused_imports": {
        "potential": 50,
        "pass_rate": 0.88,
        "issues": 6,
        ...
      }
    }
  },
  "Code Quality (subjective)": {
    "score": 75.0,
    "strict": 70.0,
    "detectors": {
      "subjective_assessment": {
        "components": ["Elegance", "Layering"],
        "placeholder": false,
        ...
      }
    }
  }
}
```

**C. Score Update Helper** (`print_score_update`):
Called after fix/resolve/plan operations. Shows:
```
Scores: overall 72.5/100 (+1.2)  objective 71.0/100 (+0.8)  strict 69.5/100 (+0.9)  verified 68.0/100 (+1.1)
Strict 69.5 (target: 95.0) — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement
```

**D. Next command scorecard** (from `show` surfaces):
Displays dimension health table with both `score` and `strict` columns. Example:
```
| Dimension      | Score | Strict | Issues |
|----------------|-------|--------|--------|
| File Health    | 82.5% | 79.0%  | 30/150 |
| Code Quality   | 75.0% | 70.0%  | 12/45  |
```

**E. Follow-up nudges** (bottom of `next` output):
Shows **low-scoring dimensions** below target, **stale reviews**, **unassessed dimensions**, **open review findings**, and **files needing re-review**.

---

### **4. THE SHOW COMMAND**

**`desloppify show <pattern>`** filters and displays findings by file/detector/dimension/pattern.

**Entity Resolution:**
- `show concerns`: Design concerns from mechanical signals
- `show subjective`: All subjective review findings + integrity dashboard
- `show <detector>`: Findings from one detector (e.g., `show unused_imports`)
- `show <file>`: Findings in that file
- `show <dimension>`: All findings contributing to a dimension

**Output per finding:**
```
○ T2 [high] Find orphaned imports in typescript files
  Lines: 45, 67
  Category: Code Quality
  Summary ID: ts::orphaned_imports::abc123def
```

**Summary at bottom:**
- Grouped by file
- Noise budget indicators (hidden findings due to budget)
- Plan context (if active)
- Subjective followup (dimensions below target)

---

### **5. KEY DATA STRUCTURES**

**Finding** (in state.findings dict):
```python
{
  "id": "ts::unused_imports::abc123",
  "detector": "unused_imports",
  "file": "src/main.ts",
  "tier": 2,
  "confidence": "high",
  "summary": "Unused import: 'lodash'",
  "detail": {
    "importers": 1,
    "line": 45,
    "lines": [45, 67],
    "count": 2,
    ...
  },
  "status": "open",  # or "fixed", "wontfix", "false_positive", "auto_resolved"
  "note": "Keeping for backward compat",
  "first_seen": "2025-03-01T10:00:00+00:00",
  "last_seen": "2025-03-01T14:30:00+00:00",
  "resolved_at": null,
  "reopen_count": 0,
  "suppressed": false,
  "lang": "typescript",
  "zone": "production"  # or "test", "config", "generated", "script", "vendor"
}
```

**QueueBuildOptions** (for constructing a work queue):
```python
QueueBuildOptions(
  tier=None,                      # Filter to specific tier or None
  count=1,                        # How many items to return
  scan_path=None,                 # Scope (from state.scan_path)
  scope=None,                     # Additional scope filter
  status="open",                  # "open", "fixed", "all"
  include_subjective=True,        # Include review findings
  subjective_threshold=100.0,     # Only include subjective < threshold
  chronic=False,                  # Only chronic (reopened 2+x)
  no_tier_fallback=False,         # Disable auto-fallback to next tier
  explain=False,                  # Include ranking explanation
  plan=plan_dict,                 # Living plan for reordering
  include_skipped=False,          # Include skipped items
  cluster="name",                 # Drill into cluster
  collapse_clusters=True          # Auto-collapse clusters into meta-items
)
```

**WorkQueueResult**:
```python
{
  "items": [...],                 # Ranked, filtered queue items
  "total": 45,                    # Total matching (pre-count limit)
  "tier_counts": {1: 5, 2: 12, 3: 28, 4: 0},
  "requested_tier": 2,
  "selected_tier": 2,
  "fallback_reason": None,
  "available_tiers": [1, 2, 3],
  "grouped": {"detector_name": [...], ...}
}
```

---

### **6. STRICT SCORE VS. OTHER SCORES**

| Score | Description | Usage |
|-------|-------------|-------|
| `overall_score` | Lenient (includes provisional subjective) | Dashboard, big picture |
| `objective_score` | Mechanical only (no subjective) | Pure code quality baseline |
| `strict_score` | Strict (subjective only if stable) | **Primary target metric** |
| `verified_strict_score` | Only scan-verified fixes | Only credits fixes scans see |

**When is `strict` shown?**
- Every `next` output (North Star section)
- Every `score_update` (after fix/resolve)
- In dimension scores as `"strict"` field
- In `scan_history` (progression tracking)

**Target strict score:**
- Defaults to 95.0 (configurable in `desloppify.json`)
- Displayed as: `Strict X.X (target: Y.Y) — gap: +Z.Z needed`
- Goal: Close the gap by working through next items

---

### **7. SPECIAL QUEUE ITEMS**

Beyond regular findings, the queue can include:

**A. Synthesis::pending** (ID: `"synthesis::pending"`)
- Appears as T0 blocker when plan has review findings to organize
- Shows: Total review findings, current epics, synthesis stages (observe/reflect/organize/complete)
- Contains enrichment gaps (clusters missing description/action_steps)
- Navigates user through planning phases

**B. Subjective dimension tasks** (ID: `"subjective::<dimension>"`)
- Tier 4, represents re-review need
- Shows: Dimension name, current strict score, open review count
- Primary command: `desloppify review --prepare` (for holistic) or `desloppify show subjective` (for file-based)

**C. Epics** (from plan.clusters with thesis)
- Tier 1, dependency-ordered
- Shows: thesis, direction (simplify/enhance), root_cause, action_steps, finding_ids
- Primary command: `desloppify plan focus <epic_name>` (then work through findings)

**D. Cluster meta-items** (auto-clusters)
- Single card representing multiple findings
- Shows: batch size, file distribution, sample IDs, action (command to run)
- Primary command: `desloppify next --cluster <name> --count 10` (drill in)

---

### **8. INFORMATION FLOW: NEXT → QUERY.JSON**

Every `next` command writes `.desloppify/query.json`:
```json
{
  "command": "next",
  "items": [
    {
      "id": "...",
      "kind": "finding",
      "tier": 2,
      "effective_tier": 2,
      "confidence": "high",
      "detector": "unused_imports",
      "summary": "...",
      "primary_command": "desloppify fix unused_imports --dry-run",
      "queue_position": 1,
      "plan_description": "...",
      "plan_cluster": {...},
      ...
    }
  ],
  "queue": {
    "tier_counts": {1: 5, 2: 12, ...},
    "total": 45,
    "available_tiers": [1, 2, 3]
  },
  "overall_score": 72.5,
  "objective_score": 71.0,
  "strict_score": 69.5,
  "scorecard_dimensions": [...],
  "subjective_measures": [...],
  "narrative": {...},
  "plan": {
    "active": true,
    "focus": "epic_name",
    "clusters": [...],
    "total_ordered": 12,
    "total_skipped": 3
  }
}
```

**Used by:** IDE extensions, automated agents, JSON output (`--output FILE`)

---

### **9. SUMMARY: USER EXPERIENCE**

**Typical session:**
1. `desloppify scan` → populates state + plan reconciliation
2. `desloppify plan` → shows markdown plan with dimension health
3. `desloppify next --count 5` → see top 5 tasks (team triage)
4. `desloppify next` (repeat) → work through items one at a time
5. `desloppify fix <detector> --dry-run` OR `desloppify plan done <id> --note "..." --attest "..."`
6. **Score nudges** after each operation show progress toward strict target
7. `desloppify next --tier 2` → switch to different tier if needed
8. `desloppify plan focus <epic>` → enter focused mode on an epic
9. Repeat until `desloppify next` shows "Nothing to do! Strict score: 95.0/100"

**Key Takeaway:** The system provides **continuous progress visibility** (strict score), **ranked prioritization** (tier + confidence + plan), and **plan-aware synthesis** (epics + clusters) to guide user through codebase improvements in order of business impact.

    10→def print_score_update(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    13→    *,
    14→    config: dict | None = None,
    15→    label: str = "Scores",
    16→) -> None:
    17→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    18→
    19→    Args:
    20→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    21→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    22→        config: Project config dict (loaded from disk if not provided).
    23→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    24→    """
    25→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    26→    if (
    27→        new.overall is None
    28→        or new.objective is None
    29→        or new.strict is None
    30→        or new.verified is None
    31→    ):
    32→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    33→        return
    34→
    35→    overall_s, overall_c = format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    36→    objective_s, objective_c = format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    37→    strict_s, strict_c = format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    38→    verified_s, verified_c = format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
    39→
    40→    print(
    41→        f"\n  {label}: "
    42→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
    43→        + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}", objective_c)
    44→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
    45→        + colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}", verified_c)
    46→    )
    47→

    49→    if config is None:
    50→        from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
    51→
    52→        config = config_mod.load_config()
    53→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config

    56→    print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target)

    59→def print_strict_target_nudge(
    60→    strict: float, target: float, *, show_next: bool = True,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Print a one-liner with strict→target and optional next-command nudge."""
    63→    gap = round(target - strict, 1)
    64→    if gap > 0:
    65→        suffix = " — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement" if show_next else ""
    66→        print(colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} (target: {target:.1f}){suffix}", "dim"))
    67→    else:

    69→

   113→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:

   125→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   126→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")

   145→        # Sample IDs

   152→    # Action

   358→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   359→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   360→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   361→        return
   362→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   363→    if not explain:
   364→        return
   365→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   366→    if available:
   367→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   368→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   369→
   370→
   371→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   372→    if queue.get("items"):
   373→        return False
   374→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   375→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   376→    if tier is not None:
   377→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   378→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   379→        if available:
   380→            commands = " | ".join(
   381→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   382→            )
   383→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   384→    return True

   387→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   388→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   389→    if not plan:
   390→        return
   391→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
