```
   400→def _render_subjective_task(item: dict) -> None:
   401→    """Render a subjective dimension review as a proper task card."""
   402→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   403→    dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "unknown")
   404→    strict_score = float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
   405→    open_review = int(detail.get("open_review_findings", 0))
   406→    is_stale = "stale" in item.get("summary", "")
   407→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "desloppify review --prepare")
   408→
   409→    tag = " \u2014 stale, re-review" if is_stale else ""
   410→    print(colorize(f"  (Subjective review{tag})", "bold"))
   411→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   412→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   413→    print(f"  Dimension: {dim_name}")
   414→    print(f"  Score: {strict_score:.1f}%")
   415→    if open_review:
   416→        print(f"  Open review findings: {open_review}")
   417→    print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary}", "cyan"))
   418→    print(colorize(
   419→        "  Note: re-review scores what it finds \u2014 scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   420→        "dim",
   421→    ))
   422→
   423→
   424→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   425→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   426→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   427→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   428→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   429→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   430→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   431→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   432→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   433→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   434→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   435→    stages = detail.get("stage_progress", {})
   436→    enrichment_gaps = detail.get("enrichment_gaps", [])
   437→    enriched_count = detail.get("enriched_cluster_count", 0)
   438→
   439→    print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
   440→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   441→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   442→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
   443→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
   444→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   445→    print()
   446→    if new:
   447→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   448→    if resolved:
   449→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   450→    if epic_count:
   451→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   452→
   453→    # Stage progress
   454→    print()
   455→    print("  Stages:")
   456→    for stage_name, label in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS:
   457→        if stage_name in stages:
   458→            print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label}", "green"))
   459→        elif SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
   460→            print(colorize(f"    \u2192 {label} (current)", "yellow"))
   461→        else:
   462→            print(colorize(f"    \u25cb {label}", "dim"))
   463→
   464→    # Show enrichment status when in organize phase
   465→    if "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
   466→        if enrichment_gaps:
   467→            print(colorize(f"\n  {len(enrichment_gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   468→            for gap_name, gap_fields in enrichment_gaps:
   469→                print(colorize(f"    {gap_name}: missing {', '.join(gap_fields)}", "yellow"))
   470→        elif enriched_count > 0:
   471→            print(colorize(f"\n  All {enriched_count} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
   472→
   473→    # Next command based on current stage
   474→    print()
   475→    if "observe" not in stages:
   476→        print(f"  Next: {SYNTH_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   477→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   478→        print(f"  Next: {SYNTH_CMD_REFLECT}")
   479→        print(f"    (View completed work and recurring patterns, then record reflect stage)")
   480→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   481→        if enrichment_gaps:
   482→            print("  Enrich clusters before proceeding:")
   483→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH}")
   484→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}   (when all clusters are enriched)")
   485→        elif enriched_count == 0:
   486→            print("  Defer contradictions, then create and enrich clusters:")
   487→            print("    desloppify plan skip <hash>   (temporarily defer contradictory findings)")
   488→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE}")
   489→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD}")
   490→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS}")
   491→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   492→        else:
   493→            print(f"  All clusters enriched! Record organize:")
   494→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   495→        if detail.get("has_prior_synthesis"):
   496→            print(f"  Skip: {SYNTH_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING}")
   497→    else:
   498→        print(f"  Next: {SYNTH_CMD_COMPLETE}")
   499→
   500→    print()
   501→    print(colorize("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow"))
   502→    print()
   503→    print(colorize("  Forbidden during this phase:", "red"))
   504→    print("    - desloppify fix / resolve / editing code")
   505→    print(colorize("  Allowed: plan skip (to defer contradictory findings during organize)", "dim"))
   506→    print()
   507→    print(colorize("  View plan:", "dim"))
   508→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan synthesize  \u2014 findings and stage progress", "dim"))
   509→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster list \u2014 current clusters", "dim"))
   510→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan queue        \u2014 items in priority order", "dim"))
   511→
   512→
   513→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   514→    """Render an epic card."""
   515→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   516→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   517→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   518→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   519→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   520→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   521→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   522→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   523→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   524→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   525→
   526→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   527→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   528→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   529→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   530→    if root_cause:
   531→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   532→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   533→    if total_epics:
   534→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   535→    print()
   536→    if action_steps:
   537→        print("  Steps:")
   538→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   539→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   540→        print()
   541→    if finding_ids:
   542→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   543→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   544→        for fid in shown:
   545→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   546→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   547→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   548→        print()
   549→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   550→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   551→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   552→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   553→
   554→
   555→def render_terminal_items(
   556→    items: list[dict],
   557→    dim_scores: dict,
   558→    findings_scoped: dict,
   559→    *,
   560→    group: str,
   561→    explain: bool,
   562→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   563→    plan: dict | None = None,
   564→) -> None:
   565→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   566→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   567→
   568→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   569→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   570→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   571→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   572→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   573→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   574→        visible = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed")
   575→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({visible} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   576→
   577→        # Exclude the synthesis item from the focus count — it's a queue-level
   578→        # blocker, not a cluster member, so don't count it in "N of M remaining".
   579→        focus_items = [it for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed"]
   580→        if len(focus_items) == 0:
   581→            print(colorize(
   582→                "  This cluster has no remaining items. "
   583→                "Run `desloppify plan focus --clear` to unfocus, "
   584→                "or `desloppify plan cluster show` to inspect.",
   585→                "yellow",
   586→            ))
   587→
   588→    # When synthesis is pending, show subjective review tasks (they must
   589→    # complete before synthesis) then the planning card — don't leak
   590→    # mechanical implementation tasks that would tempt agents to skip planning.
   591→    has_synthesis = any(it.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed" for it in items)
   592→    if has_synthesis:
   593→        # Collect the prefix: subjective items + synthesis card
   594→        prefix_items = []
   595→        for it in items:
   596→            if it.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   597→                prefix_items.append(it)
   598→                break
   599→            if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   600→                prefix_items.append(it)
   601→            else:
   602→                break  # non-subjective, non-synthesis — stop
   603→        for idx, it in enumerate(prefix_items):
   604→            if idx > 0:
   605→                print()
   606→            kind = it.get("kind", "")
   607→            queue_pos = it.get("queue_position")
   608→            if queue_pos and len(prefix_items) > 1:
   609→                label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   610→            else:
   611→                pos_str = f"  (#{queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   612→                label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   613→            print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   614→            if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   615→                _render_synthesis_item(it)
   616→            else:
   617→                _render_subjective_task(it)
   618→        return
   619→
   620→    if group != "item":
   621→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   622→        return
   623→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   624→        if idx > 0:
   625→            print()
   626→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   627→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   628→
   629→        # All item types get the same position label — no visual hierarchy
   630→        # that makes synthetic items look like metadata instead of tasks.
   631→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   632→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   633→        elif len(items) > 1:
   634→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   635→        else:
   636→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   637→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   638→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   639→
   640→        if kind == "epic":
   641→            _render_epic_item(item)
   642→            continue
   643→        if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   644→            _render_subjective_task(item)
   645→            continue
   646→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   647→
   648→
   649→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   650→    if len(items) != 1:
   651→        return
   652→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   653→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic", "subjective_dimension"):
   654→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   655→    if kind != "finding":
   656→        return
   657→    item = items[0]
   658→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   659→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   660→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   661→        primary = item.get(
   662→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   663→        )
   664→        print(f"    {primary}")
   665→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   666→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   667→        return
   668→
   669→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   670→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   671→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   672→        print(f"    {primary}")
   673→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   674→    else:
   675→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   676→
   677→    print(
   678→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   679→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   680→    )
   681→    print(
   682→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   683→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   684→    )
   685→
   686→
   687→def render_followup_nudges(
   688→    state: dict,
   689→    dim_scores: dict,
   690→    findings_scoped: dict,
   691→    *,
   692→    strict_score: float | None,
   693→    target_strict_score: float,
   694→) -> None:
   695→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   696→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   697→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   698→        state,
   699→        subjective_entries,
   700→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   701→        max_quality_items=3,
   702→        max_integrity_items=5,
   703→    )
   704→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   705→    if strict_score is not None:
   706→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   707→        if gap > 0:
   708→            print(
   709→                colorize(
   710→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   711→                    "cyan",
   712→                )
   713→            )
   714→        else:
   715→            print(
   716→                colorize(
   717→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   718→                    "green",
   719→                )
   720→            )
   721→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   722→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   723→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   724→
   725→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   726→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   727→        findings_scoped
   728→    )
   729→    parts: list[str] = []
   730→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   731→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   732→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   733→    open_review = [
   734→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   735→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   736→    ]
   737→    if low_dims:
   738→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   739→    if stale_count:
   740→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   741→    if unassessed_count:
   742→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   743→    if len(open_review):
   744→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   745→    if coverage_open > 0:
   746→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   747→
   748→    if parts:
   749→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   750→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   751→
   752→
   753→__all__ = [
   754→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   755→    "render_followup_nudges",
   756→    "render_queue_header",

   759→    "scorecard_subjective",
   760→    "show_empty_queue",
   761→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   762→]
   763→
```

```
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    33→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    35→
    36→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    37→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    38→
    39→    # Synthesis phase guardrail
    40→    from desloppify.engine.plan import synthesis_phase_banner
    41→    _banner = synthesis_phase_banner(plan)
    42→    if _banner:
    43→        print(colorize(f"  {_banner}", "yellow"))
    44→        print()
    45→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    46→    if total_skipped:
    47→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    48→    else:
    49→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    50→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    51→    if clusters:
    52→        manual = {n: c for n, c in clusters.items() if not c.get("auto")}
    53→        auto = {n: c for n, c in clusters.items() if c.get("auto")}
    54→        if manual:
    55→            for name, cluster in manual.items():
    56→                desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    57→                member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    58→                marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    59→                desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    60→                print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    61→        if auto:
    62→            auto_items = sum(len(c.get("finding_ids", [])) for c in auto.values())
    63→            focused_auto = f" (focused: {active})" if active and active in auto else ""
    64→            print(f"    ({len(auto)} auto-clusters, {auto_items} findings{focused_auto})")
    65→    if described or noted:
    66→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    67→    if active:
    68→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    69→    if superseded:

    71→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
    72→    if epics:

    74→        for name, epic in epics.items():

    76→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
    77→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
    78→            fcount = len(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
    79→            safe_tag = " [agent-safe]" if epic.get("agent_safe") else ""
    80→            print(f"    {name} [{direction}] ({fcount} findings, {status}){safe_tag}")
    81→            if thesis:
    82→                print(f"      {thesis}")
    83→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    84→    if meta and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
    85→        print(f"  Strategy:         {meta['strategy_summary']}")
    86→        print(f"  Last synthesis:   v{meta.get('version', 0)} ({meta.get('trigger', 'unknown')} @ {meta.get('last_run', 'never')})")

    99→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   100→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   101→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   102→
   103→    if plan_action is None:
   104→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   105→        return

   108→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   109→        return

   113→
   114→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   115→        return
   116→
   117→    if plan_action == "reset":
   118→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   119→        return
   120→
   121→    if plan_action == "move":
   122→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
   123→
   124→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   125→        return
   126→
   127→    if plan_action in (
   128→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   129→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   130→    ):
   131→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
   132→            cmd_plan_describe,
   133→            cmd_plan_done,
   134→            cmd_plan_focus,
   135→            cmd_plan_note,
   136→            cmd_plan_reopen,
   137→            cmd_plan_skip,
   138→            cmd_plan_unskip,
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        dispatch = {
   142→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   143→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   144→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   145→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   146→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   147→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   148→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   149→        }
   150→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   151→        return
   152→
   153→    if plan_action == "cluster":

   155→

   161→
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_policy import build_plan_queue
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, synthesis_phase_banner
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    40→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    41→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    42→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    46→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    47→        plan_data = plan
    48→
    49→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster
    50→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    51→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    52→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    53→        if active_cluster:
    54→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    55→
    56→    queue = build_plan_queue(
    57→        state,
    58→        tier=tier_filter,
    59→        count=None,
    60→        status="open",
    61→        plan=plan_data,
    62→        include_skipped=include_skipped,

    95→                " hidden \u2014 use --include-skipped)",
    96→                "dim",
    97→            ))
    98→
    99→    if not items:
   100→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    # Determine which items to show
   104→    display_items = items
   105→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   106→        display_items = items[:top]
   107→
   108→    # Build rows
   109→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   110→    rows: list[list[str]] = []

   115→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":

   143→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   144→            if "::" in item_id:
   145→                hash_suffix = item_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8]

   147→                summary = f"{_truncate(summary, 36)} [{hash_suffix}]"
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    build_synthesis_item,
    13→    scope_matches,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    16→    build_finding_items,
    17→    choose_fallback_tier,
    18→    group_queue_items,
    19→    item_explain,
    20→    item_sort_key,
    21→    tier_counts,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    27→class QueueBuildOptions:
    28→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    29→
    30→    tier: int | None = None
    31→    count: int | None = 1
    32→    scan_path: str | None = None
    33→    scope: str | None = None
    34→    status: str = "open"
    35→    include_subjective: bool = True
    36→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    37→    chronic: bool = False
    38→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    39→    explain: bool = False
    40→    plan: dict | None = None
    41→    include_skipped: bool = False
    42→    cluster: str | None = None
    43→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    44→
    45→
    46→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    47→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    48→
    49→    items: list[dict]
    50→    total: int
    51→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    52→    requested_tier: int | None
    53→    selected_tier: int | None
    54→    fallback_reason: str | None
    55→    available_tiers: list[int]
    56→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    57→
    58→
    59→def _apply_plan_order(
    60→    items: list[dict],
    61→    plan: dict,
    62→    *,
    63→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    64→    cluster: str | None = None,
    65→    state: dict | None = None,
    66→) -> list[dict]:
    67→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    68→
    69→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    70→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    71→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    72→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    73→    """
    74→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    75→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    76→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    77→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    78→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    79→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    80→
    81→    # Build lookup
    82→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    85→
    86→    # Build synthetic items for skipped entries missing from the work queue
    87→    if include_skipped and state:
    88→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    89→        for fid in skipped_ids:
    90→            if fid not in by_id and fid in findings:
    91→                f = findings[fid]
    92→                by_id[fid] = {
    93→                    "id": fid,
    94→                    "kind": "finding",
    95→                    "tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    96→                    "effective_tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    97→                    "confidence": f.get("confidence", "medium"),
    98→                    "detector": f.get("detector", ""),
    99→                    "file": f.get("file", ""),
   100→                    "summary": f.get("summary", ""),
   101→                    "status": f.get("status", "open"),
   102→                }
   103→
   104→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   105→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   106→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   107→        if override.get("description"):
   108→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   109→        if override.get("note"):
   110→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   111→        if override.get("cluster"):
   112→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   113→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   114→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   115→                "name": cluster_name,
   116→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   117→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   118→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   119→            }
   120→
   121→    # Normalise queue_order so subjective:: items always precede
   122→    # synthesis::pending — reviews generate the findings synthesis
   123→    # organises, so they must run first regardless of insertion order.
   124→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
   125→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in queue_order:
   126→        subj = [fid for fid in queue_order if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)]
   127→        if subj:
   128→            # Partition: subjective items first, then synthesis, then the rest
   129→            rest = [
   130→                fid for fid in queue_order
   131→                if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID and not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   132→            ]
   133→            queue_order = subj + [SYNTHESIS_ID] + rest
   134→
   135→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   136→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   137→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   138→    for fid in queue_order:
   139→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   140→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   141→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   142→
   143→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   144→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   145→    for item in items:
   146→        item_id = item["id"]
   147→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   148→            continue
   149→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   150→            skipped_items.append(item)
   151→        else:
   152→            remaining.append(item)
   153→
   154→    # Assign queue positions
   155→    result = ordered + remaining
   156→    if include_skipped:
   157→        result = result + skipped_items
   158→
   159→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   160→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   161→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   162→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   163→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   164→            if skip_entry:
   165→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   166→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   167→                if skip_reason:
   168→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   169→
   170→    # Filter to cluster if requested — but never filter out synthesis or
   171→    # subjective items; they are queue-level blockers that outrank cluster membership.
   172→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   173→    if effective_cluster:
   174→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   175→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   176→        result = [
   177→            item for item in result
   178→            if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids
   179→            or item.get("kind") in ("synthesis_needed", "subjective_dimension")
   180→        ]
   181→
   182→    return result
   183→
   184→
   185→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   186→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   187→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
   188→    if kind in ("synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   189→        return tier == 1  # Always T1
   190→    if kind == "cluster":
   191→        return any(
   192→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   193→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   194→        )
   195→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   196→
   197→
   198→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   199→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   200→    try:
   201→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   202→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   203→        if meta:
   204→            return meta.action_type
   205→    except ImportError:
   206→        pass
   207→    return "manual_fix"
   208→
   209→
   210→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   211→
   212→
   213→def _build_epic_items(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   214→    """Build synthetic queue items for each pending/in_progress synthesis-cluster."""
   215→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import synthesis_clusters
   216→
   217→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   218→    if not epics:
   219→        return []
   220→
   221→    total_epics = len(epics)
   222→    items: list[dict] = []
   223→    for epic_name, epic in sorted(
   224→        epics.items(),
   225→        key=lambda kv: kv[1].get("dependency_order", 999),
   226→    ):
   227→        status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   228→        if status == "completed":
   229→            continue
   230→        finding_ids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   231→        if not finding_ids:
   232→            continue
   233→
   234→        items.append({
   235→            "id": epic_name,
   236→            "kind": "epic",
   237→            "tier": 1,
   238→            "effective_tier": 1,
   239→            "confidence": "high",
   240→            "detector": "epic",
   241→            "file": ".",
   242→            "summary": f"[{epic.get('direction', 'simplify')}] {epic.get('thesis', '')}",
   243→            "status": "open",
   244→            "primary_command": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   247→                "direction": epic.get("direction", ""),
   248→                "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   249→                "epic_status": status,
   250→                "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   251→                "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   252→                "total_epics": total_epics,
   253→                "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   254→                "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   255→            },
   256→        })
   257→    return items
   258→
   259→
   260→def _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   261→    *,
   262→    all_items: list[dict],
   263→    plan: dict,
   264→    state: StateModel,
   265→    scope: str | None,
   266→) -> None:
   267→    """Inject synthesis::pending and epic items into the queue."""
   268→    existing_ids = {item.get("id") for item in all_items}
   269→
   270→    synthesis_item = build_synthesis_item(plan, state)
   271→    if synthesis_item and synthesis_item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   272→        if scope_matches(synthesis_item, scope):
   273→            all_items.append(synthesis_item)
   274→            existing_ids.add(synthesis_item["id"])
   275→
   276→    epic_items = _build_epic_items(plan, state)
   277→    for item in epic_items:
   278→        if item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   279→            if scope_matches(item, scope):
   280→                all_items.append(item)
   281→                existing_ids.add(item["id"])
   282→
   283→
   284→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   285→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   286→
   287→    Synthesis-clusters (those with a thesis) are represented by epic
   288→    items, so their member findings are excluded from cluster collapsing.
   289→    """
   290→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   291→
   292→    if not clusters:
   293→        return items
   294→
   295→    # Synthesis-clusters have their own epic queue items, so collect their
   296→    # finding IDs to exclude from regular cluster collapsing.
   297→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   298→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   299→        if cluster.get("thesis") and cluster.get("status") != "completed":
   300→            epic_finding_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   301→
   302→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   303→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   304→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   305→        if not cluster.get("auto") or cluster.get("thesis"):
   306→            continue
   307→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   308→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids:
   309→                fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   310→
   311→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   312→        return items
   313→
   314→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   315→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   316→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   317→
   318→    for item in items:
   319→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   320→        if cname:
   321→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   322→        else:
   323→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   324→
   325→    # Build cluster meta-items
   326→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   327→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   328→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   329→        if len(members) < 2:
   330→            result.extend(members)
   331→            continue
   332→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   333→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   334→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   335→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   336→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   337→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   338→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   339→            action_type = "reorganize"
   340→        else:
   341→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   342→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   343→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   344→                action_type = "refactor"
   345→
   346→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   347→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   348→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   349→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   350→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   351→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   352→        else:
   353→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   354→
   355→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   356→        if not primary_command:
   357→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   358→
   359→        meta_item: dict = {
   360→            "id": cname,
   361→            "kind": "cluster",
   362→            "action_type": action_type,
   363→            "summary": summary,
   364→            "members": members,
   365→            "member_count": len(members),
   366→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   367→            "cluster_name": cname,
   368→            "cluster_auto": True,
   369→            "confidence": "high",
   370→            "detector": detector,
   371→            "file": "",
   372→        }
   373→        result.append(meta_item)
   374→
   375→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   376→    return result
   377→
   378→
   379→def build_work_queue(
   380→    state: StateModel,
   381→    *,
   382→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   383→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   384→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   385→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   386→
   387→    status = resolved_options.status
   388→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   389→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   390→    try:
   391→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   392→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   393→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   394→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   395→
   396→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   397→        state,
   398→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   399→        status_filter=status,
   400→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   401→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   402→    )
   403→
   404→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   405→    if (
   406→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   407→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   408→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   409→    ):
   410→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   411→            state,
   412→            state.get("findings", {}),
   413→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   414→        )
   415→        for item in subjective_items:
   416→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   417→                all_items.append(item)
   418→
   419→    # Inject synthesis::pending and epic items when plan has them
   420→    if resolved_options.plan:
   421→        _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   422→            all_items=all_items,
   423→            plan=resolved_options.plan,
   424→            state=state,
   425→            scope=resolved_options.scope,
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   429→
   430→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   431→    if resolved_options.plan:
   432→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   433→            all_items,
   434→            resolved_options.plan,
   435→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   436→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   437→            state=state if resolved_options.include_skipped else None,
   438→        )
   439→
   440→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   441→    should_collapse = (
   442→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   443→        and resolved_options.plan
   444→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   445→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   446→    )
   447→    if should_collapse:
   448→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   449→
   450→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   451→
   452→    requested_tier = (
   453→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   454→    )
   455→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   456→    fallback_reason = None
   457→    filtered = all_items
   458→
   459→    if requested_tier is not None:
   460→        filtered = [
   461→            item
   462→            for item in all_items
   463→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   464→        ]
   465→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   466→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   467→            if chosen is not None:
   468→                selected_tier = chosen
   469→                filtered = [
   470→                    item
   471→                    for item in all_items
   472→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   473→                ]
   474→                fallback_reason = (
   475→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   476→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   477→                )
   478→        elif not filtered:
   479→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   480→
   481→    total = len(filtered)
   482→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   483→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   484→
   485→    if resolved_options.explain:
   486→        for item in filtered:
   487→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   488→
   489→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   490→    return {
   491→        "items": filtered,
   492→        "total": total,
   493→        "tier_counts": counts,
   494→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   495→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   496→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   497→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   498→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   499→    }
   500→
   501→
   502→__all__ = [
   503→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   504→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   505→    "WorkQueueResult",
   506→    "build_work_queue",
   507→    "group_queue_items",
   508→]
   509→
```

```
   131→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   132→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   133→    now = utc_now()
   134→    return {
   135→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   136→        "created": now,
   137→        "updated": now,
   138→        "queue_order": [],
   139→        "deferred": [],
   140→        "skipped": {},
   141→        "active_cluster": None,
   142→        "overrides": {},
   143→        "clusters": {},
   144→        "superseded": {},
   145→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   146→        "deleted_clusters": [],
   147→        "completed_clusters": [],
   148→    }
   149→
   150→
   151→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   152→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   153→
   154→    Handles migrations:
   155→    - v1 → v2: deferred list → skipped dict
   156→    - v3 → v4: epics dict → synthesis-clusters in clusters dict
   157→    """
   158→    defaults = empty_plan()
   159→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   160→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   161→
   162→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   163→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   164→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   165→        plan["deferred"] = []
   166→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   167→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   168→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   169→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   170→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   171→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   172→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   173→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   174→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   175→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   176→    meta = plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]
   177→    if not isinstance(meta.get("synthesis_stages"), dict):
   178→        meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
   179→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deleted_clusters"), list):
   180→        plan["deleted_clusters"] = []
   181→    if not isinstance(plan.get("completed_clusters"), list):
   182→        plan["completed_clusters"] = []
   183→
   184→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   185→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   186→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   187→    if deferred:
   188→        now = utc_now()
   189→        for fid in list(deferred):
   190→            if fid not in skipped:
   191→                skipped[fid] = {
   192→                    "finding_id": fid,
   193→                    "kind": "temporary",
   194→                    "reason": None,
   195→                    "note": None,
   196→                    "attestation": None,
   197→                    "created_at": now,
   198→                    "review_after": None,
   199→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   200→                }
   201→        deferred.clear()
   202→
   203→    # Normalize cluster fields
   204→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   205→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   206→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   207→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   208→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   209→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   210→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   211→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   212→
   213→    # Migrate epics → clusters (v3 → v4)
   214→    old_epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
   215→    if old_epics and isinstance(old_epics, dict):
   216→        clusters = plan["clusters"]
   217→        now = utc_now()
   218→        for name, epic in old_epics.items():
   219→            if name not in clusters:
   220→                clusters[name] = {
   221→                    "name": name,
   222→                    "description": epic.get("thesis"),
   223→                    "finding_ids": list(epic.get("finding_ids", [])),
   224→                    "auto": True,
   225→                    "cluster_key": f"epic::{name}",
   226→                    "action": f"desloppify plan focus {name}",
   227→                    "user_modified": False,
   228→                    "created_at": epic.get("created_at", now),
   229→                    "updated_at": epic.get("updated_at", now),
   230→                    "thesis": epic.get("thesis"),
   231→                    "direction": epic.get("direction"),
   232→                    "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause"),
   233→                    "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   234→                    "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   235→                    "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   236→                    "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   237→                    "synthesis_version": epic.get("synthesis_version", 0),
   238→                    "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),
   239→                    "dismissed": epic.get("dismissed", []),
   240→                    "source_clusters": epic.get("source_clusters", []),
   241→                }
   242→
   243→
   244→def synthesis_clusters(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   245→    """Return clusters that were created by epic synthesis (have a thesis)."""
   246→    return {
   247→        name: cluster
   248→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   249→        if cluster.get("thesis")
   250→    }
   251→
   252→
   253→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   254→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   255→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   256→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   257→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   258→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   259→
   260→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   261→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   262→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   263→    if overlap:
   264→        raise ValueError(
   265→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   266→        )
   267→
   268→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   269→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   270→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   271→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   272→            raise ValueError(
   273→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   274→            )
   275→
   276→
   277→__all__ = [
   278→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   279→    "EpicSynthesisMeta",
   280→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   281→    "Cluster",
   282→    "CompletedCluster",
   283→    "ItemOverride",
   284→    "PlanModel",
   285→    "SkipEntry",
   286→    "SupersededEntry",
   287→    "SynthesisStageReport",
   288→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   289→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   290→    "empty_plan",
   291→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   292→    "synthesis_clusters",
   293→    "validate_plan",
   294→]
   295→
```

```
1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_synthesis_guardrail_banner
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→
    13→from .apply_flow import (
    14→    _apply_and_report,
    15→    _detect,
    16→    _print_fix_summary,
    17→    _report_dry_run,
    18→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    19→)
    20→from .options import _load_fixer
    21→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    22→
    23→
    24→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    25→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    26→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    27→    if fixer_name == "review":
    28→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    29→        return
    30→
    31→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    32→    path = Path(args.path)
    33→
    34→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    35→
    36→    # Synthesis phase guardrail
    37→    print_synthesis_guardrail_banner()
    38→
    39→    if not dry_run:
    40→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    41→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    42→    if not entries:
    43→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    47→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    48→        results = raw.entries
    49→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    50→    else:
    51→        results = raw
    52→        skip_reasons = {}
    53→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    54→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    55→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    56→
    57→    if dry_run and results:
    58→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    59→
    60→    if not dry_run:
    61→        _apply_and_report(
    62→            args,
    63→            path,
    64→            fixer,
    65→            fixer_name,
    66→            entries,
    67→            results,
    68→            total_items,
    69→            lang,
    70→            skip_reasons,
    71→        )
    72→    else:
    73→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    74→    print()
    75→
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   147→    for fid in finding_ids:
   148→        # Record queue position before removal so unskip can restore it
   149→        original_pos: int | None = None
   150→        if fid in order:
   151→            original_pos = order.index(fid)
   152→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   153→        skip_entry: SkipEntry = {
   154→            "finding_id": fid,
   155→            "kind": kind,
   156→            "reason": reason,
   157→            "note": note,
   158→            "attestation": attestation,
   159→            "created_at": now,
   160→            "review_after": review_after,
   161→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   162→        }
   163→        skip_entry["original_position"] = original_pos
   164→        skipped[fid] = skip_entry
   165→        count += 1
   166→    return count
   167→
   168→
   169→def unskip_items(
   170→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   171→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   172→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   173→
   174→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   175→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   176→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   177→    """
   178→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   179→    count = 0
   180→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   181→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   182→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   183→    to_restore_pos: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
   184→    to_restore_end: list[str] = []
   185→    for fid in finding_ids:
   186→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   187→        if entry is not None:
   188→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   189→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   190→            if fid not in order:
   191→                original_pos = entry.get("original_position")
   192→                if original_pos is not None:
   193→                    to_restore_pos.append((fid, original_pos))
   194→                else:
   195→                    to_restore_end.append(fid)
   196→            count += 1
   197→    # Insert position-aware items sorted by position; +i compensates for
   198→    # positions being recorded after earlier items were already removed.
   199→    to_restore_pos.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
   200→    for i, (fid, pos) in enumerate(to_restore_pos):
   201→        order.insert(min(pos + i, len(order)), fid)
   202→    order.extend(to_restore_end)
   203→    return count, need_reopen
   204→
   205→
   206→def resurface_stale_skips(
   207→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   208→) -> list[str]:
   209→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   210→
   211→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   212→    """
   213→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   214→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   215→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   216→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   217→    for fid in list(skipped):
   218→        entry = skipped[fid]
   219→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   220→            continue
   221→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   222→        if review_after is None:
   223→            continue
   224→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   225→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   226→            skipped.pop(fid)
   227→            if fid not in order:
   228→                original_pos = entry.get("original_position")
   229→                if original_pos is not None:
   230→                    order.insert(min(original_pos, len(order)), fid)
   231→                else:
   232→                    order.append(fid)
   233→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   234→    return resurfaced
   235→
   236→
   237→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   238→# Describe / annotate
   239→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   240→
   241→def describe_finding(
   242→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   243→) -> None:
   244→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   245→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   246→    now = utc_now()
   247→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   248→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   249→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   250→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   251→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   252→
   253→
   254→def annotate_finding(
   255→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   256→) -> None:
   257→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   258→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   259→    now = utc_now()
   260→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   261→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   262→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   263→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   264→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   265→
   266→
   267→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   268→# Clusters
   269→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   270→
   271→def create_cluster(
   272→    plan: PlanModel,
   273→    name: str,
   274→    description: str | None = None,
   275→    action: str | None = None,
   276→) -> Cluster:
   277→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   278→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   279→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   280→        raise ValueError(
   281→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   282→        )
   283→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   284→        raise ValueError(
   285→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for synthesis: {name!r}"
   286→        )
   287→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   288→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   289→    now = utc_now()
   290→    cluster: Cluster = {
   291→        "name": name,
   292→        "description": description,
   293→        "finding_ids": [],
   294→        "created_at": now,
   295→        "updated_at": now,
   296→        "auto": False,
   297→        "cluster_key": "",
   298→        "action": action,
   299→        "user_modified": False,
   300→    }
   301→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   302→    return cluster
   303→
   304→
   305→def update_cluster(
   306→    plan: PlanModel,
   307→    name: str,
   308→    *,
   309→    description: str | None = None,
   310→    action: str | None = None,
   311→    action_steps: list[str] | None = None,
   312→) -> None:
   313→    """Update fields on an existing cluster. Raises ValueError if not found."""
   314→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   315→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(name)
   316→    if cluster is None:
   317→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   318→
   319→    now = utc_now()
   320→    if description is not None:
   321→        cluster["description"] = description
   322→    if action is not None:
   323→        cluster["action"] = action
   324→    if action_steps is not None:
   325→        cluster["action_steps"] = action_steps
   326→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   327→
   328→
   329→def add_to_cluster(
   330→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   331→) -> int:
   332→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   333→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   334→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   335→    if cluster is None:
   336→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   337→
   338→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   339→    count = 0
   340→    now = utc_now()
   341→    for fid in finding_ids:
   342→        if fid not in member_ids:
   343→            member_ids.append(fid)
   344→            count += 1
   345→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   346→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   347→        if fid not in overrides:
   348→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   349→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   350→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   351→
   352→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   353→    return count
   354→
   355→
   356→def remove_from_cluster(
   357→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   358→) -> int:
   359→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   360→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   361→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   362→    if cluster is None:
   363→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   364→
   365→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   366→    now = utc_now()
   367→    count = 0
   368→    for fid in finding_ids:
   369→        if fid in member_ids:
   370→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   371→            count += 1
   372→        # Clear cluster from override
   373→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   374→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   375→            override["cluster"] = None
   376→            override["updated_at"] = now
   377→
   378→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   379→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   380→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   381→
   382→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   383→    return count
   384→
   385→
   386→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   387→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   388→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   389→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   390→    if cluster is None:
   391→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   392→
   393→    # Track deleted auto-cluster keys so auto_cluster_findings() won't regenerate them
   394→    if cluster.get("auto"):
   395→        cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   396→        if cluster_key:
   397→            deleted_keys: list[str] = plan["deleted_clusters"]
   398→            if cluster_key not in deleted_keys:
   399→                deleted_keys.append(cluster_key)
   400→
   401→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   402→    now = utc_now()
   403→    for fid in orphaned:
   404→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   405→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   406→            override["cluster"] = None
   407→            override["updated_at"] = now
   408→
   409→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   410→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   411→
   412→    return orphaned
   413→
   414→
   415→def move_cluster(
   416→    plan: PlanModel,
   417→    cluster_name: str,
   418→    position: str,
   419→    target: str | None = None,
   420→    offset: int | None = None,
   421→) -> int:
   422→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   423→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   424→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   425→    if cluster is None:
   426→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   427→
   428→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   429→    if not member_ids:
   430→        return 0
   431→
   432→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   433→
   434→
   435→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   436→# Focus
   437→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   438→
   439→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   440→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   441→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   442→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   443→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   444→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   445→
   446→
   447→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   448→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   449→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   450→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   451→
   452→
   453→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   454→# Reset
   455→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   456→
   457→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   458→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   459→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   460→    plan.clear()
   461→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   462→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   463→        plan[k] = v
   464→    plan["created"] = created
   465→
   466→
   467→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   468→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   469→
   470→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   471→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   472→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   473→    """
   474→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   475→    found = 0
   476→
   477→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   478→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   479→    for fid in finding_ids:
   480→        was_present = False
   481→        if fid in order:
   482→            order.remove(fid)
   483→            was_present = True
   484→        if fid in skipped:
   485→            skipped.pop(fid)
   486→            was_present = True
   487→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   488→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   489→            if fid in ids:
   490→                ids.remove(fid)
   491→                was_present = True
   492→        if was_present:
   493→            found += 1
   494→
   495→    return found
   496→
   497→
   498→__all__ = [
   499→    "add_to_cluster",
   500→    "annotate_finding",
   501→    "clear_focus",
   502→    "create_cluster",
   503→    "delete_cluster",
   504→    "describe_finding",
   505→    "move_cluster",
   506→    "move_items",
   507→    "purge_ids",
   508→    "remove_from_cluster",
   509→    "reset_plan",
   510→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   511→    "set_focus",
   512→    "skip_items",
   513→    "unskip_items",
   514→]
   515→
```

```
   113→                hits = [fid for fid in all_resolved if fid in epic_fids]
```

```
    89→
    90→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: dict) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return a warning banner if synthesis::pending is blocking, else None."""
    92→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    93→        return None
    94→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    95→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    96→    progress = []
    97→    for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize"):
    98→        if s in stages:
    99→            progress.append(f"{s} \u2713")
   100→        else:
   101→            progress.append(f"{s} \u25cb")
   102→    arrow = " \u2192 "
   103→    stage_line = f"  Stages: {arrow.join(progress)} \u2192 commit"
   104→
   105→    # Show what to do next based on current stage
   106→    if "observe" not in stages:
   107→        next_hint = '  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see findings and begin.'
   108→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   109→        next_hint = (
   110→            '  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see completed work and recurring patterns.\n'
   111→            '  Then: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."`'
   112→        )
   113→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   114→        next_hint = (
   115→            "  Create clusters, enrich each with action steps, then record organize.\n"
   116→            '  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see progress.'
   117→        )
   118→    else:
   119→        next_hint = '  Complete with: `desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."`'
   120→
   121→    return (
   122→        "PLANNING PHASE \u2014 synthesis::pending is blocking the queue.\n"
   123→        "  Goal: resolve contradictions, then cluster by root cause with action steps.\n"
   124→        f"{stage_line}\n"
   125→        f"{next_hint}\n"
   126→        "  Do NOT fix code or resolve findings until planning is done.\n"
   127→        "  (Exception: use `plan skip` during organize to defer contradictory findings.)"
   128→    )
   129→
   130→
   131→__all__ = [
```
