```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_policy import build_plan_queue
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, synthesis_phase_banner
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    40→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    41→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    42→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    46→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    47→        plan_data = plan
    48→
    49→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster
    50→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    51→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    52→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    53→        if active_cluster:
    54→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    55→
    56→    queue = build_plan_queue(
    57→        state,
    58→        tier=tier_filter,
    59→        count=None,
    60→        status="open",
    61→        plan=plan_data,
    62→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
    63→        cluster=effective_cluster,
    64→        collapse_clusters=True,
    65→    )
    66→    items = queue.get("items", [])
    67→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    68→
    69→    # Header
    70→    total = len(items)
    71→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    72→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    73→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    74→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    75→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    76→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    77→    else:
    78→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    79→
    80→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    81→    if focus:
    82→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    83→
    84→    # Synthesis phase guardrail
    85→    _banner = synthesis_phase_banner(plan)
    86→    if _banner:
    87→        print(colorize(f"\n  {_banner}", "yellow"))
    88→
    89→    if not include_skipped and skipped_count == 0:
    90→        # Check plan for skipped items not in queue
    91→        plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    92→        if plan_skipped_count:
    93→            print(colorize(
    94→                f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    95→                " hidden \u2014 use --include-skipped)",
    96→                "dim",
    97→            ))
    98→
    99→    if not items:
   100→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    # Determine which items to show
   104→    display_items = items
   105→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   106→        display_items = items[:top]
   107→
   108→    # Build rows
   109→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   110→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   111→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   112→        pos = str(idx)
   113→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   114→
   115→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   116→            tier_str = "T1"
   117→            detector = "synthesis"
   118→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   119→            n = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   120→            summary = f"[synthesis] {n} findings need integration"
   121→            cluster_name = ""
   122→        elif kind == "epic":
   123→            tier_str = "T1"
   124→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   125→            direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   126→            fcount = len(detail.get("finding_ids", []))
   127→            status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   128→            detector = "epic"
   129→            summary = f"[{direction}] [{fcount}] [{status}] {detail.get('thesis', '')}"
   130→            cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   131→        elif kind == "cluster":
   132→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
   133→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   134→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   135→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   136→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   137→        else:
   138→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   139→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   140→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   141→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   142→            # Extract 8-char hash suffix so users can reference items by short ID
   143→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   144→            if "::" in item_id:
   145→                hash_suffix = item_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8]
   146→                # Truncate summary first, then append hash so it's never cut off
   147→                summary = f"{_truncate(summary, 36)} [{hash_suffix}]"
   148→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   149→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   150→
   151→        suffix = ""
   152→        if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   153→            suffix = " [skip]"
   154→
   155→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   156→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   157→
   158→    print()
   159→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 44, 24]
   160→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   161→
   162→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   163→        remaining = len(items) - top
   164→        print(colorize(
   165→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   166→        ))
   167→    print()
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   171→
```

```
1→"""Shared queue-construction policy for next/plan command surfaces."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.enums import Status, canonical_status_filter
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     9→
    10→
    11→def build_plan_queue(
    12→    state: dict[str, Any],
    13→    *,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int | None = None,
    16→    scope: str | None = None,
    17→    status: str = Status.OPEN.value,
    18→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
    19→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False,
    20→    explain: bool = False,
    21→    plan: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    22→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    23→    cluster: str | None = None,
    24→    collapse_clusters: bool = False,
    25→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    26→    """Build one queue with consistent policy defaults used by CLI surfaces."""
    27→    normalized_status = canonical_status_filter(status, default=Status.OPEN.value)
    28→    return build_work_queue(
    29→        state,
    30→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    31→            tier=tier,
    32→            count=count,
    33→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    34→            scope=scope,
    35→            status=normalized_status,
    36→            include_subjective=True,
    37→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
    38→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
    39→            explain=explain,
    40→            plan=plan,
    41→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    42→            cluster=cluster,
    43→            collapse_clusters=collapse_clusters,
    44→        ),
    45→    )
    46→
    47→
    48→__all__ = ["build_plan_queue"]
    49→
```

```
1→"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    10→    format_strict_target_progress,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_llm import _is_agent_environment
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    19→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    20→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    21→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    22→        return 0
    23→
    24→    streak = 0
    25→    for entry in reversed(history):
    26→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→            break
    28→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    29→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    30→            break
    31→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    32→            break
    33→        streak += 1
    34→    return streak
    35→
    36→
    37→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    38→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    39→    diff_parts = []
    40→    if diff["new"]:
    41→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    42→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    43→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    44→    if diff["reopened"]:
    45→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    46→    if diff_parts:
    47→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    48→    else:
    49→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    50→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    51→        print(
    52→            colorize(
    53→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    54→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    55→                "yellow",
    56→            )
    57→        )
    58→
    59→
    60→def show_score_delta(
    61→    state: dict,
    62→    prev_overall: float | None,
    63→    prev_objective: float | None,
    64→    prev_strict: float | None,
    65→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
    66→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
    67→    *,
    68→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    69→):
    70→    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
    71→    stats = state["stats"]
    72→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    73→
    74→    if (
    75→        new.overall is None
    76→        or new.objective is None
    77→        or new.strict is None
    78→        or new.verified is None
    79→    ):
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
    83→                "yellow",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
    89→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
    90→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
    91→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
    92→    print(
    93→        "  Scores: "
    94→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
    95→        + colorize(
    96→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
    97→            objective_color,
    98→        )
    99→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
   100→        + colorize(
   101→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   102→            verified_color,
   103→        )
   104→    )
   105→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   106→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   107→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   108→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   109→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   110→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   111→        print(
   112→            colorize(
   113→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   114→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   115→                "yellow",
   116→            )
   117→        )
   118→
   119→    # Score legend — shown on first scan or when strict gap is significant
   120→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   121→    if scan_count <= 1 or gap > 10 or _is_agent_environment():
   122→        print(colorize("  Score guide:", "dim"))
   123→        print(colorize("    overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim"))
   124→        print(colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim"))
   125→        print(colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim"))
   126→        print(colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim"))
   127→
   128→    # Show strict target progress
   129→    if target_strict is not None and new.strict is not None:
   130→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import (
   131→            print_strict_target_nudge,
   132→        )
   133→
   134→        print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
   135→
   136→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   137→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   138→        status = integrity.get("status")
   139→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   140→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   141→        if status == "penalized":
   142→            print(
   143→                colorize(
   144→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   145→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   146→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   147→                    "red",
   148→                )
   149→            )
   150→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   151→            if streak >= 2:
   152→                print(
   153→                    colorize(
   154→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   155→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   156→                        "yellow",
   157→                    )
   158→                )
   159→        elif status == "warn":
   160→            print(
   161→                colorize(
   162→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   163→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   164→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   165→                    "yellow",
   166→                )
   167→            )
   168→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   169→            if streak >= 2:
   170→                print(
   171→                    colorize(
   172→                        "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   173→                        "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   174→                        "or run a blind reviewer pass before import.",
   175→                        "yellow",
   176→                )
   177→            )
   178→
   179→
   180→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   181→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   182→    try:
   183→        from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   184→
   185→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   186→        if concerns:
   187→            print(
   188→                colorize(
   189→                    f"  {len(concerns)} potential design concern{'s' if len(concerns) != 1 else ''}"
   190→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   191→                    "cyan",
   192→                )
   193→            )
   194→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   195→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   196→
   197→
   198→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   199→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   200→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   201→    for message, style in lines:
   202→        print(colorize(message, style))
   203→    return target, gap
   204→
```

```
    29→def _detect(fixer: FixerConfig, path: Path) -> list[dict]:

    62→        print(f"  {rel(r['file'])}{extra}  →  {syms}")

    67→def _apply_and_report(
```

```
1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import Status, finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    10→
    11→
    12→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    13→    verb = "Reopened" if status == Status.OPEN.value else "Resolved"
    14→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    15→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    16→        print(f"  {fid}")
    17→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    18→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    19→
    20→
    21→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    22→    state: dict,
    23→    *,
    24→    status: str,
    25→    resolved_count: int,
    26→) -> None:
    27→    if status != Status.WONTFIX.value or resolved_count <= 10:
    28→        return
    29→    wontfix_count = sum(
    30→        1
    31→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    32→        if finding.get("status") == Status.WONTFIX.value
    33→    )
    34→    actionable_statuses = finding_status_tokens()
    35→    actionable = sum(
    36→        1
    37→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    38→        if finding.get("status") in actionable_statuses
    39→    )
    40→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    41→    print(
    42→        colorize(
    43→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    44→            "yellow",
    45→        )
    46→    )
    47→    print(
    48→        colorize(
    49→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    50→            "dim",
    51→        )
    52→    )
    53→
    54→
    55→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    56→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    57→        return ""
    58→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_score_movement(
    62→    *,
    63→    status: str,
    64→    prev_overall: float | None,
    65→    prev_objective: float | None,
    66→    prev_strict: float | None,
    67→    prev_verified: float | None,
    68→    state: dict,
    69→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    70→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    71→) -> None:
    72→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    73→    if (
    74→        new.overall is None
    75→        or new.objective is None
    76→        or new.strict is None
    77→        or new.verified is None
    78→    ):
    79→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    83→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    84→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    85→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    86→    print(
    87→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    88→        + colorize(
    89→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    90→            "dim",
    91→        )
    92→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    93→        + colorize(
    94→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    95→        )
    96→    )
    97→    if target_strict is not None:
    98→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import (
    99→            print_strict_target_nudge,
   100→        )
   101→
   102→        print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
   103→    if status == Status.WONTFIX.value:
   104→        strict_gap = round(new.overall - new.strict, 1)
   105→        if strict_gap > 0:
   106→            print(
   107→                colorize(
   108→                    f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
   109→                    f"Current gap: overall {new.overall:.1f} vs strict {new.strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
   110→                    "yellow",
   111→                )
   112→            )
   113→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
   114→        print(
   115→            colorize(
   116→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
   117→                "yellow",
   118→            )
   119→        )
   120→        print(
   121→            colorize(
   122→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
   123→                "dim",
   124→            )
   125→        )
   126→    elif status == Status.FIXED.value:
   127→        print(
   128→            colorize(
   129→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
   130→                "yellow",
   131→            )
   132→        )
   133→
   134→
   135→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   136→    *,
   137→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   138→    state: dict,
   139→    all_resolved: list[str],
   140→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   141→) -> None:
   142→    has_review = any(
   143→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   144→        for fid in all_resolved
   145→    )
   146→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    stale_dims = sorted(
   150→        dim
   151→        for dim in {
   152→            str(
   153→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   154→            ).strip()
   155→            for fid in all_resolved
   156→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   157→        }
   158→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   159→    )
   160→    if not stale_dims:
   161→        return
   162→
   163→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   164→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   165→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   166→    print(
   167→        colorize(
   168→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   169→            "yellow",
   170→        )
   171→    )
   172→    print(
   173→        colorize(
   174→            "  Next subjective step: "
   175→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   176→            "dim",
   177→        )
   178→    )
   179→
   180→
   181→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   182→    remaining = sum(
   183→        1
   184→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   185→        if finding.get("status") == Status.OPEN.value
   186→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   187→    )
   188→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   189→    if remaining > 0:
   190→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   191→        print(
   192→            colorize(
   193→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   194→                "dim",
   195→            )
   196→        )
   197→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   198→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   199→    print()
   200→    return next_command
   201→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→COMPLETED_CLUSTER_TTL_DAYS = 180
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class ReconcileResult:
    19→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    20→
    21→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    24→    changes: int = 0
    25→
    26→
    27→def _find_candidates(
    28→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    29→) -> list[str]:
    30→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    31→    candidates: list[str] = []
    32→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    33→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    34→            continue
    35→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    36→            candidates.append(fid)
    37→    return candidates
    38→
    39→
    40→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    42→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    43→    if finding is None:
    44→        return False
    45→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    46→
    47→
    48→def _supersede_id(
    49→    plan: PlanModel,
    50→    state: StateModel,
    51→    finding_id: str,
    52→    now: str,
    53→) -> bool:
    54→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    55→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    56→    detector = ""
    57→    file = ""
    58→    summary = ""
    59→    if finding:
    60→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    61→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    62→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    63→
    64→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    65→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    66→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    67→
    68→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    69→        "original_id": finding_id,
    70→        "original_detector": detector,
    71→        "original_file": file,
    72→        "original_summary": summary,
    73→        "status": "superseded",
    74→        "superseded_at": now,
    75→        "remapped_to": None,
    76→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    77→    }
    78→
    79→    # Preserve any existing override note
    80→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    81→    if override and override.get("note"):
    82→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    83→
    84→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    85→
    86→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    87→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    88→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    89→    if finding_id in order:
    90→        order.remove(finding_id)
    91→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    92→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    93→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    94→        if finding_id in ids:
    95→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    96→
    97→    return True
    98→
    99→
   100→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   101→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   102→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   103→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   104→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   105→
   106→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   107→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   108→        try:
   109→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   110→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   111→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   112→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   113→                to_prune.append(fid)
   114→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   115→            to_prune.append(fid)
   116→
   117→    for fid in to_prune:
   118→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   119→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   120→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   121→
   122→    return to_prune
   123→
   124→
   125→def _archive_cluster(plan: PlanModel, cluster: dict, now: str) -> None:
   126→    """Append a synthesis-cluster to the completed_clusters archive."""
   127→    archive: list[dict] = plan.setdefault("completed_clusters", [])
   128→    archive.append({
   129→        "name": cluster.get("name", ""),
   130→        "thesis": cluster.get("thesis"),
   131→        "description": cluster.get("description"),
   132→        "action_steps": cluster.get("action_steps", []),
   133→        "finding_ids": list(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])),
   134→        "direction": cluster.get("direction"),
   135→        "root_cause": cluster.get("root_cause"),
   136→        "completed_at": now,
   137→        "synthesis_version": cluster.get("synthesis_version", 0),
   138→    })
   139→
   140→
   141→def _prune_old_completed_clusters(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> int:
   142→    """Remove completed clusters older than TTL. Returns count pruned."""
   143→    archive: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   144→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=COMPLETED_CLUSTER_TTL_DAYS)
   145→    original = len(archive)
   146→    kept: list[dict] = []
   147→    for entry in archive:
   148→        ts = entry.get("completed_at", "")
   149→        try:
   150→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   151→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   152→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   153→            if entry_dt >= cutoff:
   154→                kept.append(entry)
   155→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   156→            pass  # Drop malformed entries
   157→    plan["completed_clusters"] = kept
   158→    return original - len(kept)
   159→
   160→
   161→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   162→    plan: PlanModel,
   163→    state: StateModel,
   164→) -> ReconcileResult:
   165→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   166→
   167→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   168→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   169→    """
   170→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   171→    result = ReconcileResult()
   172→    now = utc_now()
   173→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   174→
   175→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   176→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   177→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   178→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   179→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   180→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   181→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   182→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   183→        # Include dismissed lists from synthesis-clusters
   184→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("dismissed", []))
   185→
   186→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   187→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   188→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   189→    referenced_ids.discard(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   190→    referenced_ids = {
   191→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   192→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   193→    }
   194→
   195→    # Check each referenced ID
   196→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   197→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   198→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   199→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   200→                result.changes += 1
   201→
   202→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   203→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   204→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   205→
   206→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   207→    if resurfaced:
   208→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   209→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   210→
   211→    # Prune old superseded entries
   212→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   213→    result.pruned = pruned
   214→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   215→
   216→    # Clean up synthesis-clusters: remove dead findings, mark completed, archive empty
   217→    dead_ids = {fid for fid in result.superseded}
   218→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   219→    for cname in list(clusters.keys()):
   220→        cluster = clusters[cname]
   221→        if not cluster.get("thesis"):
   222→            continue  # Only clean up synthesis-clusters
   223→        fids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   224→        alive = [fid for fid in fids if fid not in dead_ids]
   225→        if len(alive) != len(fids):
   226→            cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   227→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   228→            result.changes += 1
   229→        # Mark completed if all findings resolved
   230→        if not alive and cluster.get("status") != "completed":
   231→            cluster["status"] = "completed"
   232→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   233→        # Archive and delete empty synthesis-clusters
   234→        if not alive:
   235→            _archive_cluster(plan, cluster, now)
   236→            del clusters[cname]
   237→
   238→    # Prune old completed cluster archives
   239→    pruned_completed = _prune_old_completed_clusters(plan, now_dt)
   240→    result.changes += pruned_completed
   241→
   242→    return result
   243→
   244→
   245→__all__ = [
   246→    "ReconcileResult",
   247→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   248→]
   249→
```

```
    32→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    33→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    37→    state: dict,
    38→    dim_scores: dict,
    39→    *,
    40→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    41→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    42→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    43→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    44→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    45→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    46→            continue
    47→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    48→        if strict_val < threshold:
    49→            low.append(
    50→                (
    51→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    52→                    strict_val,
    53→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    54→                )
    55→            )
    56→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    57→    return low
    58→
    59→
    60→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    61→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    62→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    63→    state = runtime.state
    64→    config = runtime.config
    65→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    66→        return
    67→
    68→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    69→    if stale_warning:
    70→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    71→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    72→    if skill_warning:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    74→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    75→    if config_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→
    78→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    82→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    83→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    84→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    85→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    86→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    87→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    88→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    89→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    90→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    91→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    92→
    93→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    94→
    95→    # Load the living plan
    96→    plan = load_plan()
    97→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    98→    if (
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    ):
   103→        plan_data = plan
   104→
   105→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   106→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   107→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   108→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   109→        if active_cluster:
   110→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   111→
   112→    queue = build_plan_queue(
   113→        state,
   114→        tier=tier,
   115→        count=count,
   116→        scope=scope,
   117→        status=status,
   118→        target_strict=target_strict,
   119→        no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   120→        explain=explain,
   121→        plan=plan_data,
   122→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   123→        cluster=effective_cluster,
   124→    )
   125→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   126→
   127→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   128→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   129→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   130→        state,
   131→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   132→    )
   133→
   134→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   135→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   136→    )
   137→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   138→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   139→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   140→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   141→        state,
   142→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   143→    )
   144→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   145→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   146→    ]
   147→    write_query(payload)
   148→
   149→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   150→    if output_file:
   151→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   152→            output_file,
   153→            payload,
   154→            len(items),
   155→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   156→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   157→        ):
   158→            return
   159→        raise SystemExit(1)
   160→
   161→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   162→        return
   163→
   164→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   165→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   166→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   167→        return
   168→
   169→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   170→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   171→        state.get("findings", {}),
   172→        state.get("scan_path"),
   173→    )
   174→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   175→    try:
   176→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   177→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   178→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   179→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   180→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   181→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   182→    )
   183→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   184→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   185→        state,
   186→        dim_scores,
   187→        findings_scoped,
   188→        strict_score=strict_score,
   189→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   190→    )
   191→    print()
   192→
   193→
   194→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   195→
```

```
    77→    if skill_warning:

    83→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
```

```
    19→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    20→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    21→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    22→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    23→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    24→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    25→            continue
    26→        try:
    27→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    28→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    29→            continue
    30→
    31→
    32→def _import_mode_from_args(args: argparse.Namespace) -> str:
    33→    """Derive canonical import mode while preserving legacy-flag validation."""
    34→    explicit = str(getattr(args, "import_mode", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
    35→    if explicit != "auto":
    36→        return explicit
    37→    manual = bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False))
    38→    attested = bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False))
    39→    if manual and not attested:
    40→        return "manual_override"
    41→    if attested and not manual:
    42→        return "attested_external"
    43→    return "auto"
    44→
    45→
    46→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    47→    """Prepare or import subjective code review findings."""
    48→    _enable_live_review_output()
    49→    runtime = command_runtime(args)

    57→            file=sys.stderr,
    58→        )
    59→        sys.exit(1)
    60→
    61→    merge = bool(getattr(args, "merge", False))
    62→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))
    63→    external_start = bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False))

    69→    mode_flags = [

    73→        external_submit,
    74→        import_mode,
    75→        bool(validate_import_file),

    77→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
```

```
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
```

```
1→"""Shared strict-target progress rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def format_strict_target_progress(
     7→    strict_target: dict | None,
     8→) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, str]], float | None, float | None]:
     9→    """Return display lines and numeric values for strict target progress."""
    10→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    11→    if not isinstance(strict_target, dict):
    12→        return lines, None, None
    13→
    14→    warning = strict_target.get("warning")
    15→    if isinstance(warning, str) and warning.strip():
    16→        lines.append((f"  * {warning.strip()}", "yellow"))
    17→
    18→    target = strict_target.get("target")
    19→    current = strict_target.get("current")
    20→    gap = strict_target.get("gap")
    21→    state = strict_target.get("state")
    22→    if not isinstance(target, int | float):
    23→        return lines, None, None
    24→    target_f = float(target)
    25→
    26→    if not isinstance(current, int | float):
    27→        lines.append(
    28→            (
    29→                f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · current strict score unavailable",
    30→                "dim",
    31→            )
    32→        )
    33→        return lines, target_f, None
    34→
    35→    current_f = float(current)
    36→    gap_f = (
    37→        float(gap) if isinstance(gap, int | float) else round(target_f - current_f, 1)
    38→    )
    39→    if state == "below":
    40→        lines.append((
    41→            f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · currently {current_f:.1f}/100 ({gap_f:.1f} below target)",
    42→            "yellow",
    43→        ))
    44→    elif state == "above":
    45→        lines.append((
    46→            f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · currently {current_f:.1f}/100 ({abs(gap_f):.1f} above target)",
    47→            "green",
    48→        ))
    49→    else:
    50→        lines.append((f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · on target", "green"))
    51→
    52→    return lines, target_f, gap_f
    53→
```

```
1→"""Summary-line rendering helpers for the status command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD, colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def score_summary_lines(
    10→    *,
    11→    overall_score: float | None,
    12→    objective_score: float | None,
    13→    strict_score: float | None,
    14→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    15→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    16→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    17→    """Return formatted top-line score summary rows."""
    18→    if (
    19→        overall_score is not None
    20→        and objective_score is not None
    21→        and strict_score is not None
    22→        and verified_strict_score is not None
    23→    ):
    24→        lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
    25→            (
    26→                f"\n  Scores: overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 · "
    27→                f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 · "
    28→                f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 · "
    29→                f"verified {verified_strict_score:.1f}/100",
    30→                "bold",
    31→            ),
    32→            (
    33→                "  Score guide: overall (lenient) · objective (mechanical only) · "
    34→                "strict (penalizes wontfix) · verified (scan-confirmed only)",
    35→                "dim",
    36→            ),
    37→        ]
    38→        if target_strict is not None:
    39→            gap = round(target_strict - strict_score, 1)
    40→            if gap > 0:
    41→                lines.append((
    42→                    f"  Strict {strict_score:.1f} (target: {target_strict:.1f})"
    43→                    " — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement",
    44→                    "dim",
    45→                ))
    46→            else:
    47→                lines.append((
    48→                    f"  Strict {strict_score:.1f} — target {target_strict:.1f} reached!",
    49→                    "green",
    50→                ))
    51→        else:
    52→            lines.append(("  Focus on strict — it's your north star.", "dim"))
    53→        return lines
    54→    return [
    55→        ("\n  Scores unavailable", "bold"),
    56→        ("  Run a full scan to compute overall/objective/strict scores.", "yellow"),
    57→    ]
    58→
    59→
    60→def print_scan_metrics(state: dict) -> None:
    61→    """Print aggregate codebase metrics from the last scan."""
    62→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    63→    total_files = sum(m.get("total_files", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    64→    total_loc = sum(m.get("total_loc", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    65→    total_dirs = sum(m.get("total_directories", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    66→    if total_files:
    67→        loc_str = f"{total_loc:,}" if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    68→        print(
    69→            colorize(
    70→                f"  {total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} dirs · "
    71→                f"Last scan: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    72→                "dim",
    73→            )
    74→        )
    75→        return
    76→    print(
    77→        colorize(
    78→            f"  Scans: {state.get('scan_count', 0)} | Last: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    79→            "dim",
    80→        )
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def print_scan_completeness(state: dict) -> None:
    85→    """Warn when one or more language scans were partial."""
    86→    completeness = state.get("scan_completeness", {})
    87→    incomplete = [lang for lang, status in completeness.items() if status != "full"]
    88→    if incomplete:
    89→        print(
    90→            colorize(
    91→                f"  * Incomplete scan ({', '.join(incomplete)} — slow phases skipped)",
    92→                "yellow",
    93→            )
    94→        )
    95→
    96→
    97→def print_open_scope_breakdown(state: dict) -> None:
    98→    """Print open counts with explicit in-scope/out-of-scope semantics."""
    99→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   100→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    counts = state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   104→    print(
   105→        colorize(
   106→            "  "
   107→            f"open (in-scope): {counts['in_scope']} · "
   108→            f"open (out-of-scope carried): {counts['out_of_scope']} · "
   109→            f"open (global): {counts['global']}",
   110→            "dim",
   111→        )
   112→    )
   113→
   114→
   115→__all__ = [
   116→    "print_open_scope_breakdown",
   117→    "print_scan_completeness",
   118→    "print_scan_metrics",
   119→    "score_summary_lines",
   120→]
   121→
```

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive report of the queue lifecycle:

## Queue Lifecycle Analysis - Key Findings

Based on my thorough examination of the code, here's how desloppify tracks queue state and lifecycle:

### 1. **Queue Storage & Structure**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`

The plan data structure (PlanModel) tracks the queue via:
- **`queue_order`** (list[str]): Ordered list of finding IDs to work on
- **`skipped`** (dict[str, SkipEntry]): Items temporarily/permanently deferred with metadata (kind: "temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out")
- **`deferred`** (list[str]): Legacy field (migrated to skipped in v2)
- **`clusters`** (dict[str, Cluster]): Grouped findings with synthesis-clusters marked with `thesis` field
- **`completed_clusters`** (list[CompletedCluster]): Archive of finished synthesis-clusters
- **`superseded`** (dict[str, SupersededEntry]): Finding IDs no longer alive (removed, resolved, etc.)

### 2. **Queue "Empty" Detection**

**Files:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py:371` → `show_empty_queue()`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py:99-101` → checks `if not items`

**Current behavior:**
```python
# When queue has no items:
print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))  # Line 100 in queue_render.py

# In next command:
print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))  # Line 375 in render.py
```

**Test coverage:** `test_allowed_when_queue_empty()` in `test_review_preflight.py` and `test_empty_state_returns_empty_queue()` in `test_work_queue.py`

### 3. **Queue Lifecycle States**

**No explicit "started" vs "in progress" vs "completed" states exist**, but there is implicit progression:

1. **Queue started:** When a plan is first created with `empty_plan()` and items added via `queue_order`
2. **Queue in progress:** Items are processed via:
   - `move_items()` - reorder queue_order
   - `skip_items()` - move to skipped (can be temporary with `review_after`)
   - `unskip_items()` - restore from skipped back to queue_order
3. **Queue completed:** When `queue_order` is empty AND `skipped` has no temporary items (only permanent/false_positive)

### 4. **When Items Shrink from Queue**

**Files:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py`

Items are removed via:
- **`skip_items()`** (line 130-166): Move to skipped dict with kind metadata
- **`unskip_items()`** (line 169-203): Restore from skipped back to queue_order
- **`purge_ids()`** (line 467-495): Remove entirely from `queue_order`, `skipped`, and cluster memberships (used after findings resolve)
- **`reconcile_plan_after_scan()`**: Post-scan, findings no longer in state are moved to `superseded` and removed from `queue_order`

### 5. **Completion Events**

**No explicit "celebration" or completion event exists**, but there are two completion mechanisms:

#### A. **Synthesis-cluster completion** (`reconcile.py:229-236`)
```python
# When all findings in a synthesis-cluster are resolved:
if not alive and cluster.get("status") != "completed":
    cluster["status"] = "completed"
    _archive_cluster(plan, cluster, now)
    del clusters[cname]  # Remove from active clusters
```
- Clusters are archived to `completed_clusters` with a timestamp
- Archives are kept for 180 days (COMPLETED_CLUSTER_TTL_DAYS)

#### B. **Queue empty detection** (`render.py:375` and `queue_render.py:100`)
- Shows green "Nothing to do!" or "Queue is empty."
- **NO special celebration or milestone tracking**
- Just returns silently if no items

### 6. **Progress Tracking Through Queue**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:59-182` → `_apply_plan_order()`

Progress is tracked implicitly via:
- **`queue_position`** (assigned line 160): Position in current queue (1-indexed)
- **Plan metadata**: `plan_description`, `plan_note`, `plan_cluster` are annotated on items
- **Tier counts**: `tier_counts` dict shows remaining items per tier (T1, T2, T3, T4)
- **Skipped count**: Items marked with `plan_skipped: True` indicate deferred work

### 7. **Key Insights**

1. **No "Queue started" event**: Plan creation doesn't record when work began
2. **No "Queue in progress" tracking**: No timestamp or metadata indicates work is active
3. **No "Queue completed" celebration**: When queue empties, just prints "Nothing to do!" in green
4. **Synthesis-clusters have completion**: Only synthesis-created clusters (with `thesis`) are explicitly marked as "completed" and archived
5. **Regular findings have no completion tracking**: Auto-detected findings are just purged when reconciled; no archive trail

### 8. **Potential Enhancement Opportunities**

The code is structured to support:
- Adding a `queue_status` field to track "pending" | "in_progress" | "completed"
- Recording `queue_started_at` and `queue_completed_at` timestamps in epic_synthesis_meta
- Celebrating milestone completions (e.g., "Tier 1 queue cleared!")
- Archiving completed regular findings (not just synthesis-clusters)
- Tracking completion rate percentage as queue shrinks

All the plumbing exists in `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` to detect completion events—it just doesn't record them for celebration or historical tracking.

## COMPREHENSIVE REPORT: Score Display and Queue Completion in Desloppify

Based on my thorough analysis of the codebase, here are ALL the places where scores are displayed to users and the queue completion logic:

### **1. SCORE DISPLAY LOCATIONS**

#### **A. Centralized Score Update Function**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/score_update.py`

- **`print_score_update()` (lines 10-46)** - Core function that prints all 4 scores with deltas
  - Shows: overall, objective, strict, verified (all with /100 scale)
  - Displays delta indicators (±)
  - Always calls `print_strict_target_nudge()` to show strict target progress
  - Called by: resolve, fix, and ignore_pattern commands

- **`print_strict_target_nudge()` (lines 59-68)** - Shows strict score gap to target
  - When gap > 0: prints "(target: X.X)" with "run `desloppify next`" nudge
  - When gap ≤ 0: prints "target X.X reached!" in green
  - Used throughout for consistent nudging

---

#### **B. Next Command - "North Star" Display**

  - Shows available tiers when tier was requested but no items found

  - **Lines 705-720:** Displays North Star section:

### **3. TARGET REACHED LOGIC**

**Pattern:** When strict score meets or exceeds target (gap ≤ 0)

- **`print_strict_target_nudge()`** (lines 59-68 in score_update.py)
  - Green message: `"Strict {strict:.1f} — target {target:.1f} reached!"`

- **`score_summary_lines()`** (lines 47-50 in summary.py)
  - Green message: `"Strict {strict:.1f} — target {target:.1f} reached!"`

- **`render_followup_nudges()`** (lines 714-720 in next_parts/render.py)
  - Green message: `"North star: strict X.X/100 meets target Y.Y"`

---

### **4. SCORE METRICS DISPLAYED IN EACH COMMAND**

| Command | Scores Shown | Context |
|---------|-------------|---------|
| **scan** | overall, objective, strict, verified | Summary after scan with deltas, always shows guide |
| **status** | overall, objective, strict, verified | Dashboard view with full context |
| **next** | strict (in North Star section) | Shows progress toward target |
| **fix** (auto-fix) | overall, objective, strict, verified | After applying fixes with deltas |
| **resolve** | overall, objective, strict, verified | After resolving findings with deltas |
| **ignore** | overall, objective, strict, verified | After adding ignore pattern with deltas |
| **show** | None in main flow | Focuses on findings, not scores |
| **plan** | None in queue display | Shows queue metadata, not scores |

---

### **5. KEY PATTERNS FOR DETECTING "ALL DONE"**

1. **`show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict)`** - Returns True when no items
2. **`if not items:`** checks in plan queue render
3. **SYNTHESIS_ID checks** - Blocks queue until synthesis complete
4. **Stale dimension injection** - Auto-adds items when queue empties
5. **`total_candidates == 0`** in review flow

---

### **6. STRICT SCORE DISPLAY CONSISTENCY**

The strict score is displayed/tracked in:
- North Star section (next command) - **PRIMARY USER-FACING**
- All state-changing command summaries (fix, resolve, ignore)
- Status dashboard
- Scan report with delta calculation
- Target progress calculations in multiple places

The strict score is the "north star" metric mentioned throughout, used as the definitive measure of codebase health excluding wontfix items.

> DEVELOPER

I'm designing a UX change for desloppify where the score is hidden during the "execution phase" (working through the queue) and only revealed when the queue is fully cleared or when starting a new plan cycle.

## Current State

### Score display locations (all need to change):

1. **`next` command** (`app/commands/next_parts/render.py:705-720`): Shows "North Star: strict X.X/100 → target Y.Y (+Z.Z needed)" at bottom of every `next` output
2. **`fix` command** (`app/commands/fix/apply_flow.py:85-86`): Calls `print_score_update(state, prev)` showing all 4 scores with deltas after every fix
3. **`resolve` command** (`app/commands/resolve/render.py:61-132` and `cmd.py:242`): Shows all 4 scores with deltas after resolving findings
4. **`plan queue` command** (`app/commands/plan/queue_render.py:78-100`): Shows queue header with item counts
5. **`scan` command** (`app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_summary.py:60-134`): Shows all 4 scores after scan
6. **`status` command** (`app/commands/status_parts/summary.py:9-57`): Shows 4-score summary
7. **Empty queue** (`app/commands/next_parts/render.py:371-384`): Shows "Nothing to do!" with strict score suffix

### Score update helper:
- `print_score_update()` in `app/commands/helpers/score_update.py:10-46` - central function that prints all 4 scores with deltas, always calls `print_strict_target_nudge()`
- `print_strict_target_nudge()` in same file, lines 59-68 - shows strict vs target gap

### Queue system:
- Queue items are built by `build_work_queue()` in `engine/_work_queue/core.py`
- Queue order stored in plan's `queue_order` list
- Items removed via `purge_ids()` and `skip_items()` in `engine/_plan/operations.py`
- No explicit queue progress tracking exists currently
- Empty queue detected in `show_empty_queue()` - just prints "Nothing to do!"

## Desired Behavior

**Core principle:** The score is the REWARD for clearing the entire queue. During execution, only show queue progress.

### During execution (queue has items):
- `next`: Show queue progress ("12 items remaining") instead of North Star score. Add message: "Clear the queue to see your updated score"
- `fix`: Show queue progress ("12 → 11 items remaining") instead of 4-score update
- `resolve`: Same as fix - queue progress, not scores
- `plan queue`: Already shows item counts - keep this, maybe enhance

### Score reveal moments:
- **Queue cleared** (next shows "Nothing to do!"): This is the big moment - show the full score with celebration
- **Starting new plan** (after scan): Scan should still show scores since it's the assessment point
- **`plan` creation**: Show score prominently as the starting point

### What about `status` and `scan`?
- `scan` should keep showing scores - it's the natural re-assessment point and kicks off a new plan cycle
- `status` is trickier - maybe show a simplified version or just queue progress with a note about running scan for scores

## Design Requirements

1. Need a lightweight way to get queue item count (total remaining) that can be called from fix/resolve/next without building full work queue
2. Need a "queue progress" display function to replace `print_score_update()` in execution contexts
3. Need an enhanced "score reveal" for when queue is cleared
4. The score_update.py helper needs to become context-aware (are we mid-queue or at a milestone?)
5. Keep internal score computation unchanged - only change what's DISPLAYED

Please design a detailed implementation plan covering:
- What each file change looks like
- New helper functions needed
- How to detect "queue has items" vs "queue is clear" cheaply
- The exact messages/formatting for queue progress vs score reveal
- Edge cases (what if there's no plan? what if queue was never started?)

> AGENT

I'll start by thoroughly exploring the codebase to understand the current score display system, queue mechanics, and rendering patterns before designing the implementation plan.

```
1→"""Centralized score update display for all state-changing commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→def print_score_update(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    13→    *,
    14→    config: dict | None = None,
    15→    label: str = "Scores",
    16→) -> None:
    17→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    18→
    19→    Args:
    20→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    21→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    22→        config: Project config dict (loaded from disk if not provided).
    23→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    24→    """
    25→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    26→    if (
    27→        new.overall is None
    28→        or new.objective is None
    29→        or new.strict is None
    30→        or new.verified is None
    31→    ):
    32→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    33→        return
    34→
    35→    overall_s, overall_c = format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    36→    objective_s, objective_c = format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    37→    strict_s, strict_c = format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    38→    verified_s, verified_c = format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
    39→
    40→    print(
    41→        f"\n  {label}: "
    42→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
    43→        + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}", objective_c)
    44→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
    45→        + colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}", verified_c)
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    # Always show strict target + next-command nudge
    49→    if config is None:
    50→        from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
    51→
    52→        config = config_mod.load_config()
    53→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    54→
    55→    target = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    56→    print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target)
    57→
    58→
    59→def print_strict_target_nudge(
    60→    strict: float, target: float, *, show_next: bool = True,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Print a one-liner with strict→target and optional next-command nudge."""
    63→    gap = round(target - strict, 1)
    64→    if gap > 0:
    65→        suffix = " — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement" if show_next else ""
    66→        print(colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} (target: {target:.1f}){suffix}", "dim"))
    67→    else:
    68→        print(colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} — target {target:.1f} reached!", "green"))
    69→
    70→# Backward compatibility for older import paths.
    71→_print_strict_target_nudge = print_strict_target_nudge
    72→
    73→__all__ = ["print_score_update", "print_strict_target_nudge"]
    74→
```

```
   358→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:

   387→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
```
