```
   400→def _render_subjective_task(item: dict) -> None:
   401→    """Render a subjective dimension review as a proper task card."""
   402→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   403→    dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "unknown")
   404→    strict_score = float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
   405→    open_review = int(detail.get("open_review_findings", 0))
   406→    is_stale = "stale" in item.get("summary", "")
   407→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "desloppify review --prepare")
   408→
   409→    tag = " \u2014 stale, re-review" if is_stale else ""
   410→    print(colorize(f"  (Subjective review{tag})", "bold"))
   411→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   412→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   413→    print(f"  Dimension: {dim_name}")
   414→    print(f"  Score: {strict_score:.1f}%")
   415→    if open_review:
   416→        print(f"  Open review findings: {open_review}")
   417→    print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary}", "cyan"))
   418→    print(colorize(
   419→        "  Note: re-review scores what it finds \u2014 scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   420→        "dim",
   421→    ))
   422→
   423→
   424→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   425→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   426→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   427→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   428→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   429→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   430→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   431→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   432→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   433→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   434→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   435→    stages = detail.get("stage_progress", {})
   436→    enrichment_gaps = detail.get("enrichment_gaps", [])
   437→    enriched_count = detail.get("enriched_cluster_count", 0)
   438→
   439→    print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
   440→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   441→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   442→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
   443→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
   444→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   445→    print()
   446→    if new:
   447→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   448→    if resolved:
   449→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   450→    if epic_count:
   451→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   452→
   453→    # Stage progress
   454→    print()
   455→    print("  Stages:")
   456→    for stage_name, label in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS:
   457→        if stage_name in stages:
   458→            print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label}", "green"))
   459→        elif SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
   460→            print(colorize(f"    \u2192 {label} (current)", "yellow"))
   461→        else:
   462→            print(colorize(f"    \u25cb {label}", "dim"))
   463→
   464→    # Show enrichment status when in organize phase
   465→    if "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
   466→        if enrichment_gaps:
   467→            print(colorize(f"\n  {len(enrichment_gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   468→            for gap_name, gap_fields in enrichment_gaps:
   469→                print(colorize(f"    {gap_name}: missing {', '.join(gap_fields)}", "yellow"))
   470→        elif enriched_count > 0:
   471→            print(colorize(f"\n  All {enriched_count} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
   472→
   473→    # Next command based on current stage
   474→    print()
   475→    if "observe" not in stages:
   476→        print(f"  Next: {SYNTH_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   477→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   478→        print(f"  Next: {SYNTH_CMD_REFLECT}")
   479→        print(f"    (View completed work and recurring patterns, then record reflect stage)")
   480→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   481→        if enrichment_gaps:
   482→            print("  Enrich clusters before proceeding:")
   483→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH}")
   484→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}   (when all clusters are enriched)")
   485→        elif enriched_count == 0:
   486→            print("  Defer contradictions, then create and enrich clusters:")
   487→            print("    desloppify plan skip <hash>   (temporarily defer contradictory findings)")
   488→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE}")
   489→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD}")
   490→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS}")
   491→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   492→        else:
   493→            print(f"  All clusters enriched! Record organize:")
   494→            print(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   495→        if detail.get("has_prior_synthesis"):
   496→            print(f"  Skip: {SYNTH_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING}")
   497→    else:
   498→        print(f"  Next: {SYNTH_CMD_COMPLETE}")
   499→
   500→    print()
   501→    print(colorize("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow"))
   502→    print()
   503→    print(colorize("  Forbidden during this phase:", "red"))
   504→    print("    - desloppify fix / resolve / editing code")
   505→    print(colorize("  Allowed: plan skip (to defer contradictory findings during organize)", "dim"))
   506→    print()
   507→    print(colorize("  View plan:", "dim"))
   508→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan synthesize  \u2014 findings and stage progress", "dim"))
   509→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster list \u2014 current clusters", "dim"))
   510→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan queue        \u2014 items in priority order", "dim"))
   511→
   512→
   513→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   514→    """Render an epic card."""
   515→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   516→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   517→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   518→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   519→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   520→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   521→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   522→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   523→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   524→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   525→
   526→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   527→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   528→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   529→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   530→    if root_cause:
   531→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   532→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   533→    if total_epics:
   534→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   535→    print()
   536→    if action_steps:
   537→        print("  Steps:")
   538→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   539→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   540→        print()
   541→    if finding_ids:
   542→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   543→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   544→        for fid in shown:
   545→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   546→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   547→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   548→        print()
   549→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   550→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   551→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   552→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   553→
   554→
   555→def render_terminal_items(
   556→    items: list[dict],
   557→    dim_scores: dict,
   558→    findings_scoped: dict,
   559→    *,
   560→    group: str,
   561→    explain: bool,
   562→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   563→    plan: dict | None = None,
   564→) -> None:
   565→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   566→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   567→
   568→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   569→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   570→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   571→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   572→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   573→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   574→        visible = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed")
   575→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({visible} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   576→
   577→        # Exclude the synthesis item from the focus count — it's a queue-level
   578→        # blocker, not a cluster member, so don't count it in "N of M remaining".
   579→        focus_items = [it for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed"]
   580→        if len(focus_items) == 0:
   581→            print(colorize(
   582→                "  This cluster has no remaining items. "
   583→                "Run `desloppify plan focus --clear` to unfocus, "
   584→                "or `desloppify plan cluster show` to inspect.",
   585→                "yellow",
   586→            ))
   587→
   588→    # When synthesis is pending, show subjective review tasks (they must
   589→    # complete before synthesis) then the planning card — don't leak
   590→    # mechanical implementation tasks that would tempt agents to skip planning.
   591→    has_synthesis = any(it.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed" for it in items)
   592→    if has_synthesis:
   593→        # Collect the prefix: subjective items + synthesis card
   594→        prefix_items = []
   595→        for it in items:
   596→            if it.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   597→                prefix_items.append(it)
   598→                break
   599→            if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   600→                prefix_items.append(it)
   601→            else:
   602→                break  # non-subjective, non-synthesis — stop
   603→        for idx, it in enumerate(prefix_items):
   604→            if idx > 0:
   605→                print()
   606→            kind = it.get("kind", "")
   607→            queue_pos = it.get("queue_position")
   608→            if queue_pos and len(prefix_items) > 1:
   609→                label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   610→            else:
   611→                pos_str = f"  (#{queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   612→                label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   613→            print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   614→            if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   615→                _render_synthesis_item(it)
   616→            else:
   617→                _render_subjective_task(it)
   618→        return
   619→
   620→    if group != "item":
   621→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   622→        return
   623→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   624→        if idx > 0:
   625→            print()
   626→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   627→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   628→
   629→        # All item types get the same position label — no visual hierarchy
   630→        # that makes synthetic items look like metadata instead of tasks.
   631→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   632→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   633→        elif len(items) > 1:
   634→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   635→        else:
   636→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   637→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   638→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   639→
   640→        if kind == "epic":
   641→            _render_epic_item(item)
   642→            continue
   643→        if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   644→            _render_subjective_task(item)
   645→            continue
   646→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   647→
   648→
   649→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   650→    if len(items) != 1:
   651→        return
   652→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   653→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic", "subjective_dimension"):
   654→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   655→    if kind != "finding":
   656→        return
   657→    item = items[0]
   658→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   659→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   660→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   661→        primary = item.get(
   662→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   663→        )
   664→        print(f"    {primary}")
   665→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   666→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   667→        return
   668→
   669→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   670→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   671→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   672→        print(f"    {primary}")
   673→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   674→    else:
   675→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   676→
   677→    print(
   678→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   679→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   680→    )
   681→    print(
   682→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   683→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   684→    )
   685→
   686→
   687→def render_followup_nudges(
   688→    state: dict,
   689→    dim_scores: dict,
   690→    findings_scoped: dict,
   691→    *,
   692→    strict_score: float | None,
   693→    target_strict_score: float,
   694→) -> None:
   695→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   696→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   697→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   698→        state,
   699→        subjective_entries,
   700→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   701→        max_quality_items=3,
   702→        max_integrity_items=5,
   703→    )
   704→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   705→    if strict_score is not None:
   706→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   707→        if gap > 0:
   708→            print(
   709→                colorize(
   710→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   711→                    "cyan",
   712→                )
   713→            )
   714→        else:
   715→            print(
   716→                colorize(
   717→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   718→                    "green",
   719→                )
   720→            )
   721→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   722→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   723→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   724→
   725→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   726→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   727→        findings_scoped
   728→    )
   729→    parts: list[str] = []
   730→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   731→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   732→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   733→    open_review = [
   734→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   735→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   736→    ]
   737→    if low_dims:
   738→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   739→    if stale_count:
   740→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   741→    if unassessed_count:
   742→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   743→    if len(open_review):
   744→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   745→    if coverage_open > 0:
   746→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   747→
   748→    if parts:
   749→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   750→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   751→
   752→
   753→__all__ = [
   754→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   755→    "render_followup_nudges",
   756→    "render_queue_header",
   757→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   758→    "render_terminal_items",
   759→    "scorecard_subjective",
   760→    "show_empty_queue",
   761→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   762→]
   763→
```

```
    58→
    59→def _apply_plan_order(
    60→    items: list[dict],
    61→    plan: dict,
    62→    *,
    63→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    64→    cluster: str | None = None,
    65→    state: dict | None = None,
    66→) -> list[dict]:
    67→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    68→
    69→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    70→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    71→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    72→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    73→    """
    74→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    75→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    76→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    77→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    78→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    79→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    80→
    81→    # Build lookup
    82→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    85→
    86→    # Build synthetic items for skipped entries missing from the work queue
    87→    if include_skipped and state:
    88→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    89→        for fid in skipped_ids:
    90→            if fid not in by_id and fid in findings:
    91→                f = findings[fid]
    92→                by_id[fid] = {
    93→                    "id": fid,
    94→                    "kind": "finding",
    95→                    "tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    96→                    "effective_tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    97→                    "confidence": f.get("confidence", "medium"),
    98→                    "detector": f.get("detector", ""),
    99→                    "file": f.get("file", ""),
   100→                    "summary": f.get("summary", ""),
   101→                    "status": f.get("status", "open"),
   102→                }
   103→
   104→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   105→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   106→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   107→        if override.get("description"):
   108→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   109→        if override.get("note"):
   110→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   111→        if override.get("cluster"):
   112→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   113→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   114→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   115→                "name": cluster_name,
   116→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   117→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   118→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   119→            }
   120→
   121→    # Normalise queue_order so subjective:: items always precede
   122→    # synthesis::pending — reviews generate the findings synthesis
   123→    # organises, so they must run first regardless of insertion order.
   124→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
   125→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in queue_order:
   126→        subj = [fid for fid in queue_order if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)]
   127→        if subj:
   128→            # Partition: subjective items first, then synthesis, then the rest
   129→            rest = [
   130→                fid for fid in queue_order
   131→                if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID and not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   132→            ]
   133→            queue_order = subj + [SYNTHESIS_ID] + rest
   134→
   135→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   136→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   137→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   138→    for fid in queue_order:
   139→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   140→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   141→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   142→
   143→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   144→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   145→    for item in items:
   146→        item_id = item["id"]
   147→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   148→            continue
   149→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   150→            skipped_items.append(item)
   151→        else:
   152→            remaining.append(item)
   153→
   154→    # Assign queue positions
   155→    result = ordered + remaining
   156→    if include_skipped:
   157→        result = result + skipped_items
   158→
   159→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   160→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   161→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   162→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   163→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   164→            if skip_entry:
   165→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   166→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   167→                if skip_reason:
   168→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   169→
   170→    # Filter to cluster if requested — but never filter out synthesis or
   171→    # subjective items; they are queue-level blockers that outrank cluster membership.
   172→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   173→    if effective_cluster:
   174→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   175→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   176→        result = [
   177→            item for item in result
   178→            if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids
   179→            or item.get("kind") in ("synthesis_needed", "subjective_dimension")
   180→        ]
   181→
   182→    return result
   183→
   184→
   185→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   186→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   187→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
   188→    if kind in ("synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   189→        return tier == 1  # Always T1
   190→    if kind == "cluster":
   191→        return any(
   192→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   193→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   194→        )
   195→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   196→
   197→
   198→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   199→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   200→    try:
   201→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   202→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   203→        if meta:
   204→            return meta.action_type
   205→    except ImportError:
   206→        pass
   207→    return "manual_fix"
   208→
   209→
   210→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   211→
   212→
   213→def _build_epic_items(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   214→    """Build synthetic queue items for each pending/in_progress synthesis-cluster."""
   215→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import synthesis_clusters
   216→
   217→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   218→    if not epics:
   219→        return []
   220→
   221→    total_epics = len(epics)
   222→    items: list[dict] = []
   223→    for epic_name, epic in sorted(
   224→        epics.items(),
   225→        key=lambda kv: kv[1].get("dependency_order", 999),
   226→    ):
   227→        status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   228→        if status == "completed":
   229→            continue
   230→        finding_ids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   231→        if not finding_ids:
   232→            continue
   233→
   234→        items.append({
   235→            "id": epic_name,
   236→            "kind": "epic",
   237→            "tier": 1,
   238→            "effective_tier": 1,
   239→            "confidence": "high",
   240→            "detector": "epic",
   241→            "file": ".",
   242→            "summary": f"[{epic.get('direction', 'simplify')}] {epic.get('thesis', '')}",
   243→            "status": "open",
   244→            "primary_command": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   247→                "direction": epic.get("direction", ""),
   248→                "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   249→                "epic_status": status,
   250→                "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   251→                "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   252→                "total_epics": total_epics,
   253→                "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   254→                "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   255→            },
   256→        })
   257→    return items
   258→
   259→
   260→def _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   261→    *,
   262→    all_items: list[dict],
   263→    plan: dict,
   264→    state: StateModel,
   265→    scope: str | None,
   266→) -> None:
   267→    """Inject synthesis::pending and epic items into the queue."""
   268→    existing_ids = {item.get("id") for item in all_items}
   269→
   270→    synthesis_item = build_synthesis_item(plan, state)
   271→    if synthesis_item and synthesis_item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   272→        if scope_matches(synthesis_item, scope):
   273→            all_items.append(synthesis_item)
   274→            existing_ids.add(synthesis_item["id"])
   275→
   276→    epic_items = _build_epic_items(plan, state)
   277→    for item in epic_items:
   278→        if item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   279→            if scope_matches(item, scope):
   280→                all_items.append(item)
   281→                existing_ids.add(item["id"])
   282→
   283→
   284→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   285→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   286→
   287→    Synthesis-clusters (those with a thesis) are represented by epic
   288→    items, so their member findings are excluded from cluster collapsing.
   289→    """
   290→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   291→
   292→    if not clusters:
   293→        return items
   294→
   295→    # Synthesis-clusters have their own epic queue items, so collect their
   296→    # finding IDs to exclude from regular cluster collapsing.
   297→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   298→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   299→        if cluster.get("thesis") and cluster.get("status") != "completed":
   300→            epic_finding_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   301→
   302→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   303→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   304→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   305→        if not cluster.get("auto") or cluster.get("thesis"):
   306→            continue
   307→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   308→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids:
   309→                fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   310→
   311→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   312→        return items
   313→
   314→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   315→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   316→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   317→
   318→    for item in items:
   319→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   320→        if cname:
   321→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   322→        else:
   323→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   324→
   325→    # Build cluster meta-items
   326→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   327→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   328→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   329→        if len(members) < 2:
   330→            result.extend(members)
   331→            continue
   332→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   333→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   334→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   335→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   336→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   337→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   338→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   339→            action_type = "reorganize"
   340→        else:
   341→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   342→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   343→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   344→                action_type = "refactor"
   345→
   346→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   347→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   348→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   349→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   350→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   351→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   352→        else:
   353→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   354→
   355→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   356→        if not primary_command:
   357→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   358→
   359→        meta_item: dict = {
   360→            "id": cname,
   361→            "kind": "cluster",
   362→            "action_type": action_type,
   363→            "summary": summary,
   364→            "members": members,
   365→            "member_count": len(members),
   366→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   367→            "cluster_name": cname,
   368→            "cluster_auto": True,
   369→            "confidence": "high",
   370→            "detector": detector,
   371→            "file": "",
   372→        }
   373→        result.append(meta_item)
   374→
   375→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   376→    return result
   377→
   378→
   379→def build_work_queue(
   380→    state: StateModel,
   381→    *,
   382→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   383→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   384→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   385→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   386→
   387→    status = resolved_options.status
   388→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   389→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   390→    try:
   391→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   392→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   393→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   394→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   395→
   396→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   397→        state,
   398→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   399→        status_filter=status,
   400→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   401→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   402→    )
   403→
   404→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   405→    if (
   406→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   407→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   408→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   409→    ):
   410→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   411→            state,
   412→            state.get("findings", {}),
   413→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   414→        )
   415→        for item in subjective_items:
   416→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   417→                all_items.append(item)
   418→
   419→    # Inject synthesis::pending and epic items when plan has them
   420→    if resolved_options.plan:
   421→        _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   422→            all_items=all_items,
   423→            plan=resolved_options.plan,
   424→            state=state,
   425→            scope=resolved_options.scope,
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   429→
   430→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   431→    if resolved_options.plan:
   432→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   433→            all_items,
   434→            resolved_options.plan,
   435→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   436→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   437→            state=state if resolved_options.include_skipped else None,
   438→        )
   439→
   440→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   441→    should_collapse = (
   442→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   443→        and resolved_options.plan
   444→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   445→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   446→    )
   447→    if should_collapse:
   448→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   449→
   450→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   451→
   452→    requested_tier = (
   453→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   454→    )
   455→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   456→    fallback_reason = None
   457→    filtered = all_items
   458→
   459→    if requested_tier is not None:
   460→        filtered = [
   461→            item
   462→            for item in all_items
   463→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   464→        ]
   465→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   466→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   467→            if chosen is not None:
   468→                selected_tier = chosen
   469→                filtered = [
   470→                    item
   471→                    for item in all_items
   472→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   473→                ]
   474→                fallback_reason = (
   475→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   476→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   477→                )
   478→        elif not filtered:
   479→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   480→
   481→    total = len(filtered)
   482→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   483→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   484→
   485→    if resolved_options.explain:
   486→        for item in filtered:
   487→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   488→
   489→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   490→    return {
   491→        "items": filtered,
   492→        "total": total,
   493→        "tier_counts": counts,
   494→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   495→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   496→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   497→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   498→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   499→    }
   500→
   501→
   502→__all__ = [
   503→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   504→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   505→    "WorkQueueResult",
   506→    "build_work_queue",
   507→    "group_queue_items",
   508→]
   509→
```

```
    12→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    13→    verb = "Reopened" if status == Status.OPEN.value else "Resolved"
    14→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))

    21→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    22→    state: dict,
    23→    *,
    24→    status: str,
    25→    resolved_count: int,
    26→) -> None:
    27→    if status != Status.WONTFIX.value or resolved_count <= 10:
    28→        return
    29→    wontfix_count = sum(
    30→        1
    31→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    32→        if finding.get("status") == Status.WONTFIX.value
    33→    )
    34→    actionable_statuses = finding_status_tokens()
    35→    actionable = sum(
    36→        1
    37→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    38→        if finding.get("status") in actionable_statuses
    39→    )
    40→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    41→    print(
    42→        colorize(
    43→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    44→            "yellow",
    45→        )
    46→    )
    47→    print(
    48→        colorize(
    49→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    50→            "dim",
    51→        )
    52→    )

    54→
    55→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    56→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    57→        return ""
    58→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_score_movement(

   166→    print(
   167→        colorize(
   168→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   169→            "yellow",
   170→        )
   171→    )
   172→    print(
   173→        colorize(
   174→            "  Next subjective step: "
   175→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   176→            "dim",
   177→        )
   178→    )
   179→
   180→
   181→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   182→    remaining = sum(
   183→        1
   184→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   185→        if finding.get("status") == Status.OPEN.value
   186→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   187→    )
   188→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   189→    if remaining > 0:
   190→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   191→        print(
   192→            colorize(
   193→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   194→                "dim",
   195→            )
   196→        )
   197→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   198→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   199→    print()
   200→    return next_command
   201→
```

```
1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_synthesis_guardrail_banner
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→
    13→from .apply_flow import (
    14→    _apply_and_report,
    15→    _detect,
    16→    _print_fix_summary,
    17→    _report_dry_run,
    18→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    19→)
    20→from .options import _load_fixer
    21→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    22→
    23→
    24→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    25→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    26→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    27→    if fixer_name == "review":
    28→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    29→        return
    30→
    31→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    32→    path = Path(args.path)
    33→
    34→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    35→
    36→    # Synthesis phase guardrail
    37→    print_synthesis_guardrail_banner()
    38→
    39→    if not dry_run:
    40→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    41→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    42→    if not entries:
    43→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    47→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    48→        results = raw.entries
    49→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    50→    else:
    51→        results = raw
    52→        skip_reasons = {}
    53→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    54→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    55→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    56→
    57→    if dry_run and results:
    58→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    59→
    60→    if not dry_run:
    61→        _apply_and_report(
    62→            args,
    63→            path,
    64→            fixer,
    65→            fixer_name,
    66→            entries,
    67→            results,
    68→            total_items,
    69→            lang,
    70→            skip_reasons,
    71→        )
    72→    else:
    73→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    74→    print()
    75→
```

```
1→"""Apply and reporting helpers for fix command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import subprocess
     7→import sys
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    15→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
    16→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    17→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    18→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    19→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    20→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    21→
    22→from .options import _COMMAND_POST_FIX
    23→
    24→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    25→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixerConfig
    26→    from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun

    28→
    29→def _detect(fixer: FixerConfig, path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    30→    print(colorize(f"\nDetecting {fixer.label}...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
    32→    file_count = len(set(e["file"] for e in entries))
    33→    print(
    34→        colorize(
    35→            f"  Found {len(entries)} {fixer.label} across {file_count} files\n", "dim"
    36→        ),
    37→        file=sys.stderr,
    38→    )
    39→    return entries
    40→
    41→
    42→def _print_fix_summary(
    43→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    44→    results: list[dict],
    45→    total_items: int,
    46→    total_lines: int,
    47→    dry_run: bool,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    verb = fixer.dry_verb if dry_run else fixer.verb
    50→    lines_str = f" ({total_lines} lines)" if total_lines else ""
    51→    print(
    52→        colorize(
    53→            f"\n  {verb} {total_items} {fixer.label} across {len(results)} files{lines_str}\n",
    54→            "bold",
    55→        )
    56→    )
    57→    for r in results[:30]:
    58→        syms = ", ".join(r["removed"][:5])
    59→        if len(r["removed"]) > 5:
    60→            syms += f" (+{len(r['removed']) - 5})"
    61→        extra = f"  ({r['lines_removed']} lines)" if r.get("lines_removed") else ""
    62→        print(f"  {rel(r['file'])}{extra}  →  {syms}")
    63→    if len(results) > 30:
    64→        print(f"  ... and {len(results) - 30} more files")
    65→
    66→
    67→def _apply_and_report(
    68→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    69→    path: Path,
    70→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    71→    fixer_name: str,
    72→    entries: list[dict],
    73→    results: list[dict],
    74→    total_items: int,
    75→    lang: LangRun | None,
    76→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
    77→) -> None:
    78→    state_file = state_path(args)
    79→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    80→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    81→    resolved_ids = _resolve_fixer_results(state, results, fixer.detector, fixer_name)
    82→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    83→
    84→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    85→    print(f"\n  Auto-resolved {len(resolved_ids)} findings in state")
    86→    print_score_update(state, prev)
    87→
    88→    if fixer.post_fix:
    89→        fixer.post_fix(path, state, prev.overall or 0, False, lang=lang)
    90→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    91→
    92→    if skip_reasons is None:
    93→        skip_reasons = {}
    94→    fix_lang = resolve_lang(args)
    95→    fix_lang_name = fix_lang.name if fix_lang else None
    96→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    97→        state,
    98→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=fix_lang_name, command="fix"),
    99→    )
   100→    typecheck_cmd = getattr(lang, "typecheck_cmd", "")
   101→    if typecheck_cmd:
   102→        next_action = (
   103→            f"Run `{typecheck_cmd}` to verify, then `desloppify scan` to update state"
   104→        )
   105→    else:
   106→        next_action = "Run `desloppify scan` to update state"
   107→    write_query(
   108→        {
   109→            "command": "fix",
   110→            "fixer": fixer_name,
   111→            "files_fixed": len(results),
   112→            "items_fixed": total_items,
   113→            "findings_resolved": len(resolved_ids),
   114→            "overall_score": new.overall,
   115→            "objective_score": new.objective,
   116→            "strict_score": new.strict,
   117→            "prev_overall_score": prev.overall,
   118→            "prev_objective_score": prev.objective,
   119→            "prev_strict_score": prev.strict,
   120→            "skip_reasons": skip_reasons,
   121→            "next_action": next_action,
   122→            "narrative": narrative,
   123→        }
   124→    )
   125→    _print_fix_retro(
   126→        fixer_name, len(entries), total_items, len(resolved_ids), skip_reasons
   127→    )
   128→
   129→
   130→def _report_dry_run(
   131→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   132→    fixer_name: str,
   133→    entries: list[dict],
   134→    results: list[dict],
   135→    total_items: int,
   136→) -> None:
   137→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   138→    fix_lang = resolve_lang(args)
   139→    fix_lang_name = fix_lang.name if fix_lang else None
   140→    state = runtime.state
   141→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   142→        state,
   143→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=fix_lang_name, command="fix"),
   144→    )
   145→    write_query(
   146→        {
   147→            "command": "fix",
   148→            "fixer": fixer_name,
   149→            "dry_run": True,
   150→            "files_would_fix": len(results),
   151→            "items_would_fix": total_items,
   152→            "narrative": narrative,
   153→        }
   154→    )
   155→    skipped = len(entries) - total_items
   156→    if skipped > 0:
   157→        print(colorize("\n  ── Review ──", "dim"))
   158→        print(
   159→            colorize(
   160→                f"  {total_items} of {len(entries)} entries would be fixed ({skipped} skipped).",
   161→                "dim",
   162→            )
   163→        )
   164→        for q in [
   165→            "Do the sample changes look correct? Any false positives?",
   166→            "Are the skipped items truly unfixable, or could the fixer be improved?",
   167→            "Ready to run without --dry-run? (git push first!)",
   168→        ]:
   169→            print(colorize(f"  - {q}", "dim"))
   170→
   171→
   172→def _resolve_fixer_results(
   173→    state: dict, results: list[dict], detector: str, fixer_name: str
   174→) -> list[str]:
   175→    resolved_ids = []
   176→    for r in results:
   177→        rfile = rel(r["file"])
   178→        for sym in r["removed"]:
   179→            fid = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{sym}"
   180→            if fid in state["findings"] and state["findings"][fid]["status"] == "open":
   181→                state["findings"][fid]["status"] = "fixed"
   182→                state["findings"][fid]["note"] = (
   183→                    f"auto-fixed by desloppify fix {fixer_name}"
   184→                )
   185→                resolved_ids.append(fid)
   186→    return resolved_ids
   187→
   188→
   189→def _warn_uncommitted_changes() -> None:
   190→    try:
   191→        r = subprocess.run(
   192→            ["git", "status", "--porcelain"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
   193→        )
   194→        if r.stdout.strip():
   195→            print(
   196→                colorize(
   197→                    "\n  ⚠ You have uncommitted changes. Consider running:", "yellow"
   198→                )
   199→            )
   200→            print(
   201→                colorize(
   202→                    "    git add -A && git commit -m 'pre-fix checkpoint' && git push",
   203→                    "yellow",
   204→                )
   205→            )
   206→            print(
   207→                colorize(
   208→                    "    This ensures you can revert if the fixer produces unexpected results.\n",
   209→                    "dim",
   210→                )
   211→            )
   212→    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   213→        return
   214→
   215→
   216→def _cascade_unused_import_cleanup(
   217→    path: Path,
   218→    state: dict,
   219→    _prev_score: float,
   220→    dry_run: bool,
   221→    *,
   222→    lang: LangRun | None = None,
   223→) -> None:
   224→    if not lang or "unused-imports" not in getattr(lang, "fixers", {}):
   225→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no unused-imports fixer for this language", "dim"))
   226→        return
   227→
   228→    fixer = lang.fixers["unused-imports"]
   229→    print(colorize("\n  Running cascading import cleanup...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   230→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
   231→    if not entries:
   232→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
   233→        return
   234→
   235→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
   236→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
   237→        results = raw.entries
   238→    else:
   239→        results = raw
   240→
   241→    if not results:
   242→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
   243→        return
   244→    n_removed = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
   245→    n_lines = sum(r["lines_removed"] for r in results)
   246→    print(
   247→        colorize(
   248→            f"  Cascade: removed {n_removed} now-orphaned imports "
   249→            f"from {len(results)} files ({n_lines} lines)",
   250→            "green",
   251→        )
   252→    )
   253→    resolved = _resolve_fixer_results(
   254→        state, results, fixer.detector, "cascade-unused-imports"
   255→    )
   256→    if resolved:
   257→        print(f"  Cascade: auto-resolved {len(resolved)} import findings")
   258→
   259→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["debug-logs"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   260→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["dead-useeffect"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   261→
   262→_SKIP_REASON_LABELS = {
   263→    "rest_element": "has ...rest (removing changes rest contents)",
   264→    "array_destructuring": "array destructuring (positional — can't remove)",
   265→    "function_param": "function/callback parameter (use `fix unused-params` to prefix with _)",
   266→    "standalone_var_with_call": "standalone variable with function call (may have side effects)",
   267→    "no_destr_context": "destructuring member without context",
   268→    "out_of_range": "line out of range (stale data?)",
   269→    "other": "other patterns (needs manual review)",
   270→}
   271→
   272→
   273→def _print_fix_retro(
   274→    fixer_name: str,
   275→    detected: int,
   276→    fixed: int,
   277→    resolved: int,
   278→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   279→):
   280→    skipped = detected - fixed
   281→    print(colorize("\n  ── Post-fix check ──", "dim"))
   282→    print(
   283→        colorize(
   284→            f"  Fixed {fixed}/{detected} ({skipped} skipped, {resolved} findings resolved)",
   285→            "dim",
   286→        )
   287→    )
   288→    if skip_reasons and skipped > 0:
   289→        print(colorize(f"\n  Skip reasons ({skipped} total):", "dim"))
   290→        for reason, count in sorted(skip_reasons.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):
   291→            print(
   292→                colorize(
   293→                    f"    {count:4d}  {_SKIP_REASON_LABELS.get(reason, reason)}", "dim"
   294→                )
   295→            )
   296→        print()
   297→    checklist = [
   298→        "Run your language typecheck/build command — does it still build?",
   299→        "Spot-check a few changed files — do the edits look correct?",
   300→    ]
   301→    if skipped > 0 and not skip_reasons:
   302→        checklist.append(
   303→            f"{skipped} items were skipped. Should the fixer handle more patterns?"
   304→        )
   305→    checklist += [
   306→        "Run `desloppify scan` to update state. Did score improve as expected?",
   307→        "Are there cascading effects? (e.g., removing vars may orphan imports)",
   308→        "`git diff --stat` — review before committing. Anything surprising?",
   309→    ]
   310→    print(colorize("  Checklist:", "dim"))
   311→    for i, item in enumerate(checklist, 1):
   312→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. {item}", "dim"))
   313→
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_policy import build_plan_queue
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, synthesis_phase_banner
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    40→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    41→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    42→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    46→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    47→        plan_data = plan
    48→
    49→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster
    50→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    51→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    52→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    53→        if active_cluster:
    54→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    55→
    56→    queue = build_plan_queue(
    57→        state,
    58→        tier=tier_filter,
    59→        count=None,
    60→        status="open",
    61→        plan=plan_data,
    62→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
    63→        cluster=effective_cluster,
    64→        collapse_clusters=True,
    65→    )
    66→    items = queue.get("items", [])
    67→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    68→
    69→    # Header
    70→    total = len(items)
    71→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    72→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    73→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    74→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    75→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    76→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    77→    else:
    78→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    79→
    80→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    81→    if focus:
    82→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    83→
    84→    # Synthesis phase guardrail
    85→    _banner = synthesis_phase_banner(plan)
    86→    if _banner:
    87→        print(colorize(f"\n  {_banner}", "yellow"))
    88→
    89→    if not include_skipped and skipped_count == 0:
    90→        # Check plan for skipped items not in queue
    91→        plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    92→        if plan_skipped_count:
    93→            print(colorize(
    94→                f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    95→                " hidden \u2014 use --include-skipped)",
    96→                "dim",
    97→            ))
    98→
    99→    if not items:
   100→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    # Determine which items to show
   104→    display_items = items
   105→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   106→        display_items = items[:top]
   107→
   108→    # Build rows
   109→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   110→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   111→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   112→        pos = str(idx)
   113→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   114→
   115→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   116→            tier_str = "T1"
   117→            detector = "synthesis"
   118→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   119→            n = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   120→            summary = f"[synthesis] {n} findings need integration"
   121→            cluster_name = ""
   122→        elif kind == "epic":
   123→            tier_str = "T1"
   124→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   125→            direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   126→            fcount = len(detail.get("finding_ids", []))
   127→            status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   128→            detector = "epic"
   129→            summary = f"[{direction}] [{fcount}] [{status}] {detail.get('thesis', '')}"
   130→            cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   131→        elif kind == "cluster":
   132→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
   133→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   134→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   135→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   136→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   137→        else:
   138→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   139→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   140→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   141→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   142→            # Extract 8-char hash suffix so users can reference items by short ID
   143→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   144→            if "::" in item_id:
   145→                hash_suffix = item_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8]
   146→                # Truncate summary first, then append hash so it's never cut off
   147→                summary = f"{_truncate(summary, 36)} [{hash_suffix}]"
   148→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   149→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   150→
   151→        suffix = ""
   152→        if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   153→            suffix = " [skip]"
   154→
   155→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   156→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   157→
   158→    print()
   159→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 44, 24]
   160→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   161→
   162→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   163→        remaining = len(items) - top
   164→        print(colorize(
   165→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   166→        ))
   167→    print()
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   171→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta

    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    19→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    20→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    21→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    22→        return 0
    23→
    24→    streak = 0
    25→    for entry in reversed(history):
    26→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→            break
    28→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    29→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    30→            break
    31→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    32→            break
    33→        streak += 1
    34→    return streak
    35→
    36→
    37→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    38→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    39→    diff_parts = []
    40→    if diff["new"]:
    41→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    42→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    43→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    44→    if diff["reopened"]:
    45→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    46→    if diff_parts:
    47→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    48→    else:
    49→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    50→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    51→        print(
    52→            colorize(
    53→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    54→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    55→                "yellow",
    56→            )
    57→        )
    58→
    59→
    60→def show_score_delta(
    61→    state: dict,
    62→    prev_overall: float | None,
    63→    prev_objective: float | None,
    64→    prev_strict: float | None,
    65→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
    66→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
    67→    *,
    68→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    69→):
    70→    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
    71→    stats = state["stats"]
    72→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    73→
    74→    if (
    75→        new.overall is None
    76→        or new.objective is None
    77→        or new.strict is None
    78→        or new.verified is None
    79→    ):
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
    83→                "yellow",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→        return
    87→
    88→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
    89→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
    90→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
    91→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
    92→    print(
    93→        "  Scores: "
    94→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
    95→        + colorize(
    96→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
    97→            objective_color,
    98→        )
    99→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
   100→        + colorize(
   101→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   102→            verified_color,
   103→        )
   104→    )
   105→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   106→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   107→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   108→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   109→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   110→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   111→        print(
   112→            colorize(
   113→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   114→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   115→                "yellow",
   116→            )
   117→        )
   118→
   119→    # Score legend — shown on first scan or when strict gap is significant
   120→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   121→    if scan_count <= 1 or gap > 10 or _is_agent_environment():
   122→        print(colorize("  Score guide:", "dim"))
   123→        print(colorize("    overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim"))
   124→        print(colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim"))
   125→        print(colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim"))
   126→        print(colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim"))
   127→
   128→    # Show strict target progress
   129→    if target_strict is not None and new.strict is not None:
   130→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import (
   131→            print_strict_target_nudge,
   132→        )
   133→
   134→        print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
   135→
   136→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   137→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   138→        status = integrity.get("status")
   139→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   140→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   141→        if status == "penalized":
   142→            print(
   143→                colorize(
   144→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   145→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   146→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   147→                    "red",
   148→                )
   149→            )
   150→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   151→            if streak >= 2:
   152→                print(
   153→                    colorize(
   154→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   155→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   156→                        "yellow",
   157→                    )
   158→                )
   159→        elif status == "warn":
   160→            print(
   161→                colorize(
   162→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   163→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   164→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   165→                    "yellow",
   166→                )
   167→            )
   168→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   169→            if streak >= 2:
   170→                print(
   171→                    colorize(
   172→                        "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   173→                        "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   174→                        "or run a blind reviewer pass before import.",
   175→                        "yellow",
   176→                )
   177→            )
   178→
   179→
   180→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   181→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   182→    try:
   183→        from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   184→
   185→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   186→        if concerns:
   187→            print(

   189→                    f"  {len(concerns)} potential design concern{'s' if len(concerns) != 1 else ''}"
   190→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   191→                    "cyan",
   192→                )
   193→            )
   194→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   195→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   196→
   197→
   198→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   199→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   200→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   201→    for message, style in lines:
   202→        print(colorize(message, style))
   203→    return target, gap
```

```
    20→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    21→from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
    22→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    23→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness, check_tool_staleness
    24→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    25→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    26→
    27→
    28→def _scorecard_subjective(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    dim_scores: dict,
    31→) -> list[dict]:
    32→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    33→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    37→    state: dict,
    38→    dim_scores: dict,
    39→    *,
    40→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    41→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    42→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    43→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    44→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    45→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    46→            continue
    47→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    48→        if strict_val < threshold:
    49→            low.append(
    50→                (
    51→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    52→                    strict_val,
    53→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    54→                )
    55→            )
    56→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    57→    return low
    58→
    59→
    60→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    61→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    62→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    63→    state = runtime.state
    64→    config = runtime.config
    65→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    66→        return
    67→
    68→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    69→    if stale_warning:
    70→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    71→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    72→    if skill_warning:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    74→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    75→    if config_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→
    78→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    82→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    83→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    84→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    85→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    86→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    87→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    88→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    89→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    90→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    91→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    92→
    93→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    94→
    95→    # Load the living plan
    96→    plan = load_plan()
    97→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    98→    if (
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    ):
   103→        plan_data = plan
   104→
   105→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   106→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   107→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   108→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   109→        if active_cluster:
   110→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   111→
   112→    queue = build_plan_queue(
   113→        state,
   114→        tier=tier,
   115→        count=count,
   116→        scope=scope,
   117→        status=status,
   118→        target_strict=target_strict,
   119→        no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   120→        explain=explain,
   121→        plan=plan_data,
   122→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   123→        cluster=effective_cluster,
   124→    )
   125→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   126→
   127→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   128→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   129→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   130→        state,
   131→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   132→    )
   133→
   134→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   135→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   136→    )
   137→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   138→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   139→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   140→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   141→        state,
   142→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   143→    )
   144→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   145→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   146→    ]
   147→    write_query(payload)
   148→
   149→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   150→    if output_file:
   151→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   152→            output_file,
   153→            payload,
   154→            len(items),
   155→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   156→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   157→        ):
   158→            return
   159→        raise SystemExit(1)
   160→
   161→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   162→        return
   163→
   164→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   165→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   166→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   167→        return
   168→
   169→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   170→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   171→        state.get("findings", {}),
   172→        state.get("scan_path"),
   173→    )
   174→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   175→    try:
   176→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   177→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   178→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   179→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   180→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   181→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   182→    )
   183→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   184→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   185→        state,
   186→        dim_scores,
   187→        findings_scoped,
   188→        strict_score=strict_score,
   189→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   190→    )
   191→    print()
   192→
   193→
   194→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   195→
```
