    89→
    90→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: dict) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return a warning banner if synthesis::pending is blocking, else None."""
    92→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    93→        return None
    94→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    95→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    96→    progress = []
    97→    for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize"):
    98→        if s in stages:
    99→            progress.append(f"{s} \u2713")
   100→        else:
   101→            progress.append(f"{s} \u25cb")
   102→    arrow = " \u2192 "
   103→    stage_line = f"  Stages: {arrow.join(progress)} \u2192 commit"
   104→
   105→    # Show what to do next based on current stage
   106→    if "observe" not in stages:
   107→        next_hint = '  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see findings and begin.'
   108→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   109→        next_hint = (
   110→            '  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see completed work and recurring patterns.\n'
   111→            '  Then: `desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."`'
   112→        )
   113→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   114→        next_hint = (
   115→            "  Create clusters, enrich each with action steps, then record organize.\n"
   116→            '  Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see progress.'
   117→        )
   118→    else:
   119→        next_hint = '  Complete with: `desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."`'
   120→
   121→    return (
   122→        "PLANNING PHASE \u2014 synthesis::pending is blocking the queue.\n"
   123→        "  Goal: resolve contradictions, then cluster by root cause with action steps.\n"
   124→        f"{stage_line}\n"
   125→        f"{next_hint}\n"
   126→        "  Do NOT fix code or resolve findings until planning is done.\n"
   127→        "  (Exception: use `plan skip` during organize to defer contradictory findings.)"
   128→    )
   129→
   130→
   131→__all__ = [

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 4
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}
    12→
    13→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = frozenset({
    14→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    15→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    16→})
    17→
    18→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    19→
    20→
    21→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    22→    finding_id: Required[str]
    23→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    24→    reason: str | None
    25→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    26→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    27→    created_at: str
    28→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    29→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    30→    original_position: int | None  # queue position before skip (for restore)
    31→
    32→
    33→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    34→    finding_id: Required[str]
    35→    description: str | None
    36→    note: str | None
    37→    cluster: str | None
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→
    41→
    42→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    43→    name: Required[str]
    44→    description: str | None
    45→    finding_ids: list[str]
    46→    created_at: str
    47→    updated_at: str
    48→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    49→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    50→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    51→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    52→    # Epic fields (optional — only present on synthesis-created clusters)
    53→    thesis: str | None
    54→    direction: str | None  # One of VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS
    55→    root_cause: str | None
    56→    supersedes: list[str]
    57→    dismissed: list[str]
    58→    agent_safe: bool
    59→    dependency_order: int
    60→    action_steps: list[str]
    61→    source_clusters: list[str]
    62→    status: str | None  # "pending" | "in_progress" | "completed"
    63→    synthesis_version: int
    64→
    65→
    66→class CompletedCluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    67→    """A synthesis-cluster archived after all its findings were resolved."""
    68→
    69→    name: Required[str]
    70→    thesis: str | None
    71→    description: str | None
    72→    action_steps: list[str]
    73→    finding_ids: list[str]       # IDs that were in this cluster when completed
    74→    direction: str | None
    75→    root_cause: str | None
    76→    completed_at: str            # ISO timestamp
    77→    synthesis_version: int
    78→
    79→
    80→class SynthesisStageReport(TypedDict, total=False):
    81→    """Record of a completed synthesis stage (observe, reflect, or organize)."""
    82→
    83→    stage: Required[str]       # "observe" | "reflect" | "organize"
    84→    report: Required[str]      # Free-text report
    85→    cited_ids: list[str]       # Finding IDs extracted from report
    86→    timestamp: str             # ISO timestamp
    87→    finding_count: int         # How many findings existed at time of report
    88→    recurring_dimensions: list[str]  # Dimensions with recurring patterns (reflect only)
    89→
    90→
    91→class EpicSynthesisMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
    92→    synthesized_ids: list[str]
    93→    last_run: str
    94→    version: int
    95→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
    96→    finding_snapshot_hash: str
    97→    strategy_summary: str
    98→    trigger: str
    99→    synthesis_stages: dict[str, SynthesisStageReport]
   100→    last_completed_at: str  # ISO timestamp of last synthesis completion
   101→
   102→
   103→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
   104→    original_id: Required[str]
   105→    original_detector: str
   106→    original_file: str
   107→    original_summary: str
   108→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
   109→    superseded_at: str
   110→    remapped_to: str | None
   111→    candidates: list[str]
   112→    note: str | None
   113→
   114→
   115→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   116→    version: Required[int]
   117→    created: Required[str]
   118→    updated: Required[str]
   119→    queue_order: list[str]
   120→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   121→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   122→    active_cluster: str | None
   123→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   124→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   125→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   126→    epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta
   127→    deleted_clusters: list[str]  # cluster_keys of deleted auto-clusters
   128→    completed_clusters: list[CompletedCluster]
   129→
   130→
   131→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   132→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   133→    now = utc_now()
   134→    return {
   135→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   136→        "created": now,
   137→        "updated": now,
   138→        "queue_order": [],
   139→        "deferred": [],
   140→        "skipped": {},
   141→        "active_cluster": None,
   142→        "overrides": {},
   143→        "clusters": {},
   144→        "superseded": {},
   145→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   146→        "deleted_clusters": [],
   147→        "completed_clusters": [],
   148→    }
   149→
   150→
   151→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   152→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   153→
   154→    Handles migrations:
   155→    - v1 → v2: deferred list → skipped dict
   156→    - v3 → v4: epics dict → synthesis-clusters in clusters dict
   157→    """
   158→    defaults = empty_plan()
   159→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   160→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   161→
   162→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   163→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   164→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   165→        plan["deferred"] = []
   166→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   167→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   168→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   169→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   170→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   171→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   172→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   173→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   174→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   175→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   176→    meta = plan["epic_synthesis_meta"]
   177→    if not isinstance(meta.get("synthesis_stages"), dict):
   178→        meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
   179→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deleted_clusters"), list):
   180→        plan["deleted_clusters"] = []
   181→    if not isinstance(plan.get("completed_clusters"), list):
   182→        plan["completed_clusters"] = []
   183→
   184→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   185→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   186→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   187→    if deferred:
   188→        now = utc_now()
   189→        for fid in list(deferred):
   190→            if fid not in skipped:
   191→                skipped[fid] = {
   192→                    "finding_id": fid,
   193→                    "kind": "temporary",
   194→                    "reason": None,
   195→                    "note": None,
   196→                    "attestation": None,
   197→                    "created_at": now,
   198→                    "review_after": None,
   199→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   200→                }
   201→        deferred.clear()
   202→
   203→    # Normalize cluster fields
   204→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   205→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   206→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   207→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   208→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   209→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   210→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   211→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   212→
   213→    # Migrate epics → clusters (v3 → v4)
   214→    old_epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
   215→    if old_epics and isinstance(old_epics, dict):
   216→        clusters = plan["clusters"]
   217→        now = utc_now()
   218→        for name, epic in old_epics.items():
   219→            if name not in clusters:
   220→                clusters[name] = {
   221→                    "name": name,
   222→                    "description": epic.get("thesis"),
   223→                    "finding_ids": list(epic.get("finding_ids", [])),
   224→                    "auto": True,
   225→                    "cluster_key": f"epic::{name}",
   226→                    "action": f"desloppify plan focus {name}",
   227→                    "user_modified": False,
   228→                    "created_at": epic.get("created_at", now),
   229→                    "updated_at": epic.get("updated_at", now),
   230→                    "thesis": epic.get("thesis"),
   231→                    "direction": epic.get("direction"),
   232→                    "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause"),
   233→                    "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   234→                    "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   235→                    "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   236→                    "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   237→                    "synthesis_version": epic.get("synthesis_version", 0),
   238→                    "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),
   239→                    "dismissed": epic.get("dismissed", []),
   240→                    "source_clusters": epic.get("source_clusters", []),
   241→                }
   242→
   243→
   244→def synthesis_clusters(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   245→    """Return clusters that were created by epic synthesis (have a thesis)."""
   246→    return {
   247→        name: cluster
   248→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   249→        if cluster.get("thesis")
   250→    }
   251→
   252→
   253→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   254→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   255→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   256→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   257→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   258→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   259→
   260→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   261→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   262→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   263→    if overlap:
   264→        raise ValueError(
   265→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   266→        )
   267→
   268→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   269→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   270→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   271→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   272→            raise ValueError(
   273→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   274→            )
   275→
   276→
   277→__all__ = [
   278→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   279→    "EpicSynthesisMeta",
   280→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   281→    "Cluster",
   282→    "CompletedCluster",
   283→    "ItemOverride",
   284→    "PlanModel",
   285→    "SkipEntry",
   286→    "SupersededEntry",
   287→    "SynthesisStageReport",
   288→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   289→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   290→    "empty_plan",
   291→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   292→    "synthesis_clusters",
   293→    "validate_plan",
   294→]
   295→

1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    24→
    25→
    26→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    27→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    28→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    29→    if not plan_path.exists():
    30→        return empty_plan()
    31→
    32→    try:
    33→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    34→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    35→        print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    36→        return empty_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    39→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    40→        return empty_plan()
    41→
    42→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    43→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    44→        print(
    45→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    46→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    47→            file=sys.stderr,
    48→        )
    49→
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    51→    try:
    52→        validate_plan(data)
    53→    except ValueError as ex:
    54→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    55→        return empty_plan()
    56→
    57→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    58→
    59→
    60→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    61→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    62→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    63→
    64→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    65→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    66→    validate_plan(plan)
    67→
    68→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    69→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    70→
    71→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    72→
    73→    if plan_path.exists():
    74→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    75→        try:
    76→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    77→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    78→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    82→    except OSError as ex:
    83→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
    84→        raise
    85→
    86→
    87→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
    88→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
    89→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
    90→
    91→
    92→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
    93→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
    94→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return False
    97→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    98→    return bool(
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→__all__ = [
   106→    "PLAN_FILE",
   107→    "has_living_plan",
   108→    "load_plan",
   109→    "plan_path_for_state",
   110→    "save_plan",
   111→]
   112→

1→"""Shared queue-construction policy for next/plan command surfaces."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.enums import Status, canonical_status_filter
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     9→
    10→
    11→def build_plan_queue(
    12→    state: dict[str, Any],
    13→    *,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int | None = None,
    16→    scope: str | None = None,
    17→    status: str = Status.OPEN.value,
    18→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
    19→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False,
    20→    explain: bool = False,
    21→    plan: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    22→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    23→    cluster: str | None = None,
    24→    collapse_clusters: bool = False,
    25→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    26→    """Build one queue with consistent policy defaults used by CLI surfaces."""
    27→    normalized_status = canonical_status_filter(status, default=Status.OPEN.value)
    28→    return build_work_queue(
    29→        state,
    30→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    31→            tier=tier,
    32→            count=count,
    33→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    34→            scope=scope,
    35→            status=normalized_status,
    36→            include_subjective=True,
    37→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
    38→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
    39→            explain=explain,
    40→            plan=plan,
    41→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    42→            cluster=cluster,
    43→            collapse_clusters=collapse_clusters,
    44→        ),
    45→    )
    46→
    47→
    48→__all__ = ["build_plan_queue"]
    49→

1→"""Summary-line rendering helpers for the status command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD, colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def score_summary_lines(
    10→    *,
    11→    overall_score: float | None,
    12→    objective_score: float | None,
    13→    strict_score: float | None,
    14→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    15→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    16→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    17→    """Return formatted top-line score summary rows."""
    18→    if (
    19→        overall_score is not None
    20→        and objective_score is not None
    21→        and strict_score is not None
    22→        and verified_strict_score is not None
    23→    ):
    24→        lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
    25→            (
    26→                f"\n  Scores: overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 · "
    27→                f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 · "
    28→                f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 · "
    29→                f"verified {verified_strict_score:.1f}/100",
    30→                "bold",
    31→            ),
    32→            (
    33→                "  Score guide: overall (lenient) · objective (mechanical only) · "
    34→                "strict (penalizes wontfix) · verified (scan-confirmed only)",
    35→                "dim",
    36→            ),
    37→        ]
    38→        if target_strict is not None:
    39→            gap = round(target_strict - strict_score, 1)
    40→            if gap > 0:
    41→                lines.append((
    42→                    f"  Strict {strict_score:.1f} (target: {target_strict:.1f})"
    43→                    " — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement",
    44→                    "dim",
    45→                ))
    46→            else:
    47→                lines.append((
    48→                    f"  Strict {strict_score:.1f} — target {target_strict:.1f} reached!",
    49→                    "green",
    50→                ))
    51→        else:
    52→            lines.append(("  Focus on strict — it's your north star.", "dim"))
    53→        return lines
    54→    return [
    55→        ("\n  Scores unavailable", "bold"),
    56→        ("  Run a full scan to compute overall/objective/strict scores.", "yellow"),
    57→    ]
    58→
    59→
    60→def print_scan_metrics(state: dict) -> None:
    61→    """Print aggregate codebase metrics from the last scan."""
    62→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    63→    total_files = sum(m.get("total_files", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    64→    total_loc = sum(m.get("total_loc", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    65→    total_dirs = sum(m.get("total_directories", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    66→    if total_files:
    67→        loc_str = f"{total_loc:,}" if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    68→        print(
    69→            colorize(
    70→                f"  {total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} dirs · "
    71→                f"Last scan: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    72→                "dim",
    73→            )
    74→        )
    75→        return
    76→    print(
    77→        colorize(
    78→            f"  Scans: {state.get('scan_count', 0)} | Last: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    79→            "dim",
    80→        )
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def print_scan_completeness(state: dict) -> None:
    85→    """Warn when one or more language scans were partial."""
    86→    completeness = state.get("scan_completeness", {})
    87→    incomplete = [lang for lang, status in completeness.items() if status != "full"]
    88→    if incomplete:
    89→        print(
    90→            colorize(
    91→                f"  * Incomplete scan ({', '.join(incomplete)} — slow phases skipped)",
    92→                "yellow",
    93→            )
    94→        )
    95→
    96→
    97→def print_open_scope_breakdown(state: dict) -> None:
    98→    """Print open counts with explicit in-scope/out-of-scope semantics."""
    99→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   100→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    counts = state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   104→    print(
   105→        colorize(
   106→            "  "
   107→            f"open (in-scope): {counts['in_scope']} · "
   108→            f"open (out-of-scope carried): {counts['out_of_scope']} · "
   109→            f"open (global): {counts['global']}",
   110→            "dim",
   111→        )
   112→    )
   113→
   114→
   115→__all__ = [
   116→    "print_open_scope_breakdown",
   117→    "print_scan_completeness",
   118→    "print_scan_metrics",
   119→    "score_summary_lines",
   120→]
   121→

    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→

    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→
    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    80→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
    81→    suppression: dict,
    82→    narrative: dict,
    83→    ignores: list[str],
    84→    overall_score: float | None,
    85→    objective_score: float | None,
    86→    strict_score: float | None,
    87→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    88→    plan: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> None:
    90→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    91→    open_scope = (
    92→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    93→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    94→        else None
    95→    )
    96→    write_query(
    97→        {
    98→            "command": "status",
    99→            "overall_score": overall_score,
   100→            "objective_score": objective_score,
   101→            "strict_score": strict_score,
   102→            "verified_strict_score": verified_strict_score,
   103→            "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   104→            "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   105→            "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   106→            "stats": stats,
   107→            "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   108→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   109→            "by_tier": by_tier,
   110→            "ignores": ignores,
   111→            "suppression": suppression,
   112→            "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   113→            "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   114→            "open_scope": open_scope,
   115→            "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   116→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),
   117→            "narrative": narrative,
   118→            **({"plan": {
   119→                "active": True,
   120→                "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   121→                "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   122→                "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   123→                "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   124→            }} if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters") or plan.get("skipped")) else {}),
   125→        }
   126→    )

   128→
   129→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""
   131→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   132→    for p in ignores[:10]:
   133→        print(colorize(f"    {p}", "dim"))
   134→
   135→    last_ignored = int(suppression.get("last_ignored", 0) or 0)
   136→    last_raw = int(suppression.get("last_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   137→    last_pct = float(suppression.get("last_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   138→
   139→    if last_raw > 0:
   140→        style = "red" if last_pct >= 30 else "yellow" if last_pct >= 10 else "dim"
   141→        print(
   142→            colorize(
   143→                f"  Ignore suppression (last scan): {last_ignored}/{last_raw} findings hidden ({last_pct:.1f}%)",
   144→                style,
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):
   148→        print(colorize("  Ignore suppression (last scan): 0 findings hidden", "dim"))
   149→
   150→    recent_scans = int(suppression.get("recent_scans", 0) or 0)
   151→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   152→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   153→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   154→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
   157→                f"    Recent ({recent_scans} scans): {recent_ignored}/{recent_raw} findings hidden ({recent_pct:.1f}%)",
   158→                "dim",
   159→            )
   160→        )
   161→
   162→
   163→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   164→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   165→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   166→        state,
   167→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   176→    lowest_name = None
   177→    lowest_score = 101.0
   178→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   179→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   180→        if not ds:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   183→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   189→            lowest_score = strict_val
   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   191→    return lowest_name
   192→
   193→
   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   195→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   196→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   197→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   198→        return {}
   199→
   200→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for finding in findings.values():
   202→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   203→            continue
   204→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   205→            continue
   206→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   207→        dimension = ""
   208→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   209→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   210→        if not dimension:
   211→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   212→        if not dimension:
   213→            continue
   214→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   215→    return counts
   216→
   217→
   218→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   219→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   222→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   223→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   224→        if not ds:
   225→            continue
   226→        score_val = ds["score"]
   227→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   228→        checks = ds["checks"]
   229→
   230→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   231→
   232→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   233→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   234→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   235→        print(
   236→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   237→        )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   241→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   242→    *,
   243→    lowest_name: str | None,
   244→    bar_len: int,
   245→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   248→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   249→        return
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   253→            "dim",
   254→        )
   255→    )
   256→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   257→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   258→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   259→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   260→        tier = 4
   261→
   262→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   263→
   264→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   265→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   266→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   267→        placeholder_tag = (
   268→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   269→        )
   270→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   271→        cli_keys = [
   272→            str(key).strip()
   273→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   274→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   275→        ]
   276→        if dim_key:
   277→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   278→        elif cli_keys:
   279→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   280→        else:
   281→            issue_count = 0
   282→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   283→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   284→        print(
   285→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   286→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict], state: dict | None = None) -> None:
   291→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   292→    print(
   293→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   294→    )
   295→    print(
   296→        colorize(
   297→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
   298→            "dim",
   299→        )
   300→    )
   301→    stale_keys = [
   302→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   303→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   304→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   305→    ]
   306→    if stale_keys:
   307→        n = len(stale_keys)
   308→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   309→        print(
   310→            colorize(
   311→                f"  [stale] = subjective assessment outdated, not an unresolved finding"
   312→                f" — re-review to refresh",
   313→                "yellow",
   314→            )
   315→        )
   316→        print(
   317→            colorize(
   318→                f"  {n} stale dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   319→                f": `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}`",
   320→                "yellow",
   321→            )
   322→        )
   323→
   324→
   325→def show_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   326→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   327→    print()
   328→    bar_len = 20
   329→    print(
   330→        colorize(
   331→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",
   332→            "dim",
   333→        )
   334→    )
   335→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 86, "dim"))
   336→
   337→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   338→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   339→    review_issue_counts = _open_review_issue_counts(state)
   340→
   341→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   342→    _render_subjective_dimensions(
   343→        scorecard_subjective,
   344→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   345→        bar_len=bar_len,
   346→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   347→    )
   348→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective, state=state)
   349→    print()
   350→
   351→
   352→def show_focus_suggestion(
   353→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   354→) -> None:
   355→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   356→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   357→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   358→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   359→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   360→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   361→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   362→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   363→        print(
   364→            colorize(
   365→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   366→                "cyan",
   367→            )
   368→        )
   369→        print()
   370→        return
   371→
   372→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   373→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   374→    if not lowest_name:
   375→        return
   376→
   377→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   378→    lowest_kind = None
   379→    lowest_score = 101.0
   380→    lowest_issues = 0
   381→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   382→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   383→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   384→            if ds:
   385→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   386→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   387→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   388→            break
   389→    else:
   390→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   391→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   392→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   394→                lowest_issues = 0
   395→                break
   396→
   397→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   398→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   399→            print(
   400→                colorize(
   401→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   409→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   410→        if target_dim:
   411→            impact = 0.0
   412→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   413→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   414→                    {
   415→                        k: {
   416→                            "score": v["score"],
   417→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   418→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   419→                        }
   420→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   421→                        if "score" in v
   422→                    },
   423→                    potentials,
   424→                    det,
   425→                    lowest_issues,
   426→                )
   427→                if impact > 0:
   428→                    break
   429→
   430→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   431→            print(
   432→                colorize(
   433→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   434→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   435→                    "cyan",
   436→                )
   437→            )
   438→            print()
   439→

   443→) -> None:

   445→    if not dim_scores:

   492→
   493→def _collect_structural_areas(
   494→    state: dict,
   495→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   496→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   497→
   498→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   499→    """
   500→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   501→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   502→    )
   503→
   504→    structural = [
   505→        f
   506→        for f in findings.values()
   507→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   508→    ]
   509→
   510→    if len(structural) < 5:
   511→        return None
   512→
   513→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   514→    for f in structural:
   515→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   516→        areas[area].append(f)
   517→
   518→    if len(areas) < 2:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   522→
   523→
   524→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   525→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   526→    rows = []
   527→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   528→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   529→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   530→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   531→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   532→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   533→        rows.append(
   534→            [
   535→                area,
   536→                str(len(area_findings)),
   537→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   538→                str(open_count),
   539→                str(debt_count),
   540→                str(weight),
   541→            ]
   542→        )
   543→    return rows
   544→
   545→
   546→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   547→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   548→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   549→    if remaining > 0:
   550→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   551→
   552→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   553→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   554→    print(
   555→        colorize(
   556→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   557→        )
   558→    )
   559→    print(
   560→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   561→    )
   562→    print()
   563→
   564→
   565→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   566→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   567→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   568→    if sorted_areas is None:
   569→        return
   570→
   571→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   572→    print(
   573→        colorize(
   574→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   575→            "dim",
   576→        )
   577→    )
   578→    print()
   579→
   580→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   581→    print_table(
   582→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   583→    )
   584→
   585→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   586→
   587→
   588→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   589→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   590→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   591→    review_open = [
   592→        f
   593→        for f in findings.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if not review_open:
   597→        return
   598→    uninvestigated = sum(
   599→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   600→    )
   601→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   602→    if uninvestigated:
   603→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   604→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   605→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   606→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   607→        print(
   608→            colorize(
   609→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   610→                "dim",
   611→            )
   612→        )
   613→    print()
   614→

    41→    data = review_mod.prepare_review(

    46→    )
    47→
    48→    if data["total_candidates"] == 0:

    61→        _coverage_total, _reasons, holistic_reasons = (

    64→        holistic_open = sum(holistic_reasons.values())

181→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   182→    remaining = sum(
   183→        1
   184→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   185→        if finding.get("status") == Status.OPEN.value
   186→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   187→    )
   188→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   189→    if remaining > 0:
   190→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   191→        print(
   192→            colorize(
   193→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   194→                "dim",
   195→            )
   196→        )
   197→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   198→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   199→    print()
   200→    return next_command

1→"""State-path and scan-gating helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import auto_detect_lang_name
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    10→
    11→# Backward-compatible override hook for tests.
    12→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    13→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    14→
    15→
    16→def _runtime_project_root() -> Path:
    17→    override = PROJECT_ROOT
    18→    if override is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(override, Path | str):
    19→        return Path(override).resolve()
    20→    return get_project_root()
    21→
    22→
    23→def _sole_existing_lang_state_file() -> Path | None:
    24→    """Return the only existing language-specific state file, if unambiguous."""
    25→    state_dir = _runtime_project_root() / ".desloppify"
    26→    if not state_dir.exists():
    27→        return None
    28→    candidates = sorted(path for path in state_dir.glob("state-*.json") if path.is_file())
    29→    if len(candidates) == 1:
    30→        return candidates[0]
    31→    return None
    32→
    33→
    34→def _allow_lang_state_fallback(args) -> bool:
    35→    """Whether command can safely fallback to the sole existing lang state file."""
    36→    # Scan should always honor detected/explicit language mapping to avoid cross-lang merges.
    37→    return getattr(args, "command", None) != "scan"
    38→
    39→
    40→def state_path(args) -> Path | None:
    41→    """Get state file path from args, or None for default."""
    42→    path_arg = getattr(args, "state", None)
    43→    if path_arg:
    44→        return Path(path_arg)
    45→    lang_name = getattr(args, "lang", None)
    46→    if not lang_name:
    47→        lang_name = auto_detect_lang_name(args)
    48→    if lang_name:
    49→        resolved = _runtime_project_root() / ".desloppify" / f"state-{lang_name}.json"
    50→        if resolved.exists() or not _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    51→            return resolved
    52→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    53→        if fallback is not None:
    54→            return fallback
    55→        return resolved
    56→
    57→    if _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    58→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    59→        if fallback is not None:
    60→            return fallback
    61→    return None
    62→
    63→
    64→def require_completed_scan(state: dict) -> bool:
    65→    """Return True when the state contains at least one completed scan."""
    66→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    67→    if not has_completed_scan:
    68→        print(colorize("No scans yet. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    69→    return has_completed_scan
    70→

1→"""Centralized score update display for all state-changing commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→def print_score_update(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    13→    *,
    14→    config: dict | None = None,
    15→    label: str = "Scores",
    16→) -> None:
    17→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    18→
    19→    Args:
    20→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    21→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    22→        config: Project config dict (loaded from disk if not provided).
    23→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    24→    """
    25→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    26→    if (
    27→        new.overall is None
    28→        or new.objective is None
    29→        or new.strict is None
    30→        or new.verified is None
    31→    ):
    32→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    33→        return
    34→

Now I have a thorough understanding of the entire system. Let me compile my complete design.

---

## Implementation Plan: Score-as-Reward UX

### 1. Design Philosophy

The core idea: during the "execution phase" (when the work queue has items), the numeric score is noise. Replace it with queue progress -- a countdown that gives the user tangible momentum. The score becomes the payoff for clearing the queue.

### 2. Cheaply Detecting Queue State

**Problem:** Building the full work queue (`build_work_queue`) is expensive -- it loads state, iterates all findings, sorts, applies plan ordering, collapses clusters. We need a cheap way to ask "are there queue items?"

**Solution:** A new lightweight function in `desloppify/app/commands/helpers/score_update.py` (or a new module) that counts open findings from state without building the full queue. The existing pattern from `resolve/render.py:182-187` already does this:

```python
remaining = sum(
    1
    for finding in state["findings"].values()
    if finding.get("status") == Status.OPEN.value
    and not finding.get("suppressed")
)
```

However, "queue items" and "open findings" are not identical. The queue includes subjective dimension items, synthesis items, and epic items. A truly accurate count requires `build_work_queue`. 

**Recommended approach:** A two-tier strategy:
1. **Cheap check:** `has_queue_items(state) -> bool` -- just checks if any open, unsuppressed findings exist. This covers 95% of cases and is O(n) over findings only.
2. **Accurate count (when needed for display):** When we actually need to show "12 items remaining", we should use the queue `total` field from `WorkQueueResult`. The `fix` and `resolve` commands already have `state` loaded; building the queue at that point is acceptable because we're at the end of the command (after the actual work is done).

But there is a simpler, even cheaper approach: The plan's `queue_order` list already contains the ordered item IDs. We can count items in `queue_order` minus items in `skipped`, cross-referenced with open findings in state. However, this still misses items not explicitly in queue_order (auto-discovered findings). 

**Final recommendation:** Create a `queue_progress_count(state, plan)` function that:
1. Counts open, unsuppressed findings in state
2. Adds subjective dimension items (count of dimensions below threshold)
3. This is cheap (just iterates the findings dict + a small dimension check)
4. This matches what the queue builder produces to within ~1-2 items, which is fine for a progress display

### 3. New Module: Queue Progress Display

Create a new file: **`desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py`**

This module centralizes all queue-progress and score-reveal display logic.

```python
"""Queue progress display for execution-phase commands."""

from __future__ import annotations

from desloppify import state as state_mod
from desloppify.core.enums import Status
from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize


def queue_item_count(state: dict) -> int:
    """Cheaply count approximate queue items from state.
    
    Counts open, unsuppressed findings. This is an approximation --
    the real queue may include subjective items, synthesis items, etc.
    But it's fast (O(n) over findings) and accurate enough for display.
    """
    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
        return 0
    return sum(
        1
        for f in findings.values()
        if isinstance(f, dict)
        and f.get("status") == Status.OPEN.value
        and not f.get("suppressed")
    )


def has_active_queue(state: dict) -> bool:
    """Return True if the execution queue has remaining items."""
    return queue_item_count(state) > 0


def print_queue_progress(
    state: dict,
    prev_count: int | None = None,
    *,
    label: str = "Queue",
) -> None:
    """Print queue progress instead of scores during execution phase.
    
    Args:
        state: Current state dict.
        prev_count: If provided, shows delta (e.g., "12 -> 11 items remaining").
        label: Prefix label.
    """
    current = queue_item_count(state)
    
    if prev_count is not None and prev_count != current:
        resolved = prev_count - current
        print(
            colorize(
                f"\n  {label}: {prev_count} -> {current} items remaining "
                f"({resolved} resolved)",
                "green" if resolved > 0 else "yellow",
            )
        )
    else:
        suffix = "s" if current != 1 else ""
        print(
            colorize(
                f"\n  {label}: {current} item{suffix} remaining",
                "cyan",
            )
        )
    
    print(
        colorize(
            "  Clear the queue to see your updated score.",
            "dim",
        )
    )


def print_queue_cleared_celebration(
    state: dict,
    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    *,
    config: dict | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Show the full score reveal when the queue is cleared.
    
    This is the reward moment -- show scores prominently with celebration.
    """
    from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
    
    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    if new.strict is None:
        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared! Run `desloppify scan` for scores.", "green"))
        return
    
    print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
    print(colorize("  " + "=" * 50, "green"))
    
    if new.overall is not None and new.strict is not None:
        overall_s, overall_c = format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
        strict_s, strict_c = format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
        
        print(
            colorize(f"  Overall: {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
        )
        print(
            colorize(f"  Strict:  {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
        )
        if new.objective is not None:
            objective_s, objective_c = format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
            print(
                colorize(
                    f"  Objective: {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}",
                    objective_c,
                )
            )
        if new.verified is not None:
            verified_s, verified_c = format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
            print(
                colorize(
                    f"  Verified: {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}",
                    verified_c,
                )
            )
    
    print(colorize("  " + "=" * 50, "green"))
    
    if config is None:
        from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
        config = config_mod.load_config()
    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    
    target = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    gap = round(target - (new.strict or 0), 1)
    if gap > 0:
        print(
            colorize(
                f"  Strict {new.strict:.1f} -> target {target:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} to go)",
                "cyan",
            )
        )
        print(
            colorize(
                "  Run `desloppify scan` to refresh findings, then `desloppify plan` for next cycle.",
                "dim",
            )
        )
    else:
        print(colorize(f"  Target {target:.1f} reached!", "green"))


def print_execution_or_score(
    state: dict,
    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    *,
    config: dict | None = None,
    label: str = "Scores",
    prev_queue_count: int | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Context-aware display: queue progress during execution, scores at milestones.
    
    This is the drop-in replacement for print_score_update() in execution commands.
    """
    current_count = queue_item_count(state)
    
    if current_count == 0:
        # Queue is clear -- this is the reward moment!
        print_queue_cleared_celebration(state, prev, config=config)
    else:
        # Still working -- show progress, not scores
        print_queue_progress(state, prev_queue_count, label=label)
```

#### 4j. `desloppify/app/commands/review/import_cmd.py` (line 372) and `review/merge.py` (line 221)

**Decision:** These are borderline. Importing review results and merging reviews are significant operations that fundamentally change the scoring landscape. They could be considered "assessment moments" like scan. However, they happen mid-cycle.

**Recommendation:** Apply the same `print_execution_or_score` pattern here. When queue has items, show queue progress. When queue is empty, show the celebration.

### 5. Edge Cases

**No plan exists:** If `load_plan()` returns an empty plan (no `queue_order`, no `clusters`), we are in "unplanned" mode. In this case, `has_active_queue(state)` still works because it checks open findings in state, not the plan's queue_order. An unplanned queue with open findings should still show queue progress. An empty state with no findings shows scores (or "run scan").

**Queue was never started (no scans yet):** `require_completed_scan(state)` gates most commands. If state is empty, commands like `next`, `fix`, `resolve` either bail early or scores are unavailable. No special handling needed.

**User wants to peek at scores mid-queue:** This is deliberate -- the point is to hide scores during execution. However, `status --json` still dumps full scores for programmatic access. And `scan` always shows scores. So if they really want to see scores, they can run `scan`.

**Synthesis phase:** During synthesis, the queue has items (synthesis::pending) but no mechanical work is happening. The queue progress display should still work -- "1 item remaining" (the synthesis task). This is correct because synthesis IS the blocker.

**Score becomes unavailable:** If scores are `None` (no dimension scores computed), both old and new code handle this with "run `desloppify scan`" messages. No change needed.
